# Mineland Creative+ coding: guide for writing code JSON

This file teaches you (a language model) how to write code for the visual coding of Mineland Creative+ (Minecraft 1.21.11).
You write one JSON object. The player uploads it to the server. The server checks it strictly.
Read this guide first, then use the action catalog below it. Never invent ids, argument keys or marker keys.

## 1. What Creative+ coding is

- A plot is the player's own world. Every plot has a separate code area (the "dev" area) with floors of code.
- Code is a set of lines. In JSON a line is called a handler.
- Every line starts with one starter block:
  - an EVENT handler (`"kind":"event"`): runs when a game event happens, for example a player joins;
  - a FUNCTION (`"kind":"function"`): runs only when another line calls it by name;
  - a LOOP (`"kind":"loop"`): a timer that repeats every N ticks after it was started with `GAME_START_LOOP`.
- After the starter block come nodes (actions, conditions, selections, controls). They run left to right, which is the order of the `nodes` array.
- Code runs only while the world is in play mode. 20 ticks = 1 second.

## 2. How the code gets into the world

1. Write ONE JSON object (section 3). Do not add comments inside JSON.
2. Publish it on paste.mineland.net (section 2.1), or give it to the player to paste there.
3. In the world, one of these commands is run (by the player, or by you if you control the game through a mod):
   - `/codeshare import <key>`: REPLACES ALL code of the plot. Only the plot owner can do it. If the plot already has code, or the check found warnings, the server waits 30 seconds for one of two commands (the chat also shows them as buttons):
     - `/codeshare confirm`: replace now;
     - `/codeshare confirm backup`: first upload the old code to paste.mineland.net (the link is shown in chat), then replace.
     If the plot has no code and there are no warnings, the code is replaced at once.
     The number in the warning (`строк: N`) is how many lines the CURRENT code has: they will be lost.
   - `/codeshare check <key>`: only checks the code and prints the problems. The code of the plot is not changed. Only the plot owner can do it.
4. `<key>` may also be the full link `https://paste.mineland.net/<key>` or `https://paste.mineland.net/raw/<key>`.
5. `/codeshare export` (owner or editor) uploads the current code of the plot in the same format and shows the link. Read it, change it and import the full new version. The export is normalized: every line gets its `position`, item components are written as SNBT text, one-value arrays become single values. That is the same code.
6. Cooldowns: import 30 seconds (counted from the command, also when you confirm later), export 60 seconds, check 2 minutes.
7. The new code is applied at once. If the world is in play mode, it is restarted exactly like `/play`: every player (except those in the dev area) is moved to the spawn, `PLOT_WORLD_STARTS` and `PLAYER_JOIN` run again and loops can be started again. If the world is in build mode, code does not run there: run `/play` to start it.
   All `/codeshare` commands work from anywhere in the world (play, build or dev area); entering the dev area is not needed.
8. Warnings (non-blocking problems) are printed even when the import is accepted. Read them: they usually mean a part of the code will not work.
9. Every event and action must be allowed for the player's rank. If one line uses something the player cannot use, the whole import is refused with "no access". Ask the player which actions their rank has if this happens.
10. Broken JSON, invalid component SNBT, item `components` longer than 65536 characters, a `condition` inside another `condition`, too many items or values in the whole JSON, a value object without its `item`, or a `position`, `ticks` or `count` that is not a number make the whole import fail with one error line and no problem list.

If the check finds a blocking problem, the import is rejected. The server prints up to 10 problems in chat, one per line:
`<CODE> <path>: <detail>`, for example
`UNKNOWN_ARGUMENT handlers[2] event PLAYER_CHAT > nodes[0] PLAYER_MESSAGE > args > bogus_arg: [texts]` (the detail lists the valid keys).
Read the path, fix exactly that place and import the full JSON again. All codes are in section 11.

Ask the player when you need facts you cannot know: block coordinates in their world (they can see them with F3), how many code floors their plot has, item names.

### 2.1 Publish and read code yourself (paste.mineland.net API)

- Publish: `POST https://paste.mineland.net/documents` with the raw JSON as the request body (`Content-Type: text/plain`). The answer is `{"key":"<key>"}`. Then run `/codeshare import <key>`.
  ```
  curl -s -X POST -H "Content-Type: text/plain" --data-binary @code.json https://paste.mineland.net/documents
  {"key":"abcdefgh"}
  ```
- Read: `GET https://paste.mineland.net/raw/<key>` returns the stored text. Use it for links from `/codeshare export` and for backups.
- Publish only the JSON. Documents are public, so never publish secrets.

Loop for an agent that controls the game: write JSON → publish → `/codeshare import <key>` → read chat: problem lines (fix and repeat), the warning with the 30-second choice (`/codeshare confirm`), or "code loaded" → test (the code already runs if the world is in play mode, otherwise `/play`).
The import cooldown is 30 seconds, so fix ALL problems you can see before the next import. When the code works, `/codeshare export` shows what the server really stored (for example items it lowered).

## 3. Code format

### 3.1 Top level

```json
{"handlers": [ ... ]}
```

- `handlers`: required array of lines. It must not be empty (`EMPTY_PROGRAM`).
- Nothing else is needed at the top level. Unknown top-level keys are ignored.
- Ids of actions, events, game values and targets may be written in any case (`player_message` = `PLAYER_MESSAGE`). The catalog uses upper case.

### 3.2 Handler (one line)

| key | used for | meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `kind` | all | `"event"`, `"function"` or `"loop"`. If missing, it is `"event"`. Any other text breaks the line (`BROKEN_HANDLER`). |
| `event` | event | Event id from the event list (section 9). Required for events. |
| `name` | function, loop | Name of the function or loop. Required, not blank. Use plain names without color codes. |
| `ticks` | loop | Period in ticks. Default 20. Minimum 5: a smaller value is raised to 5 on import. |
| `channel` | event | Only for `PLOT_DATA_TRANSFER`: the channel name this line listens to. A `PLOT_DATA_TRANSFER` line without `channel` never runs. Do not use it on other events. |
| `position` | all | Optional integer: `floor * 23 + row` (both start at 0). Row 0..22 on floor 0 is position 0..22. Row 0 is the row next to the place where you appear in the code area, the next rows go away from it. If you omit it, or it is taken, or the floor does not exist on the plot, the line goes to the first free row. Usually omit it. |
| `nodes` | all | Array of nodes. May be empty. |

- Two functions (or two loops) with the same name: only the first one is used.
- Several event lines with the same event are allowed. All of them run.

### 3.3 Node

```json
{"action": "PLAYER_MESSAGE", "args": {"texts": "Hi"}, "switches": {"separator": 0}, "target": "ALL"}
```

| key | meaning |
|---|---|
| `action` | Required. Action id from the catalog. Unknown id = `UNKNOWN_ACTION` warning, the node is kept but never runs. |
| `args` | Object: argument key → one value, or an array of values. Keys and value types come from the catalog. |
| `switches` | Object: marker key → integer option index (0 .. options-1). Missing marker = option 0. |
| `target` | Optional. Runs this one node on another group of targets (section 5.3). |
| `not` | Optional `true`. Only on conditions: inverts the result. |
| `elseIf` | Optional `true`. Only on conditions: makes this condition an "else if" of the condition right before it (section 5.4). |
| `condition` | Only on selection actions `SELECT_*_BY_CONDITION`: one condition node (without `children`) used as the filter. |
| `children` | Required on every condition and on `ELSE`. Forbidden on everything else. Array of nodes inside the bracket. |

- Gap node: `{"action":"GAP"}` is an empty slot. When the code reaches a GAP, the rest of that node list does NOT run. Exports of old plots may contain it; do not add it yourself.
- One value or an array of values: both forms are read the same way. `"texts":"Hi"` is the same as `"texts":["Hi"]`. Use an array only when you give several values.
- A JSON array in `args` always means "several slots", never a list. To pass a list into a `LIST` argument write one list value: `"list":{"list":[...]}` or a variable.
- An argument array may contain `null` for an empty chest slot. Usually you do not need it.
- The number of values in one argument must not exceed its `max` from the catalog (`TOO_MANY_VALUES`).
- Argument order inside an array is the slot order. For multi-value arguments the first value is the first slot.

## 4. Values

### 4.1 Value JSON

| JSON | type name | notes |
|---|---|---|
| `"Hello %player%"` | TEXT | Max 1024 chars are used. Placeholders work (section 7). |
| `5` | NUMBER | A JSON number. |
| `{"loc":[x,y,z,yaw,pitch]}` | LOCATION | Block coordinates in the plot world. yaw and pitch are optional (default 0). |
| `{"vec":[x,y,z]}` | VECTOR | Direction or velocity: exactly 3 numbers. +X = east, +Y = up, +Z = south. Velocity is in blocks per tick. The look direction of a player is `{"val":"CURRENT_EYE_VIEW"}`. See section 14.7. |
| `{"item":"minecraft:diamond","count":3}` | ITEM | `item` = Minecraft item id. `count` is optional (default 1, at most 6400). Optional `components` = the 1.21 item components (section 4.1.1). `{"item":"minecraft:air"}` is an empty slot item. |
| `{"potion":"minecraft:speed","duration":200,"amplifier":0}` | POTION | duration in ticks (default 1200), amplifier from 0 (default 0). |
| `{"particle":"minecraft:flame"}` | PARTICLE | Minecraft particle id. |
| `{"comp":{"text":"Hi","color":"gold"}}` | COMPONENT | JSON chat component, as an object or as a JSON string. Invalid JSON is shown as plain text. |
| `{"egg":"minecraft:zombie"}` | ENTITY_TYPE | Minecraft entity type id. |
| `{"list":[ value, ... ]}` | LIST | Elements are any values. |
| `{"map":{"key": value, ...}}` | MAP (dictionary) | Keys are text. |
| `{"set":[ value, ... ]}` | SET | Unique values. |
| `{"val":"CURRENT_HEALTH","sel":"CURRENT"}` | game value | Read when the node runs. `sel` is optional (section 8). |
| `{"var":"score"}` | variable | Optional `"save":true` (kept between restarts) and `"collection"`: `"LIST"`, `"DICT"` or `"SET"` (section 6). |
| `null` | empty slot | Only inside an argument array. |

The rule behind the table: text is a JSON string, a number is a JSON number, every other value is an object that contains exactly one type key (`loc`, `vec`, `item`, `potion`, `particle`, `comp`, `egg`, `list`, `map`, `set`, `val`, `var`); key order does not matter. `true`, `false` and nested arrays are not values (`UNKNOWN_VALUE_TYPE`).

#### 4.1.1 Items

- `{"item":"minecraft:bread","count":16}`
- `{"item":"minecraft:diamond_sword","components":{"minecraft:custom_name":"§bStarter sword","minecraft:enchantments":{"minecraft:sharpness":3}}}`
- `components` is the same map as in `/give` of Minecraft 1.21.11 (`minecraft:custom_name`, `minecraft:lore`, `minecraft:enchantments`, `minecraft:dyed_color`, …). Write it as a JSON object. Typed NBT that JSON cannot express (for example `1b`) can be given as an SNBT string instead: `"components":"{\"minecraft:unbreakable\":{}}"`. Exports write `components` as an SNBT string.
- Items are checked on import: too strong enchantments, attributes and effects are lowered, unknown item ids and too heavy items are rejected (`BAD_ITEM`). Unknown or invalid components are dropped without a message, so check the item after the import.

### 4.2 Which value fits which argument type

The argument type is in the catalog (`TEXT`, `NUMBER`, `LOCATION`, `VECTOR`, `ITEM`, `BLOCK`, `POTION`, `PARTICLE`, `COMPONENT`, `ENTITY_TYPE`, `VARIABLE`, `LIST`, `MAP`, `SET`, `ANY`). The server rule:

1. The same type always fits.
2. A variable (`{"var":..}`) fits every argument type.
3. A game value (`{"val":..}`) fits every type except `VARIABLE`.
4. `ANY` and `TEXT` arguments accept every value.
5. An ITEM value fits a `BLOCK` argument (use the block's item, for example `minecraft:gold_block`).
6. A TEXT value fits `NUMBER` and `COMPONENT` arguments. For NUMBER the text is read after placeholders, so `"%var(x)%"` or `"%math(2*3)%"` works.
7. A `VARIABLE` argument accepts ONLY a variable (`{"var":..}`). This is where actions write their result.

Anything else gives `WRONG_VALUE_TYPE`. An object without a known type key gives `UNKNOWN_VALUE_TYPE`.

### 4.3 Locations

- A location is the real block position in the plot world, the numbers the player sees with F3.
- Each plot has its own world, and plots can have different centers. Do not guess coordinates. Ask the player.
- Actions that move or spawn things keep the result inside the plot. Use coordinates of your own plot.
- To get one number out of a location (for example Y), use `VARIABLE_GET_COORDINATES_OF_LOCATION`. Give only the outputs you need. The numbers are exact (a player can stand at Y `64.5`); round them with `VARIABLE_ROUND` if needed. Example for the player's Y:
  `{"action":"VARIABLE_GET_COORDINATES_OF_LOCATION","args":{"location":{"val":"CURRENT_LOCATION"},"y":{"var":"y_%player_uuid%"}}}`
- Do not print a location as text (`%var(loc)%` or a location in a TEXT argument). It gives a long technical string. Print the coordinates one by one instead.
- Block tests (`IF_PLAYER_BLOCK_EQUALS`, `IF_PLAYER_STANDING_ON`, `IF_PLAYER_LOOKING_AT`, `IF_ENTITY_STANDING_ON_BLOCK`) compare block positions: the fractional part of your location is cut off, so `[10.7, 64, 5.2]` matches the block `10 64 5`. Write whole block coordinates. For a negative Y the cut goes toward 0 (`-10.5` matches the block at `-10`). These conditions are TRUE when the block type is one of `blocks` OR the position is one of `locations`. To require both, put one condition inside the other. With only `locations` given, the type test checks for air.

## 5. How code runs

### 5.1 Targets of a line ("selection")

Every node works on a list of targets called the selection.

- Event line: the selection starts as the default target of the event.
  - `PLAYER_*` events: the player of the event. Example: in `PLAYER_KILL_PLAYER` it is the killer; the killed player is `VICTIM`.
  - `ENTITY_*` events: usually the entity of the event. Some entity events use a player instead (for example `ENTITY_KILL_PLAYER`: default = the killed player, the mob is `%entity%` / `DEFAULT_ENTITY`).
  - `PLOT_*` events: usually the world default: the first player in the world, or the last spawned entity when no player is there. The exact starting target of every event is in its `default target` line in the event catalog. If that player is in coding mode, the selection is empty.
- Loop: on each run the selection is every player in play mode who stands inside the plot. With no such players the selection is empty.
- Function: the selection of the caller at the moment of the call.
- An action runs once for EACH target in its selection. This is true also for world and variable actions. In a loop with 5 players, `VARIABLE_INCREMENT` adds 5 times. Use `SELECT_RANDOM_PLAYER` (or another select) first if you need one run.
- Players who are not in play mode (for example coding) or not in the plot world are removed from the selection before an action runs.

### 5.2 Selection actions

- `SELECT_*` (category CREATE_SELECTION) replace the selection, `SELECT_ADD_*` add to it, `SELECT_FILTER_*` remove from it.
- The exact rule (one line run):
  1. A `SELECT_*` changes the selection for all following nodes of the same node list.
  2. The children of a bracket start with the selection made by the LAST `SELECT_*` that ran in this line run, even if that select was inside an earlier bracket. If no select ran yet, they start with the line's starting selection.
  3. When a bracket ends, the nodes after it continue with the selection they had before the bracket. A select inside the bracket does not change them.
  4. `GAME_WAIT` saves the same selection as rule 2 (see 5.5).
  5. A function gets a copy of the caller's current selection. A `SELECT_*` inside the function never changes the caller's selection.
- Safe habit: when a bracket or a `GAME_WAIT` must work on a certain group, put the `SELECT_*` right before it in the same list, or as the first child of the bracket. Never rely on rule 2 picking up a select from an earlier bracket.
- `SELECT_PLAYERS_BY_CONDITION`, `SELECT_MOBS_BY_CONDITION`, `SELECT_ENTITIES_BY_CONDITION`, `SELECT_ADD_*_BY_CONDITION` and `SELECT_FILTER_SELECTION_BY_CONDITION` need a `condition` key with ONE condition node. The condition is tested for each candidate alone. `"not": true` on that condition works. Do not give it `children`. Put the condition's `args` and `switches` INSIDE the `condition` node (the server copies them to the selection block).

```json
{"action": "SELECT_PLAYERS_BY_CONDITION", "condition": {"action": "IF_PLAYER_SNEAKING"}}
```

### 5.3 `target` on one node

`target` changes the targets of ONE node only. The selection of the line does not change. The node then runs once for EACH target of the new list, exactly like with a normal selection.

Example: in `PLAYER_JOIN`, `{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","target":"ALL","args":{"texts":"§e%player% joined"}}` sends the message once to every player in play mode. `%player%` stays the player who joined; `%selected%` is the receiver. With `"target":"ALL"` a `VARIABLE_INCREMENT` adds once per player.

On a condition, `target` only changes WHO is tested. The `children` still run for the line's selection (section 5.4).

Player target list (all nodes except IF_ENTITY conditions):

| target | who |
|---|---|
| `SELECTION` | current selection (default) |
| `DEFAULT` | default target of the event |
| `RANDOM` | one random player of the world. It is picked from ALL players there, coders too; if a coder is picked, the node does nothing this time |
| `ALL` | every player of the world who is in play mode. Players who are coding are not included |
| `KILLER` | the killer from the event |
| `DAMAGER` | the attacker from the event |
| `SHOOTER` | the shooter from the event |
| `VICTIM` | the victim of a damage or kill event |

Entity target list (only for IF_ENTITY conditions):

| target | who |
|---|---|
| `CURRENT` | current selection (default) |
| `DEFAULT` | default target of the event |
| `DEFAULT_ENTITY` | the entity of the event |
| `KILLER`, `DAMAGER`, `SHOOTER`, `PROJECTILE`, `VICTIM` | from the event |
| `RANDOM_ENTITY` | one random non-player entity in the world |
| `ALL_MOBS` | all living non-player entities |
| `ALL_ENTITIES` | all non-player entities |
| `LAST_LIVING_ENTITY` | the last living entity spawned by code |

- Write targets in UPPER CASE. An unknown target is a warning (`UNKNOWN_TARGET`) and works as the default.
- If the event has no killer/damager/etc., that target is empty and the node does nothing.
- `target` is ignored on selection nodes, `GAME_WAIT` and function calls.
- In `PLAYER_*` events, IF_ENTITY conditions without a target test the event entity (if there is one).

### 5.4 Conditions, ELSE, else-if

- A condition node has `children`. If the condition is true, the children run. Then the line continues after the condition.
- A condition gives ONE true/false for the whole selection. It does not filter: the children run for the whole selection (rule 2 in 5.2), not only for the targets that passed. To keep only the players that pass, use `SELECT_FILTER_SELECTION_BY_CONDITION` (or `SELECT_PLAYERS_BY_CONDITION`) instead of a bracket.
- `"not": true` inverts the result.
- How several targets combine depends on the condition (see the catalog). Many player conditions need all targets to pass; many variable conditions only look at the first target. With an empty selection the result may be true or false depending on the condition. Avoid conditions on an empty selection: to count players by a condition and then decide, set the counter, select by condition, `VARIABLE_INCREMENT`, then `SELECT_ALL_PLAYERS` (or `SELECT_RANDOM_PLAYER`) BEFORE the condition that reads the counter.
- ELSE: put `{"action":"ELSE","children":[...]}` DIRECTLY after the condition. It runs when the condition was false.
- Else-if: put another condition with `"elseIf": true` directly after the first condition (or after another else-if). It is tested only when all conditions before it in the chain were false. Then an `ELSE` may follow.
- The chain is: one normal condition, then any number of `elseIf` conditions, then optionally one `ELSE`. Any other node between them ends the chain.
- An `ELSE` or `elseIf` condition that does not directly follow a chain never runs.
- Do not use the `ELSE_IF` action. It is an empty editor block. Use `"elseIf": true` on a condition.

```json
{"action":"IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER","args":{"num1":{"var":"x"},"num2":10},"switches":{"state":0},"children":[ ... ]},
{"action":"IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER","elseIf":true,"args":{"num1":{"var":"x"},"num2":5},"switches":{"state":0},"children":[ ... ]},
{"action":"ELSE","children":[ ... ]}
```

### 5.5 Waiting: `GAME_WAIT`

`{"action":"GAME_WAIT","args":{"duration":3},"switches":{"state":1}}`

- `duration`: a whole number. `state`: 0 = ticks (default), 1 = seconds, 2 = minutes.
- The rest of the CURRENT node list runs later. The selection is saved at the moment of the wait: it is the selection of the last `SELECT_*` that ran in this line run (rule 2 in 5.2). Without brackets this is simply the current selection.
- Inside a bracket or a function, only the rest of that bracket or function waits. The nodes after the bracket (or after the function call) run at once, without waiting.
- If the selection is empty when `GAME_WAIT` is reached, the rest of the list is dropped.
- Longest wait: 30 minutes. Longer waits stop the code run.
- Waits are cancelled when the world leaves play mode or the code restarts.

### 5.6 Functions

- Define: `{"kind":"function","name":"reward","nodes":[...]}`.
- Call: `{"action":"CALL_FUNCTION","args":{"name":"reward"},"switches":{"async":0}}`.
  - `args.name` must be a TEXT value and not blank (`EMPTY_NAME`).
  - `async`: 0 = run now and continue after it finishes (default); 1 = run on the next tick, the caller does not wait.
- `GAME_CALL_FUNCTION_WITH_VARIABLE` does the same, but the name is read for every target, so the name can contain placeholders or a variable.
- The function gets the caller's current selection and the same event (event values and `%player%` are those of the caller's event). Selects inside the function do not change the caller's selection: after the call the caller goes on with its own selection.
- Unknown function name: nothing happens, no error.
- Color codes are ignored when a function or loop is looked up by name (`§aReward` = `Reward`).
- If the selection is empty, the call does nothing.

### 5.7 Loops

- Define: `{"kind":"loop","name":"timer","ticks":20,"nodes":[...]}`.
- A loop does NOT start by itself. Start it with `{"action":"GAME_START_LOOP","args":{"names":"timer"}}` and stop it with `GAME_STOP_LOOP`.
- `GAME_START_LOOP` needs a non-empty selection (it runs once per target; starting a running loop does nothing).
- Starting a loop that already runs does NOT restart its countdown. A new loop runs for the first time `ticks` ticks after the start, not at once.
- `GAME_STOP_LOOP` only stops future runs. A run that is going on right now (also the loop stopping itself) finishes its nodes, and nodes waiting after a `GAME_WAIT` in that run still run later. The remaining countdown is remembered: a later `GAME_START_LOOP` continues it, it does not start from 0.
- A loop starts a new run every `ticks` ticks even if the last run is still waiting in `GAME_WAIT`.
- Every run of a loop uses the event and target of the `GAME_START_LOOP` call that started it: `%player%` and event values inside the loop are those of that start.
- Good place to start loops: `PLAYER_JOIN` (it runs for every player when the world starts and when someone enters).
- Loops stop when the code restarts. The code restarts when the world switches to play mode, after an import, and when the first player enters an empty world. So also create bossbars and scoreboards and start loops in `PLAYER_JOIN`; creating an existing bossbar or scoreboard does nothing.

### 5.8 Errors and limits while running

- If an action fails, all players in the world get a chat message "error executing ..." (click teleports coders to the block). The line continues with the next node.
- Every world has limits for actions, messages, variable writes, waiting timers, teleports, entities, particles, memory and CPU time. They grow with the number of players. Normal games do not reach them.
- If a limit is exceeded, the current run of the line stops and the owner gets a message. If it happens too often, the world is switched to build mode (all code stops). Fix the code as in section 14.9.
- Avoid heavy work in fast events (`PLAYER_MOVE`, `PLAYER_TURNS_AROUND`) and in loops with small `ticks`.

### 5.9 What happens when (the life of a world)

| moment | what the server does | events, in this order |
|---|---|---|
| The owner switches the world to play mode (`/play` in build mode), or code is imported while the world plays | The code restarts. Every player who is not coding is prepared and moved to the spawn. | `PLAYER_JOIN` for each player, then `PLOT_WORLD_STARTS` once |
| A player enters a world that is in play mode | If nobody was in the world, the code restarts first. The player is prepared and moved to the spawn. | `PLAYER_JOIN` only. `PLOT_WORLD_STARTS` does NOT run |
| A player who is playing types `/play` | The code does NOT restart: variables, loops, waits and entities stay. The player is prepared again and moved to the spawn. | `PLAYER_REJOIN`, `PLAYER_QUIT`, `PLAYER_JOIN` |
| A coder types `/play` | The code restarts for the whole world. Only this player is prepared and moved. | `PLAYER_JOIN` |
| The owner switches the world to build mode (`/build`) | All code stops. | `PLOT_WORLD_STOPS` |
| A player dies | 5 ticks in spectator mode, then the old game mode back and a move to the spawn (a game mode that `PLAYER_DEATH` sets stays: it runs after this). The items drop at the death place, unless `PLAYER_SET_DEATH_DROPS` keeps them (items with Curse of Vanishing are lost). | `PLAYER_DEATH`, then `PLAYER_KILL_PLAYER` or `ENTITY_KILL_PLAYER` |
| A player leaves the world | | `PLAYER_QUIT` |

- "The code restarts" means: variables without `save` are deleted, loops stop, waiting `GAME_WAIT`s are dropped. Saved variables stay, also when NEW code is imported: a new version of a game sees the saved data and scoreboards of the old one. If the new version must start clean, give its saved variables and scoreboards new names (or remove old scoreboards with `GAME_REMOVE_SCOREBOARD`).
- Scoreboards, bossbars and holograms made by code survive a restart with their old lines and titles. Creating them again does nothing, so set their lines again when a round starts.
- Entities spawned by code (mobs, visualizers, dropped items) are never removed by the server: they stay through restarts, build mode and server restarts, because they are saved with the world. Remove them yourself (section 14.1).
- "Prepared" means: inventory, armor and off hand cleared, effects removed, full health and food, adventure mode, no flying. The last hotbar slot (slot 8) may then get the world compass (the owner always gets one, right after `PLAYER_JOIN`), so do not put game items into slot 8.
- The spawn is where players appear. In `PLAYER_JOIN` the player already stands there, so `CURRENT_LOCATION` is the spawn.

## 6. Variables

`{"var":"coins_%player_uuid%","save":true}`

- All variables are global for the whole world (plot). There is no per-player scope. For per-player data put `%player_uuid%` or `%player%` into the name. The name is read with placeholders when the node runs.
- `%player_uuid%` is ALWAYS the player of the event, also when the node runs for other selected players. In a node that runs for several selected players (after `SELECT_ALL_PLAYERS`, in a loop, with `"target":"ALL"`), write `%selected_uuid%` (or `%uuid%`): it is the target the node runs for right now. Example: reset a flag of every player with `SELECT_ALL_PLAYERS`, then `VARIABLE_SET` on `{"var":"dead_%selected_uuid%"}`.
- `save:true`: the variable is stored with the world and survives restarts.
- `save:false`: the variable lives in memory only. It is cleared when the code restarts (see 5.7).
- The `save` flag of the node that WRITES decides. Writing the same name with `save:false` makes a saved variable unsaved. Use the same `save` value for one name everywhere.
- Reading a variable that does not exist gives nothing: numbers read as the default (often 0), texts as empty.
- Names: up to 1024 chars. Color codes are removed from names.
- A list variable and a normal variable may have the same name. They do not mix. Use `"collection":"LIST"` on a variable that holds a list, `"collection":"DICT"` for a dictionary and `"collection":"SET"` for a set. It is optional for reading but helps the editor show the right item.
- Actions write results into the argument of type `VARIABLE` (often called `variable`, `output` or similar; see the catalog).
- Numbers are stored as decimals. A number variable or number value printed in text (`%var(x)%`, a variable or a JSON number in a TEXT argument, `%math(...)%`) has no trailing zeros: `5.0` is shown as `5`, `2.50` as `2.5`. Other digits are NOT cut: `10 / 3` is shown as `3.3333333333333335`, `0.1 + 0.2` as `0.30000000000000004`.
- To show fewer digits, round first with `VARIABLE_ROUND` (`scale` = digits after the dot; marker `state`: 0 = normal, 1 = down, 2 = up), then print the variable:
  `{"action":"VARIABLE_ROUND","args":{"variable":{"var":"avg"},"number":{"var":"avg"},"scale":2}}` turns `3.3333…` into `3.33`.
  Inside text you can also write `%math(round(%var(avg)% * 100) / 100)%`. Note: `round` in `%math%` rounds a half to the even number (`round(2.5)` = 2).
- A game value put straight into a TEXT argument, or read with `%value(...)%`, may be printed in raw form: `20.0` instead of `20`, or many digits (health `17.833333492279053`). To print it cleanly, first copy it into a variable (`VARIABLE_SET`, or `VARIABLE_ROUND` with the game value as `number`) and print `%var(...)%`.
- Numbers INSIDE a list, set or dict are printed with `.0`: a list of 1, 2 is shown as `[1.0, 2.0]`.
- Readable list text: put the list variable into a TEXT value or write `%var(name)%`. A list prints as `[a, b, c]` (values joined with `, `, in square brackets), a set as `(a, b)`, a dict as `{key=value, key2=value2}`. To drop the brackets, use `VARIABLE_REPLACE_TEXT` on that text. `LIST_TO_STRING` is NOT a join: it writes a multi-line debug text (``<Array `name` (3) >``, then ``#0 = `a` `` per element counting from 0, then ``</Array `name`>``). Do not show it to players.

## 7. Text, placeholders, colors

### 7.1 Placeholders

They work in TEXT values, in variable names and in texts read as numbers.

Where placeholders are replaced:
- Almost every argument: TEXT arguments (also single keys such as `DICT_GET_BY_KEY` `key`, scoreboard and bossbar names, function names), texts given to NUMBER arguments, variable names, COMPONENT values, list/dict/set variable names, and text values in ANY arguments (`VARIABLE_SET` `values`, `LIST_APPEND` `values`, `LIST_CREATE_OR_CLEAR` `values` and similar).
- NOT replaced (text is used as written), each one is noted in its catalog entry: `obj` of `IF_VARIABLE_EQUALS` / `IF_VARIABLE_NOT_EQUALS` (put the text into `objects` instead), `value` of `LIST_INSERT` and `LIST_REPLACE_BY_INDEX`, `channel_name` of the data transfer actions, and a few others. When a catalog note says "NOT replaced" or "read as written", believe it.
- Texts INSIDE a literal list, dict or set value (`{"list":["%player%"]}`) are stored as written. Use `LIST_APPEND` with text values if you need the replaced text in a list.

| placeholder | value |
|---|---|
| `%player%`, `%player_uuid%` | name / UUID of the event player (the default target if it is a player) |
| `%default%`, `%default_uuid%` | default target of the event (player or entity) |
| `%selected%`, `%selected_uuid%`, `%uuid%` | the target the node is running for right now |
| `%selection%`, `%selection_uuid%` | all targets of the node, joined with `,` (cut at 1024 chars) |
| `%damager%`, `%shooter%`, `%projectile%`, `%killer%`, `%victim%` (+ `_uuid`) | from the event |
| `%entity%`, `%entity_uuid%` | the entity of the event |
| `%random%`, `%random_uuid%` | a random online player of the world |
| `%var(name)%` | value of variable `name` (empty if missing). The name may contain placeholders: `%var(coins_%player_uuid%)%` |
| `%value(ID)%`, `%value(ID, SEL)%` | a game value by id. Without SEL it is read for the event's default target. SEL is the same list as in section 8. |
| `%math(expression)%` | calculation result |

- The short placeholders are lower case and exact. `%var(`, `%value(` and `%math(` ignore case.
- A placeholder with no entity (`%killer%` in a join event) or an unknown `%value(ID)%` becomes empty text. A misspelled placeholder or a bad `%math(...)%` stays as written.
- Math: `+ - * / % ^`, brackets, constants `pi`, `e`, functions `sin cos tan ctg asin acos atan atan2 sinh cosh tanh rad deg sqrt cbrt abs log log2 log10 exp ceil floor sign round pow(a,b) min(a,b) max(a,b) mod(a,b) fact(n) clamp(x,min,max) lerp(a,b,t) dist(a,b) randint(a,b) random(a,b) randfloat(a,b)`. `randint(a,b)` and `random(a,b)` both give a whole number from a to b-1 (if a >= b the placeholder stays unchanged); use `randfloat(a,b)` for a decimal. Trig functions use radians. Division by zero or a bad expression leaves the placeholder unchanged. Example: `%math(%var(score)% * 2 + 1)%`.

### 7.2 Colors

- Use `§` color codes in TEXT: `§a` green, `§c` red, `§e` yellow, `§6` gold, `§7` gray, `§f` white, `§l` bold, `§r` reset.
- `&` is NOT converted in JSON. Write `§`.
- Hex: `§#RRGGBB` (this is what the in-game input `&#RRGGBB` becomes). Plain `§` codes are the safest.

## 8. Game values

`{"val":"CURRENT_HEALTH","sel":"KILLER"}`

- A game value is read at the moment the node runs, for the target being processed.
- `sel` matters only for values that depend on an entity (`hasSelection` in the value catalog). Options: `CURRENT` (the target being processed; same as no `sel`), `DEFAULT`, `DEFAULT_ENTITY`, `KILLER`, `DAMAGER`, `SHOOTER`, `PROJECTILE`, `VICTIM`, `LAST_LIVING_ENTITY`. Unknown text = default target.
- Categories (full list in the value catalog):
  - ENTITY_STATISTIC: `CURRENT_*` values of the target, such as health, food, name, UUID, ping, game mode.
  - LOCATION_VALUE: positions of the target (`CURRENT_LOCATION`, `CURRENT_EYE_LOCATION`, block under it, and so on).
  - ITEM_VALUE: items of the target (`MAIN_HAND_ITEM`, armor, and so on).
  - WORLD: world values such as `PLOT_ONLINE`, `PLOT_ID`, `CURRENT_UNIX_TIME` (milliseconds), `CURRENT_TIMESTAMP`.
- `PLOT_ONLINE` counts EVERY player in the world, also players who are coding. There is no game value for "players in play mode" or for the number of mobs. Count them with a selection: set the counter to 0, select, then `VARIABLE_INCREMENT` (it runs once per selected target). Put the `VARIABLE_SET` BEFORE the select, and select again afterwards if the following nodes need another selection:
  `{"action":"VARIABLE_SET","args":{"variable":{"var":"playing"},"values":0}}`,
  `{"action":"SELECT_ALL_PLAYERS"}`,
  `{"action":"VARIABLE_INCREMENT","args":{"variable":{"var":"playing"}}}`
  For mobs use `{"action":"SELECT_MOBS_BY_CONDITION","condition":{"action":"IF_ENTITY_IS_MOB"}}` instead of `SELECT_ALL_PLAYERS` (living non-player entities; armor stands count too). With nothing selected the counter stays 0. The first node needs a non-empty selection to run, so in a loop with no players nothing is counted.
  - EVENT_VALUE: data of the current event (`EVENT_*`).
- Event values are game values too. In an event that does not give that data they return 0, empty text or nothing. Common pairs (event data key → value id): `message` → `EVENT_CHAT_MESSAGE`, `damage` → `EVENT_DAMAGE`, `final_damage` → `EVENT_FINAL_DAMAGE`, `damage_cause` → `EVENT_DAMAGE_REASON`, `block` → `EVENT_BLOCK` (as item) and `EVENT_BLOCK_LOCATION`, `item` → `EVENT_ITEM`, `from`/`to` → `EVENT_OLD_LOCATION`/`EVENT_NEW_LOCATION`, `clicked_slot` → `EVENT_CLICKED_SLOT`, `exp_amount` → `EVENT_EXP_AMOUNT`.
- Cancellable events (marked `*` below) can be cancelled with `GAME_CANCEL_EVENT`. Do it before any `GAME_WAIT`.

## 9. Events

`*` = cancellable.

- PLAYER_WORLD: PLAYER_JOIN, PLAYER_QUIT, PLAYER_REJOIN, PLAYER_BLOCK_INTERACT*, PLAYER_RIGHT_CLICK*, PLAYER_LEFT_CLICK*, PLAYER_RIGHT_CLICK_ENTITY*, PLAYER_LEASH_ENTITY*, PLAYER_TAME_ENTITY*, PLAYER_BLOCK_PLACE*, PLAYER_BLOCK_BREAK*, PLAYER_FISHING*, PLAYER_BLOCK_DAMAGE*, PLAYER_EXPERIENCE_CHANGE, PLAYER_LEVEL_CHANGE, PLAYER_VOTE, PLAYER_GOLD_TRANSACTION, PLAYER_AD, PLAYER_BUY_POINTS
- PLAYER_MOVEMENT: PLAYER_SNEAK*, PLAYER_UNSNEAK*, PLAYER_SPRINT*, PLAYER_STOP_SPRINT*, PLAYER_JUMP*, PLAYER_MOVE* (when the player steps to another block), PLAYER_TURNS_AROUND*, PLAYER_HORSE_JUMP
- PLAYER_INVENTORY: PLAYER_INVENTORY_OPEN, PLAYER_INVENTORY_CLOSE, PLAYER_CLICK_INVENTORY*, PLAYER_DRAG_INVENTORY*, PLAYER_PICKUP_ITEM*, PLAYER_DROP_ITEM*, PLAYER_SWAP_HANDS*, PLAYER_CHANGE_SLOT*, PLAYER_CRAFT_ITEM*, PLAYER_FURNACE_EXTRACT, PLAYER_DAMAGE_ITEM*, PLAYER_BREAK_ITEM, PLAYER_ITEM_MEND*, PLAYER_EDIT_BOOK*, PLAYER_MANIPULATE_ARMOR_STAND*
- PLAYER_DAMAGE: PLAYER_TAKE_DAMAGE*, PLAYER_TAKE_FALL_DAMAGE*, PLAYER_DEATH, PLAYER_DAMAGE_PLAYER*, PLAYER_PROJECTILE_DAMAGE*, PLAYER_KILL_PLAYER, PLAYER_DAMAGE_MOB*, PLAYER_DAMAGE_MOB_BY_PROJECTILE*, PLAYER_KILL_MOB, PLAYER_KILL_MOB_BY_PROJECTILE, PLAYER_CHANGE_VELOCITY*
- PLAYER_ANOTHER: PLAYER_PICKUP_EXPERIENCE*, PLAYER_CHAT*, PLAYER_LAUNCH_PROJECTILE*, PLAYER_CONSUME_ITEM*, PLAYER_FOOD_LEVEL_CHANGE*, PLAYER_RESOURCE_PACK, PLAYER_BREED_ENTITIES*
- PLOT_EVENT: PLOT_WORLD_STARTS (world switched to play mode), PLOT_WORLD_STOPS, PLOT_DATA_TRANSFER (needs `channel`), PLOT_BLOCK_IGNITE*, PLOT_BLOCK_BURN*, PLOT_BLOCK_FADE*, PLOT_PISTON_EXTEND*, PLOT_PISTON_RETRACT*, PLOT_BLOCK_EXPLODE*, PLOT_LEAVES_DECAY*, PLOT_STRUCTURE_GROW*, PLOT_BLOCK_GROW*, PLOT_BLOCK_FROM_TO*, PLOT_BLOCK_BREW*, PLOT_BREWING_STAND_FUEL, PLOT_BLOCK_FORM*, PLOT_BLOCK_SPREAD*, PLOT_BLOCK_PHYSICS*, PLOT_PORTAL_CREATE*, PLOT_NOTE_PLAY*, PLOT_BLOCK_DISPENSE*, PLOT_CAULDRON_LEVEL_CHANGE*, PLOT_SIGN_CHANGE*, PLOT_BLOCK_EXP, PLOT_INVENTORY_MOVE_ITEM, PLOT_FURNACE_SMELT*, PLOT_FURNACE_BURN
- ENTITY_EVENT: ENTITY_TAKE_DAMAGE*, ENTITY_DAMAGE_PLAYER*, ENTITY_DAMAGE_ANOTHER_ENTITY*, ENTITY_DEATH, ENTITY_DEATH_BY_PROJECTILE*, ENTITY_DEATH_BY_ANOTHER_ENTITY*, ENTITY_KILL_PLAYER, ENTITY_KNOCKBACK_BY_ENTITY, ENTITY_SPAWN*, ENTITY_REMOVE_FROM_WORLD, ENTITY_PATH_FIND*, ENTITY_TARGET*, ENTITY_TELEPORT*, ENTITY_RESURRECT*, ENTITY_REGAIN_HEALTH*, ENTITY_EXPLODE*, ENTITY_ZAP*, ENTITY_CREEPER_POWER_EVENT*, ENTITY_VEHICLE_CREATE*, ENTITY_ENTER_VEHICLE*, ENTITY_EXIT_VEHICLE*, ENTITY_VEHICLE_BLOCK_COLLISION, ENTITY_VEHICLE_ENTITY_COLLISION*, ENTITY_VEHICLE_TAKE_DAMAGE*, ENTITY_PROJECTILE_LAUNCH*, ENTITY_PROJECTILE_HIT_BLOCK, ENTITY_PROJECTILE_HIT_ENTITY, ENTITY_SHOOT_BOW*, ENTITY_INTERACT*, ENTITY_CHANGE_BLOCK*, ENTITY_BLOCK_FORM*, ENTITY_BLOCK_STARTED_FALLING*, ENTITY_BLOCK_FINISHED_FALLING*, ENTITY_BREAK_DOOR*, ENTITY_HANGING_BREAK*, ENTITY_PICKUP_ITEM*, ENTITY_INVENTORY_PICKUP_ITEM*, ENTITY_ITEM_MERGE*, ENTITY_ITEM_DESPAWN*, ENTITY_EXPERIENCE_ORB_MERGE*, ENTITY_WITCH_THROW_POTION*, ENTITY_WITCH_CONSUME_POTION*, ENTITY_VILLAGER_ACQUIRE_TRADE*, ENTITY_VILLAGER_REPLENISH_TRADE*, ENTITY_UNLEASH, ENTITY_ENDER_MAN_ESCAPE*, ENTITY_SHEEP_REGROW_WOOL*, ENTITY_SHEEP_DYE_WOOL*, ENTITY_FIREWORK_EXPLODE*, ENTITY_EXPLOSION_PRIME*

`PLAYER_CHAT` does NOT fire for: messages that start with `!` (the server cancels them and tells the player that global chat is off), commands (`/...`), players who are coding, worlds that are not in play mode, and messages that were already cancelled. So never design a chat command that starts with `!`; use another prefix, for example `#` or `.`. Put `GAME_CANCEL_EVENT` first, before other work and before any `GAME_WAIT`.

`PLOT_DATA_TRANSFER` receives data sent by `PLOT_DATA_TRANSFER_WRITE` or `PLOT_DATA_TRANSFER_WRITE_LIST` from another world of the SAME owner. The line runs only when its `channel` equals the channel of the message.

## 10. Size limits

- Lines: at most 23 per code floor. A plot with N floors accepts at most 23 × N lines (`TOO_MANY_HANDLERS`). A new plot may have only 1 floor, so keep to 23 lines unless the player says they have more floors.
- Line width: at most 92 blocks (`LINE_TOO_WIDE`). Count it like this:
  - starter block: 2;
  - each normal node (action, selection, `GAME_WAIT`, call, GAP): 2;
  - each bracket (condition or `ELSE`): 4 + the width of its children.
  - Example: starter + 10 actions + one condition with 5 actions = 2 + 20 + (4 + 10) = 36.
  - If a line gets too wide, move part of it into a function.
- The number of nodes, brackets inside brackets and the size of one value are limited too (`TOO_MANY_NODES`, `CONDITION_TOO_DEEP`, `VALUE_TOO_LARGE`). Normal games do not reach these limits.

## 11. Problems the server reports

Blocking problems reject the import. Warnings let the import continue (after confirmation), but the broken part will not work. Fix warnings too.

| code | blocking | cause | fix |
|---|---|---|---|
| EMPTY_PROGRAM | yes | no valid handlers | add at least one handler |
| BROKEN_HANDLER | yes | a handler could not be read: bad `kind`, missing `event`/`name`, a node without `action`, a non-object in `nodes`, or any malformed value inside it (`loc` with fewer than 3 numbers, a non-integer switch; `kind` is case-sensitive) | follow section 3.2 and 3.3 |
| EMPTY_NAME | no | function/loop with blank `name`, or `CALL_FUNCTION` without a text `args.name` | give a name |
| LINE_TOO_WIDE | yes | line wider than 92 | split into functions |
| BAD_NAME | yes | a function/loop name, a `CALL_FUNCTION` name or a `channel` is longer than 256 chars or is not plain text; or an action, event or `target` id is longer than 64 chars | plain short name, real id |
| VALUE_TOO_LARGE | yes | one value (one slot) is too large (a huge text, list, map, component or item) | smaller value |
| CHILDREN_REQUIRED | yes | a condition or `ELSE` without `children` | add `children` (may be `[]`) |
| CHILDREN_NOT_ALLOWED | yes | `children` on an action, selection or other control | remove `children` |
| NOT_CONDITION | yes | `not`/`elseIf` on a non-condition, or `condition` holds a non-condition | use them only with conditions |
| CONDITION_NOT_ALLOWED | yes | `condition` on a node that is not a selection | remove it, or use a `SELECT_*_BY_CONDITION` action |
| TOO_MANY_HANDLERS | yes | more lines than 23 × floors | fewer lines |
| TOO_MANY_NODES | yes | too many nodes | smaller program |
| CONDITION_TOO_DEEP | yes | too many nested brackets | flatten, use functions |
| BAD_ITEM | yes | the item cannot be read or is too heavy | use a simple normal item |
| JSON_TOO_LARGE | yes | the program is too large | smaller program |
| MARKER_OUT_OF_RANGE | yes | switch value not in 0..options-1 | use a listed option index |
| TOO_MANY_VALUES | yes | more values than the argument's `max` | fewer values |
| UNKNOWN_ACTION | no | action id does not exist | use an id from the catalog |
| UNKNOWN_EVENT | no | event id does not exist (the line never runs) | use an id from section 9 |
| UNKNOWN_GAME_VALUE | no | game value id does not exist | use an id from the value catalog |
| UNKNOWN_VALUE_TYPE | no | the value is `true`/`false`, a nested array, or an object without a type key | use section 4.1 |
| UNKNOWN_ARGUMENT | no | argument key not in the action (it is ignored) | use the keys from the message |
| UNKNOWN_MARKER | no | switch key not in the action (it is ignored) | use the listed marker keys |
| UNKNOWN_TARGET | no | target not in the list for this node | use section 5.3 |
| WRONG_VALUE_TYPE | no | value type does not fit the argument | use section 4.2 |

## 12. Complete examples

All examples were checked against the real action list. Copy their structure.

### Example A: welcome message and a one-time starter kit

```json
{"handlers": [
  {"kind": "event", "event": "PLAYER_JOIN", "nodes": [
    {"action": "PLAYER_MESSAGE", "args": {"texts": "§aWelcome, §e%player%§a!"}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_TITLE", "args": {"title": "§6Welcome!", "subtitle": "§7Have fun", "duration": 60}},
    {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EXIST", "not": true, "args": {"variable": {"var": "kit_%player_uuid%", "save": true}}, "children": [
      {"action": "PLAYER_GIVE_ITEMS", "args": {"items": [{"item": "minecraft:stone_sword"}, {"item": "minecraft:bread", "count": 16}]}},
      {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "kit_%player_uuid%", "save": true}, "values": 1}},
      {"action": "PLAYER_MESSAGE", "args": {"texts": "§7You received the starter kit."}}
    ]}
  ]}
]}
```

### Example B: parkour with a timer and a saved best time

Gold block = start, diamond block = finish. Times are in seconds.

```json
{"handlers": [
  {"kind": "event", "event": "PLAYER_MOVE", "nodes": [
    {"action": "IF_PLAYER_STANDING_ON", "args": {"blocks": {"item": "minecraft:gold_block"}}, "children": [
      {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "pk_start_%player_uuid%"}, "values": {"val": "CURRENT_UNIX_TIME"}}},
      {"action": "PLAYER_ACTION_BAR", "args": {"message": "§eTimer started!"}}
    ]},
    {"action": "IF_PLAYER_STANDING_ON", "elseIf": true, "args": {"blocks": {"item": "minecraft:diamond_block"}}, "children": [
      {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "pk_start_%player_uuid%"}, "num2": 0}, "switches": {"state": 0}, "children": [
        {"action": "VARIABLE_DIFFERENCE", "args": {"variable": {"var": "pk_time_%player_uuid%"}, "number": {"val": "CURRENT_UNIX_TIME"}, "numbers": {"var": "pk_start_%player_uuid%"}}},
        {"action": "VARIABLE_QUOTIENT", "args": {"variable": {"var": "pk_time_%player_uuid%"}, "number": {"var": "pk_time_%player_uuid%"}, "numbers": 1000}},
        {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "pk_start_%player_uuid%"}, "values": 0}},
        {"action": "PLAYER_MESSAGE", "args": {"texts": "§aFinish! Time: §e%var(pk_time_%player_uuid%)%§a s"}},
        {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EXIST", "not": true, "args": {"variable": {"var": "pk_best_%player_uuid%", "save": true}}, "children": [
          {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "pk_best_%player_uuid%", "save": true}, "values": {"var": "pk_time_%player_uuid%"}}}
        ]},
        {"action": "ELSE", "children": [
          {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "pk_time_%player_uuid%"}, "num2": {"var": "pk_best_%player_uuid%", "save": true}}, "switches": {"state": 2}, "children": [
            {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "pk_best_%player_uuid%", "save": true}, "values": {"var": "pk_time_%player_uuid%"}}},
            {"action": "PLAYER_MESSAGE", "args": {"texts": "§6New personal best!"}}
          ]}
        ]}
      ]}
    ]}
  ]}
]}
```

Notes: `IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER` state 0 = `num1 > num2`, 1 = `>=`, 2 = `<`, 3 = `<=`. `pk_start` is set to 0 at the finish so the finish counts once. `pk_best` is saved, so it survives restarts.

### Example C: bossbar updated by a loop

```json
{"handlers": [
  {"kind": "event", "event": "PLAYER_JOIN", "nodes": [
    {"action": "GAME_CREATE_BOSSBAR", "args": {"name": "hud", "title": "§eLoading..."}, "switches": {"state": 2, "state1": 0}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_SHOW_BOSSBAR", "args": {"name": "hud"}},
    {"action": "GAME_START_LOOP", "args": {"names": "hud_loop"}}
  ]},
  {"kind": "loop", "name": "hud_loop", "ticks": 20, "nodes": [
    {"action": "SELECT_RANDOM_PLAYER"},
    {"action": "VARIABLE_INCREMENT", "args": {"variable": {"var": "hud_seconds"}, "number": 1}},
    {"action": "GAME_SET_BOSSBAR_TITLE", "args": {"name": "hud", "title": "§eOnline: §f%value(PLOT_ONLINE)% §7| §eUptime: §f%var(hud_seconds)%s"}},
    {"action": "GAME_SET_BOSSBAR_PROGRESS", "args": {"name": "hud", "progress": "%math(mod(%var(hud_seconds)%, 60) * 100 / 60)%"}}
  ]}
]}
```

Notes: `SELECT_RANDOM_PLAYER` makes the rest of the loop run once, not once per player. Bossbar progress is 0..100. Bossbar color `state`: 0 red, 1 blue, 2 yellow, 3 green, 4 pink, 5 white, 6 purple; style `state1`: 0 solid, 1..4 = 6, 10, 12, 20 segments.

### Example D: PvP kill counter with a function

```json
{"handlers": [
  {"kind": "event", "event": "PLAYER_JOIN", "nodes": [
    {"action": "GAME_CREATE_SCOREBOARD", "args": {"name": "kills", "display_name": "§c§lKills"}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_SHOW_SCOREBOARD", "args": {"name": "kills"}},
    {"action": "CALL_FUNCTION", "args": {"name": "update_kills"}, "switches": {"async": 0}}
  ]},
  {"kind": "event", "event": "PLAYER_KILL_PLAYER", "nodes": [
    {"action": "VARIABLE_INCREMENT", "args": {"variable": {"var": "kills_%player_uuid%", "save": true}}},
    {"action": "CALL_FUNCTION", "args": {"name": "update_kills"}, "switches": {"async": 0}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_MESSAGE", "target": "ALL", "args": {"texts": "§c%victim% §7was killed by §a%player%"}}
  ]},
  {"kind": "function", "name": "update_kills", "nodes": [
    {"action": "GAME_SET_SCOREBOARD_SCORE", "args": {"name": "kills", "tag": "%player%", "score": {"var": "kills_%player_uuid%", "save": true}}},
    {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "kills_%player_uuid%", "save": true}, "num2": 10}, "switches": {"state": 1}, "children": [
      {"action": "PLAYER_TITLE", "args": {"title": "§6Killing spree!", "subtitle": "§e%var(kills_%player_uuid%)% kills"}}
    ]}
  ]}
]}
```

Notes: in `PLAYER_KILL_PLAYER` the selection and `%player%` are the killer, `%victim%` is the killed player. The function runs for the same player and sees the same event. `VARIABLE_INCREMENT` without `number` adds 1.

### Example E: a complete game: zombie waves and a hoe weapon

The player gets a hoe. A hit with it does 6 damage, a right click blasts the zombies around (2 s cooldown). A loop sends 4 zombies when none are left. 10 kills win; `/play` starts a new round. It follows section 14: the round has a `phase` guard, the zombies have a name and are removed at the start and the end, kills are counted only for named zombies while the round runs, and the zombies spawn at the world spawn, the only place that is surely free.

```json
{"handlers": [
  {"kind": "event", "event": "PLAYER_JOIN", "nodes": [
    {"action": "PLAYER_SET_ITEMS", "args": {"hotbar": [{"item": "minecraft:iron_hoe", "components": {"minecraft:custom_name": "§6Zombie Hoe", "minecraft:lore": ["§7Hit: 6 damage", "§7Right click: blast (2 s)"]}}]}, "switches": {"state": 1}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_SET_DEATH_DROPS", "switches": {"enable": 0}},
    {"action": "GAME_CREATE_SCOREBOARD", "args": {"name": "zh", "display_name": "§c§lZombie hunt"}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_SHOW_SCOREBOARD", "args": {"name": "zh"}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_MESSAGE", "args": {"texts": "§aKill §e10 §azombies! §7Hit with the hoe, right click = blast."}},
    {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "phase"}, "num2": 1}, "switches": {"state": 2}, "children": [
      {"action": "CALL_FUNCTION", "args": {"name": "start_round"}, "switches": {"async": 0}}
    ]}
  ]},
  {"kind": "function", "name": "start_round", "nodes": [
    {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "phase"}, "values": 1}},
    {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "kills"}, "values": 0}},
    {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "wave"}, "values": 0}},
    {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "arena"}, "values": {"val": "CURRENT_LOCATION"}}},
    {"action": "GAME_SET_SCOREBOARD_SCORE", "args": {"name": "zh", "tag": "§fKills", "score": 0}},
    {"action": "GAME_SET_GAME_RULE_VALUE", "args": {"value": "false"}, "switches": {"rule": 6}},
    {"action": "GAME_TIME", "args": {"time": 18000}, "switches": {"state": 1}},
    {"action": "GAME_START_LOOP", "args": {"names": "waves"}},
    {"action": "CALL_FUNCTION", "args": {"name": "remove_zombies"}, "switches": {"async": 0}}
  ]},
  {"kind": "function", "name": "remove_zombies", "nodes": [
    {"action": "SELECT_MOBS_BY_CONDITION", "condition": {"action": "IF_ENTITY_NAME_EQUALS", "args": {"name": "Wave zombie"}}},
    {"action": "ENTITY_REMOVE"}
  ]},
  {"kind": "loop", "name": "waves", "ticks": 60, "nodes": [
    {"action": "SELECT_RANDOM_PLAYER"},
    {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "alive"}, "values": 0}},
    {"action": "SELECT_MOBS_BY_CONDITION", "condition": {"action": "IF_ENTITY_NAME_EQUALS", "args": {"name": "Wave zombie"}}},
    {"action": "VARIABLE_INCREMENT", "args": {"variable": {"var": "alive"}}},
    {"action": "SELECT_RANDOM_PLAYER"},
    {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "alive"}, "num2": 1}, "switches": {"state": 2}, "children": [
      {"action": "CALL_FUNCTION", "args": {"name": "next_wave"}, "switches": {"async": 0}}
    ]}
  ]},
  {"kind": "function", "name": "next_wave", "nodes": [
    {"action": "VARIABLE_INCREMENT", "args": {"variable": {"var": "wave"}}},
    {"action": "GAME_SPAWN_MOB", "args": {"mob": {"item": "minecraft:zombie_spawn_egg"}, "locations": [{"var": "arena"}, {"var": "arena"}, {"var": "arena"}, {"var": "arena"}], "name": "Wave zombie", "max_hp": 8, "helmet": {"item": "minecraft:leather_helmet"}}, "switches": {"far_away": 1}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_TITLE", "target": "ALL", "args": {"title": "§6Wave %var(wave)%", "subtitle": "§74 zombies", "duration": 40}}
  ]},
  {"kind": "event", "event": "ENTITY_DAMAGE_ANOTHER_ENTITY", "nodes": [
    {"action": "IF_PLAYER_HOLDING_ITEM", "target": "DAMAGER", "args": {"items": {"item": "minecraft:iron_hoe", "components": {"minecraft:custom_name": "§6Zombie Hoe", "minecraft:lore": ["§7Hit: 6 damage", "§7Right click: blast (2 s)"]}}}, "switches": {"durability": 1, "state": 1}, "children": [
      {"action": "GAME_SET_DAMAGE_OF_EVENT", "args": {"damage": 6}}
    ]}
  ]},
  {"kind": "event", "event": "PLAYER_RIGHT_CLICK", "nodes": [
    {"action": "IF_PLAYER_HOLDING_ITEM", "args": {"items": {"item": "minecraft:iron_hoe", "components": {"minecraft:custom_name": "§6Zombie Hoe", "minecraft:lore": ["§7Hit: 6 damage", "§7Right click: blast (2 s)"]}}}, "switches": {"durability": 1, "state": 1}, "children": [
      {"action": "GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"},
      {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"val": "CURRENT_UNIX_TIME"}, "num2": {"var": "cd_%player_uuid%"}}, "switches": {"state": 1}, "children": [
        {"action": "VARIABLE_SUM", "args": {"variable": {"var": "cd_%player_uuid%"}, "number": {"val": "CURRENT_UNIX_TIME"}, "numbers": 2000}},
        {"action": "PLAYER_ACTION_BAR", "args": {"message": "§6Blast!"}},
        {"action": "SELECT_MOBS_BY_CONDITION", "condition": {"action": "IF_ENTITY_NAME_EQUALS", "args": {"name": "Wave zombie"}}},
        {"action": "SELECT_FILTER_BY_DISTANCE", "args": {"center": {"val": "CURRENT_LOCATION", "sel": "DEFAULT"}, "radius": 4}},
        {"action": "PLAYER_DAMAGE", "args": {"damage": 5}},
        {"action": "PLAYER_LAUNCH_UPWARDS", "args": {"multiplier": 0.8}}
      ]},
      {"action": "ELSE", "children": [
        {"action": "PLAYER_ACTION_BAR", "args": {"message": "§7The blast is recharging..."}}
      ]}
    ]}
  ]},
  {"kind": "event", "event": "ENTITY_DEATH", "nodes": [
    {"action": "IF_ENTITY_NAME_EQUALS", "args": {"name": "Wave zombie"}, "children": [
      {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "phase"}, "num2": 1}, "switches": {"state": 1}, "children": [
        {"action": "VARIABLE_INCREMENT", "args": {"variable": {"var": "kills"}}},
        {"action": "GAME_SET_SCOREBOARD_SCORE", "args": {"name": "zh", "tag": "§fKills", "score": {"var": "kills"}}},
        {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "kills"}, "num2": 10}, "switches": {"state": 1}, "children": [
          {"action": "CALL_FUNCTION", "args": {"name": "end_round"}, "switches": {"async": 0}}
        ]}
      ]}
    ]}
  ]},
  {"kind": "function", "name": "end_round", "nodes": [
    {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "phase"}, "values": 0}},
    {"action": "GAME_STOP_LOOP", "args": {"names": "waves"}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_TITLE", "target": "ALL", "args": {"title": "§a§lYOU WIN", "subtitle": "§7%var(kills)% zombies killed", "duration": 80}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_MESSAGE", "target": "ALL", "args": {"texts": "§aYou won! §7Type /play to play again."}},
    {"action": "CALL_FUNCTION", "args": {"name": "remove_zombies"}, "switches": {"async": 0}}
  ]}
]}
```

Notes: `arena` is the world spawn because `PLAYER_JOIN` runs when the player stands there. To use another arena, ask the player for its coordinates and put them into `arena` (with `.5`, see 14.3). The zombies wear a helmet, so they do not burn even if the night is turned off. The blast uses `PLAYER_DAMAGE`, which has no attacker, so kills are counted in `ENTITY_DEATH`, not in `PLAYER_KILL_MOB`. `remove_zombies` is a function, so its select does not change the selection of `start_round` and `end_round`.

### Example F: a shop menu with coins

An emerald in the hotbar opens a menu. A click on an item buys it when the player has enough saved coins (every player starts with 50).

```json
{"handlers": [
  {"kind": "event", "event": "PLAYER_JOIN", "nodes": [
    {"action": "PLAYER_SET_ITEMS", "args": {"hotbar": [{"item": "minecraft:emerald", "components": {"minecraft:custom_name": "§aShop", "minecraft:lore": ["§7Right click to open"]}}]}, "switches": {"state": 1}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_SET_DEATH_DROPS", "switches": {"enable": 0}},
    {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EXIST", "not": true, "args": {"variable": {"var": "coins_%player_uuid%", "save": true}}, "children": [
      {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "coins_%player_uuid%", "save": true}, "values": 50}}
    ]},
    {"action": "PLAYER_ACTION_BAR", "args": {"message": "§6Coins: §e%var(coins_%player_uuid%)%"}}
  ]},
  {"kind": "event", "event": "PLAYER_RIGHT_CLICK", "nodes": [
    {"action": "IF_PLAYER_HOLDING_ITEM", "args": {"items": {"item": "minecraft:emerald", "components": {"minecraft:custom_name": "§aShop", "minecraft:lore": ["§7Right click to open"]}}}, "switches": {"state": 1}, "children": [
      {"action": "GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"},
      {"action": "PLAYER_OPEN_MENU", "args": {"name": "§2Shop", "items": [null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
        {"item": "minecraft:iron_sword", "components": {"minecraft:lore": ["§e20 coins"]}}, null,
        {"item": "minecraft:golden_apple", "components": {"minecraft:lore": ["§e10 coins"]}}, null,
        {"item": "minecraft:arrow", "count": 16, "components": {"minecraft:lore": ["§e5 coins"]}}]}, "switches": {"separator": 0}}
    ]}
  ]},
  {"kind": "event", "event": "PLAYER_CLICK_INVENTORY", "nodes": [
    {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_TEXT_EQUALS", "args": {"text": {"val": "CURRENT_INVENTORY_TITLE"}, "texts": "Shop"}, "switches": {"colors": 1}, "children": [
      {"action": "GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"},
      {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_TEXT_EQUALS", "args": {"text": {"val": "EVENT_TYPE_OF_CLICKED_INVENTORY"}, "texts": "CHEST"}, "children": [
        {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EQUALS", "args": {"obj": {"val": "EVENT_CLICKED_SLOT"}, "objects": 11}, "children": [
          {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "price_%player_uuid%"}, "values": 20}},
          {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "goods_%player_uuid%"}, "values": {"item": "minecraft:iron_sword"}}},
          {"action": "CALL_FUNCTION", "args": {"name": "buy"}, "switches": {"async": 0}}
        ]},
        {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EQUALS", "elseIf": true, "args": {"obj": {"val": "EVENT_CLICKED_SLOT"}, "objects": 13}, "children": [
          {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "price_%player_uuid%"}, "values": 10}},
          {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "goods_%player_uuid%"}, "values": {"item": "minecraft:golden_apple"}}},
          {"action": "CALL_FUNCTION", "args": {"name": "buy"}, "switches": {"async": 0}}
        ]},
        {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EQUALS", "elseIf": true, "args": {"obj": {"val": "EVENT_CLICKED_SLOT"}, "objects": 15}, "children": [
          {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "price_%player_uuid%"}, "values": 5}},
          {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "goods_%player_uuid%"}, "values": {"item": "minecraft:arrow", "count": 16}}},
          {"action": "CALL_FUNCTION", "args": {"name": "buy"}, "switches": {"async": 0}}
        ]}
      ]}
    ]}
  ]},
  {"kind": "function", "name": "buy", "nodes": [
    {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "coins_%player_uuid%", "save": true}, "num2": {"var": "price_%player_uuid%"}}, "switches": {"state": 1}, "children": [
      {"action": "VARIABLE_DIFFERENCE", "args": {"variable": {"var": "coins_%player_uuid%", "save": true}, "number": {"var": "coins_%player_uuid%", "save": true}, "numbers": {"var": "price_%player_uuid%"}}},
      {"action": "PLAYER_GIVE_ITEMS", "args": {"items": {"var": "goods_%player_uuid%"}}},
      {"action": "PLAYER_SOUND", "args": {"sound": "entity.experience_orb.pickup"}},
      {"action": "PLAYER_ACTION_BAR", "args": {"message": "§aBought! §6Coins: §e%var(coins_%player_uuid%)%"}}
    ]},
    {"action": "ELSE", "children": [
      {"action": "PLAYER_SOUND", "args": {"sound": "entity.villager.no"}},
      {"action": "PLAYER_ACTION_BAR", "args": {"message": "§cNot enough coins §7(%var(coins_%player_uuid%)%)"}}
    ]}
  ]}
]}
```

Notes: the menu is recognised by its title (`CURRENT_INVENTORY_TITLE`, compared without colors), and the click is cancelled first, so the player cannot take the items out. `EVENT_TYPE_OF_CLICKED_INVENTORY` = `CHEST` keeps only clicks in the menu itself: a click in the player's own inventory below has its own slot numbers (0, 1, 2...) and would otherwise buy too. Functions have no parameters, so every button writes the price and the item into per-player variables and calls the same `buy` function. Add a button: one more `elseIf` with its slot, price and item, and the item in `PLAYER_OPEN_MENU` at that slot.

### Example G: a round for several players, with saved wins and a top list

The round starts when 2 players are in the world (5 second countdown). Dead players watch in spectator mode, the last one alive wins. Wins are saved; the scoreboard shows them (it sorts by score itself) and the chat shows the top 3.

```json
{"handlers": [
  {"kind": "event", "event": "PLAYER_JOIN", "nodes": [
    {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "spawn"}, "values": {"val": "CURRENT_LOCATION"}}},
    {"action": "GAME_CREATE_SCOREBOARD", "args": {"name": "wins", "display_name": "§6§lWins"}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_SHOW_SCOREBOARD", "args": {"name": "wins"}},
    {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "phase"}, "num2": 1}, "switches": {"state": 1}, "children": [
      {"action": "PLAYER_SET_GAMEMODE", "switches": {"gm": 3}},
      {"action": "PLAYER_MESSAGE", "args": {"texts": "§7A round is running. You watch until the next one."}}
    ]},
    {"action": "ELSE", "children": [
      {"action": "PLAYER_MESSAGE", "args": {"texts": "§eLast one standing wins! §7The round starts when 2 players are here."}}
    ]},
    {"action": "GAME_START_LOOP", "args": {"names": "game"}}
  ]},
  {"kind": "loop", "name": "game", "ticks": 20, "nodes": [
    {"action": "SELECT_RANDOM_PLAYER"},
    {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "phase"}, "num2": 1}, "switches": {"state": 2}, "children": [
      {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "playing"}, "values": 0}},
      {"action": "SELECT_ALL_PLAYERS"},
      {"action": "VARIABLE_INCREMENT", "args": {"variable": {"var": "playing"}}},
      {"action": "SELECT_RANDOM_PLAYER"},
      {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "playing"}, "num2": 2}, "switches": {"state": 1}, "children": [
        {"action": "VARIABLE_INCREMENT", "args": {"variable": {"var": "countdown"}}},
        {"action": "PLAYER_TITLE", "target": "ALL", "args": {"title": "§e%math(6 - %var(countdown)%)%", "subtitle": "§7get ready", "duration": 20}},
        {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "countdown"}, "num2": 5}, "switches": {"state": 1}, "children": [
          {"action": "CALL_FUNCTION", "args": {"name": "start_round"}, "switches": {"async": 0}}
        ]}
      ]},
      {"action": "ELSE", "children": [
        {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "countdown"}, "values": 0}},
        {"action": "PLAYER_ACTION_BAR", "target": "ALL", "args": {"message": "§7Waiting for players: §e%var(playing)%§7/2"}}
      ]}
    ]},
    {"action": "ELSE", "children": [
      {"action": "LIST_SIZE", "args": {"list": {"var": "alive"}, "output": {"var": "alive_count"}}},
      {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "alive_count"}, "num2": 1}, "switches": {"state": 3}, "children": [
        {"action": "CALL_FUNCTION", "args": {"name": "end_round"}, "switches": {"async": 0}}
      ]}
    ]}
  ]},
  {"kind": "function", "name": "start_round", "nodes": [
    {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "phase"}, "values": 1}},
    {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "countdown"}, "values": 0}},
    {"action": "LIST_CREATE_OR_CLEAR", "args": {"list": {"var": "alive"}}},
    {"action": "SELECT_ALL_PLAYERS"},
    {"action": "LIST_APPEND", "args": {"list": {"var": "alive"}, "values": "%selected%"}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_SET_GAMEMODE", "switches": {"gm": 2}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_TELEPORT", "args": {"location": {"var": "spawn"}}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_SET_ITEMS", "args": {"hotbar": [{"item": "minecraft:stone_sword"}]}, "switches": {"state": 0}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_TITLE", "args": {"title": "§c§lFIGHT!", "subtitle": "§7last one standing wins", "duration": 40}}
  ]},
  {"kind": "event", "event": "PLAYER_DEATH", "nodes": [
    {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "phase"}, "num2": 1}, "switches": {"state": 1}, "children": [
      {"action": "LIST_REMOVE", "args": {"list": {"var": "alive"}, "value": "%player%"}, "switches": {"remove_mode": 2}},
      {"action": "PLAYER_SET_GAMEMODE", "switches": {"gm": 3}},
      {"action": "PLAYER_MESSAGE", "target": "ALL", "args": {"texts": "§c%player% is out!"}}
    ]}
  ]},
  {"kind": "event", "event": "PLAYER_QUIT", "nodes": [
    {"action": "LIST_REMOVE", "args": {"list": {"var": "alive"}, "value": "%player%"}, "switches": {"remove_mode": 2}}
  ]},
  {"kind": "function", "name": "end_round", "nodes": [
    {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "phase"}, "values": 0}},
    {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "alive_count"}, "num2": 1}, "switches": {"state": 1}, "children": [
      {"action": "LIST_GET_BY_INDEX", "args": {"list": {"var": "alive"}, "index": 1, "output_var": {"var": "winner"}}},
      {"action": "DICT_GET_BY_KEY", "args": {"dict": {"var": "wins", "save": true}, "key": "%var(winner)%", "output_var": {"var": "winner_wins"}}},
      {"action": "VARIABLE_INCREMENT", "args": {"variable": {"var": "winner_wins"}}},
      {"action": "DICT_PUT", "args": {"dict": {"var": "wins", "save": true}, "key": "%var(winner)%", "value": {"var": "winner_wins"}}},
      {"action": "GAME_SET_SCOREBOARD_SCORE", "args": {"name": "wins", "tag": "%var(winner)%", "score": {"var": "winner_wins"}}},
      {"action": "PLAYER_TITLE", "target": "ALL", "args": {"title": "§6%var(winner)% wins!", "subtitle": "§7wins: %var(winner_wins)%", "duration": 60}},
      {"action": "CALL_FUNCTION", "args": {"name": "show_top"}, "switches": {"async": 0}}
    ]},
    {"action": "SELECT_ALL_PLAYERS"},
    {"action": "PLAYER_SET_GAMEMODE", "switches": {"gm": 2}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_TELEPORT", "args": {"location": {"var": "spawn"}}}
  ]},
  {"kind": "function", "name": "show_top", "nodes": [
    {"action": "DICT_SORT", "args": {"input_dict": {"var": "wins", "save": true}, "output_dict": {"var": "wins_top"}}, "switches": {"sort_by": 1, "asc_sort": 1}},
    {"action": "DICT_SIZE", "args": {"dict": {"var": "wins_top"}, "output": {"var": "top_size"}}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_MESSAGE", "target": "ALL", "args": {"texts": "§6§lTop players:"}},
    {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "top_size"}, "num2": 1}, "switches": {"state": 1}, "children": [
      {"action": "DICT_KEY_BY_INDEX", "args": {"dict": {"var": "wins_top"}, "index": 1, "output_var": {"var": "top_name"}}},
      {"action": "DICT_VALUE_BY_INDEX", "args": {"dict": {"var": "wins_top"}, "index": 1, "output_var": {"var": "top_wins"}}},
      {"action": "PLAYER_MESSAGE", "target": "ALL", "args": {"texts": "§e1. §f%var(top_name)% §7- %var(top_wins)%"}}
    ]},
    {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "top_size"}, "num2": 2}, "switches": {"state": 1}, "children": [
      {"action": "DICT_KEY_BY_INDEX", "args": {"dict": {"var": "wins_top"}, "index": 2, "output_var": {"var": "top_name"}}},
      {"action": "DICT_VALUE_BY_INDEX", "args": {"dict": {"var": "wins_top"}, "index": 2, "output_var": {"var": "top_wins"}}},
      {"action": "PLAYER_MESSAGE", "target": "ALL", "args": {"texts": "§e2. §f%var(top_name)% §7- %var(top_wins)%"}}
    ]},
    {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "top_size"}, "num2": 3}, "switches": {"state": 1}, "children": [
      {"action": "DICT_KEY_BY_INDEX", "args": {"dict": {"var": "wins_top"}, "index": 3, "output_var": {"var": "top_name"}}},
      {"action": "DICT_VALUE_BY_INDEX", "args": {"dict": {"var": "wins_top"}, "index": 3, "output_var": {"var": "top_wins"}}},
      {"action": "PLAYER_MESSAGE", "target": "ALL", "args": {"texts": "§e3. §f%var(top_name)% §7- %var(top_wins)%"}}
    ]}
  ]}
]}
```

Notes: ONE loop owns the round: in the lobby it counts players and runs the countdown, during the round it checks how many are alive. Events only change shared data (`PLAYER_DEATH` and `PLAYER_QUIT` remove the player from the `alive` list), so a death, a quit and a `/play` in the middle of the round are all handled the same way and the round can never end twice. The `alive` list holds names (`%selected%` when it is filled for every selected player). A player who joins during a round becomes a spectator until the next one. Top lists: `DICT_SORT` by value, descending, then read the first places with `DICT_KEY_BY_INDEX` / `DICT_VALUE_BY_INDEX` (positions start at 1); check `DICT_SIZE` first, because a missing place reads as the number 0.

### Example H: a boss fight with a bossbar

A boss spawns when the fight starts. The bossbar shows its health. Every 5 seconds it stomps: players within 6 blocks take damage and are pushed away (section 14.7, recipe 3). Killing it wins; `/play` starts a new fight.

```json
{"handlers": [
  {"kind": "event", "event": "PLAYER_JOIN", "nodes": [
    {"action": "PLAYER_SET_ITEMS", "args": {"hotbar": [{"item": "minecraft:iron_sword"}]}, "switches": {"state": 1}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_SET_DEATH_DROPS", "switches": {"enable": 0}},
    {"action": "GAME_CREATE_BOSSBAR", "args": {"name": "boss", "title": "§4§lStone King"}, "switches": {"state": 0, "state1": 2}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_SHOW_BOSSBAR", "args": {"name": "boss"}},
    {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "phase"}, "num2": 1}, "switches": {"state": 2}, "children": [
      {"action": "CALL_FUNCTION", "args": {"name": "start_fight"}, "switches": {"async": 0}}
    ]}
  ]},
  {"kind": "function", "name": "start_fight", "nodes": [
    {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "phase"}, "values": 1}},
    {"action": "CALL_FUNCTION", "args": {"name": "remove_boss"}, "switches": {"async": 0}},
    {"action": "GAME_SPAWN_MOB", "args": {"mob": {"item": "minecraft:husk_spawn_egg"}, "locations": {"val": "CURRENT_LOCATION"}, "name": "Stone King", "max_hp": 60, "damage": 3, "helmet": {"item": "minecraft:golden_helmet"}}, "switches": {"far_away": 1, "name_visible": 1}},
    {"action": "GAME_SET_BOSSBAR_PROGRESS", "args": {"name": "boss", "progress": 100}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_TITLE", "target": "ALL", "args": {"title": "§4§lStone King", "subtitle": "§7defeat the boss", "duration": 60}},
    {"action": "GAME_START_LOOP", "args": {"names": "boss_attack"}}
  ]},
  {"kind": "function", "name": "remove_boss", "nodes": [
    {"action": "SELECT_MOBS_BY_CONDITION", "condition": {"action": "IF_ENTITY_NAME_EQUALS", "args": {"name": "Stone King"}}},
    {"action": "ENTITY_REMOVE"}
  ]},
  {"kind": "event", "event": "ENTITY_TAKE_DAMAGE", "nodes": [
    {"action": "IF_ENTITY_NAME_EQUALS", "args": {"name": "Stone King"}, "children": [
      {"action": "GAME_SET_BOSSBAR_PROGRESS", "args": {"name": "boss", "progress": "%math(max(0, %value(CURRENT_HEALTH)% - %value(EVENT_FINAL_DAMAGE)%) * 100 / 60)%"}}
    ]}
  ]},
  {"kind": "loop", "name": "boss_attack", "ticks": 100, "nodes": [
    {"action": "SELECT_MOBS_BY_CONDITION", "condition": {"action": "IF_ENTITY_NAME_EQUALS", "args": {"name": "Stone King"}}},
    {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "boss_loc"}, "values": {"val": "CURRENT_LOCATION"}}},
    {"action": "SELECT_ALL_PLAYERS"},
    {"action": "SELECT_FILTER_BY_DISTANCE", "args": {"center": {"var": "boss_loc"}, "radius": 6}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_ACTION_BAR", "args": {"message": "§cThe Stone King stomps!"}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_DAMAGE", "args": {"damage": 3}},
    {"action": "VARIABLE_CREATE_VECTOR_BETWEEN_LOCATIONS", "args": {"variable": {"var": "kb_%uuid%"}, "location": {"var": "boss_loc"}, "location2": {"val": "CURRENT_LOCATION"}}},
    {"action": "VARIABLE_HADAMARD_PRODUCT_OF_VECTORS", "args": {"variable": {"var": "kb_%uuid%"}, "vector": {"var": "kb_%uuid%"}, "vector2": {"vec": [1, 0, 1]}}},
    {"action": "VARIABLE_SET_LENGTH_OF_VECTOR", "args": {"variable": {"var": "kb_%uuid%"}, "vector": {"var": "kb_%uuid%"}, "length": 1.2}},
    {"action": "VARIABLE_GET_COORDINATES_OF_VECTOR", "args": {"vector": {"var": "kb_%uuid%"}, "x": {"var": "kx_%uuid%"}, "z": {"var": "kz_%uuid%"}}},
    {"action": "PLAYER_LAUNCH_TOWARD", "args": {"x": {"var": "kx_%uuid%"}, "y": 0.6, "z": {"var": "kz_%uuid%"}}}
  ]},
  {"kind": "event", "event": "ENTITY_DEATH", "nodes": [
    {"action": "IF_ENTITY_NAME_EQUALS", "args": {"name": "Stone King"}, "children": [
      {"action": "IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER", "args": {"num1": {"var": "phase"}, "num2": 1}, "switches": {"state": 1}, "children": [
        {"action": "VARIABLE_SET", "args": {"variable": {"var": "phase"}, "values": 0}},
        {"action": "GAME_STOP_LOOP", "args": {"names": "boss_attack"}},
        {"action": "GAME_SET_BOSSBAR_PROGRESS", "args": {"name": "boss", "progress": 0}},
        {"action": "PLAYER_TITLE", "target": "ALL", "args": {"title": "§6§lVICTORY", "subtitle": "§7the Stone King is defeated", "duration": 80}},
        {"action": "PLAYER_SOUND", "target": "ALL", "args": {"sound": "ui.toast.challenge_complete"}},
        {"action": "PLAYER_MESSAGE", "target": "ALL", "args": {"texts": "§aThe boss is defeated! §7Type /play to fight again."}}
      ]}
    ]}
  ]}
]}
```

Notes: `ENTITY_TAKE_DAMAGE` runs BEFORE the damage is applied, so the new health is `CURRENT_HEALTH - EVENT_FINAL_DAMAGE` (and `max(0, ...)` so it does not go below 0); `* 100 / 60` turns it into percent of the boss's `max_hp` 60. The loop selects the boss by name and saves its location before it selects the players. The boss spawns at the world spawn (section 14.3); for a real arena use its coordinates.

## 13. Common mistakes (check before you answer)

1. Inventing ids. Every `action`, `event`, game value `id`, argument key and marker key must exist in this file. If you cannot find one, say so to the player instead of guessing.
2. Writing `pos`, `x`/`y`/`z` or `line` for a handler. The key is `position` (optional integer) or nothing.
3. Using `switches` for arguments, or `args` for markers. Markers are integers in `switches`; arguments are values in `args`.
4. Several values without an array: use `"items": [ {...}, {...} ]`.
5. More values than the argument's `max`.
6. `children` on an action, or a condition / `ELSE` without `children`.
7. `ELSE` or an `elseIf` condition that does not directly follow its condition. Using the `ELSE_IF` action instead of `"elseIf": true`.
8. `not` or `elseIf` on a non-condition; `condition` on a non-selection node.
9. A non-variable value in a `VARIABLE` argument (where the result is written).
10. Wrong value types. Any value fits a TEXT or ANY argument, and a text fits NUMBER, but a text in a LOCATION argument or a number in an ITEM argument does not. Check section 4.2.
11. Unknown targets (`"target":"PLAYER"`, `"target":"EVERYONE"`). Use only the lists in section 5.3. IF_ENTITY conditions use the entity list.
12. Forgetting that actions run once per target. In loops and with `target: ALL`, variable math repeats for every player.
13. Expecting a loop to run without `GAME_START_LOOP`.
14. Putting `GAME_WAIT` inside a bracket and expecting the nodes after the bracket to wait too.
15. Using `&` color codes in JSON. Use `§`.
16. Mixing `save:true` and `save:false` for the same variable name.
17. Forgetting `%player_uuid%` (or `%player%`) in names of per-player variables. All variables are shared by the whole world.
18. Guessing coordinates. Locations are coordinates of the player's world; ask for them.
19. Wrong item format: `item` must be a real Minecraft item id with `minecraft:`, `count` a number, `components` an object of 1.21 item components.
20. Lines wider than 92 or more than 23 lines per floor. Split long logic into functions.
21. Writing `{"action":"GAP"}`: the rest of the list stops running. Do not.
22. A chat command that starts with `!`. `PLAYER_CHAT` never sees such messages (section 9).
23. Answering with only a part of the program. `/codeshare import` replaces all code, so always return the complete JSON.
24. Writing a game without the rules of section 14: no `phase` guard, mobs spawned inside blocks, kills counted from every `ENTITY_DEATH`, old entities never removed, items lost on death.
25. Inventing blocks that do not exist. There is NO repeat / for / while / for-each block, NO "stop the line" or "return" action, NO function parameters and NO local variables. Do it like this instead:
    - Do something for every player or mob: select them; the next action runs once for each target (section 5.1).
    - Do something N times or for every list element: a loop handler with a counter variable (`LIST_GET_BY_INDEX` with the counter for lists), and `GAME_STOP_LOOP` when the counter reaches N or `LIST_SIZE`.
