### PLOT_INVENTORY_MOVE_ITEM
event | category: PLOT_EVENT | default target: the world default | cancellable: no
Fires when a hopper, dropper or hopper minecart moves an item from one container to another.
event values: EVENT_BLOCK (ITEM): the block at the SOURCE container; EVENT_BLOCK_LOCATION (LOCATION): center of the source container; EVENT_ITEM (ITEM): the moved item
notes:
- Hoppers move items all the time. Keep the line very short.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"PLOT_INVENTORY_MOVE_ITEM","nodes":[{"action":"VARIABLE_INCREMENT","args":{"variable":{"var":"items_moved"}}}]}

### PLOT_FURNACE_SMELT
event | category: PLOT_EVENT | default target: the world default | cancellable: yes
Fires when a furnace, smoker or blast furnace finishes smelting one item.
event values: EVENT_BLOCK (ITEM): the furnace as an item; EVENT_BLOCK_LOCATION (LOCATION): center of the furnace; EVENT_ITEM (ITEM): the result; EVENT_FURNACE_SOURCE_ITEM (ITEM): the smelted item; EVENT_FURNACE_RESULT_ITEM (ITEM): the result
notes:
- `GAME_SET_ITEMSTACK_OF_EVENT` can change the result. It runs once per target, so it needs a non-empty selection.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"PLOT_FURNACE_SMELT","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_SET_ITEMSTACK_OF_EVENT","args":{"item":{"item":"minecraft:gold_ingot"}}}]}

### PLOT_FURNACE_BURN
event | category: PLOT_EVENT | default target: the world default | cancellable: no
Fires when a furnace burns a new fuel item.
event values: EVENT_BLOCK (ITEM): the furnace as an item; EVENT_BLOCK_LOCATION (LOCATION): center of the furnace; EVENT_ITEM (ITEM): the fuel item; EVENT_FURNACE_BURN_TIME (NUMBER): burn time in ticks
example: {"kind":"event","event":"PLOT_FURNACE_BURN","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","target":"ALL","args":{"texts":"§7Fuel burns for %value(EVENT_FURNACE_BURN_TIME)% ticks"}}]}

### ENTITY_TAKE_DAMAGE
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the damaged entity (a non-player living entity) | cancellable: yes
Fires when a non-player living entity (mob, animal, armor stand) takes damage from any source.
event values: EVENT_DAMAGE (NUMBER): damage (from an entity hit: the same as the final damage; from other causes: before armor); EVENT_FINAL_DAMAGE (NUMBER): final damage; EVENT_DAMAGE_REASON (TEXT): damage cause
notes:
- Damager = the attacking entity when the damage came from an entity. Shooter and projectile are set for projectiles. Victim (both kinds) = the damaged entity.
- `GAME_SET_DAMAGE_OF_EVENT` works only for damage that did not come from an entity (fall, fire, and so on).
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_TAKE_DAMAGE","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","target":"ALL","args":{"texts":"§7%entity% took %value(EVENT_FINAL_DAMAGE)% damage"}}]}

### ENTITY_DAMAGE_PLAYER
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the damaged player | cancellable: yes
Fires when a player is damaged by an entity that is not a player: a mob, a projectile (also one shot by a player), a harmful potion or effect cloud, fire from an entity.
event values: EVENT_DAMAGE (NUMBER): damage (the same as the final damage); EVENT_FINAL_DAMAGE (NUMBER): final damage; EVENT_DAMAGE_REASON (TEXT): damage cause
notes:
- Event entity (`DEFAULT_ENTITY`, `%entity%`) and damager = the damaging entity (for arrows: the arrow). Shooter and projectile are set for projectiles. Victim (both kinds) = the player.
- `GAME_SET_DAMAGE_OF_EVENT` does not change the damage in this event; use ENTITY_DAMAGE_ANOTHER_ENTITY for that.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_DAMAGE_PLAYER","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_ACTION_BAR","args":{"message":"§cHit by %entity%!"}}]}

### ENTITY_DAMAGE_ANOTHER_ENTITY
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the damaged entity (can be a player) | cancellable: yes
Fires on every direct entity-to-entity hit in the world, also for players: mob hits mob, mob hits player, player hits mob or player, projectile hits anything.
event values: EVENT_DAMAGE (NUMBER): damage before armor; EVENT_FINAL_DAMAGE (NUMBER): damage after armor; EVENT_DAMAGE_REASON (TEXT): damage cause
notes:
- Damager = the attacker (for arrows: the arrow). Shooter and projectile are set for projectiles. Victim (both kinds) = the damaged entity.
- `GAME_SET_DAMAGE_OF_EVENT` works here.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_DAMAGE_ANOTHER_ENTITY","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_SET_DAMAGE_OF_EVENT","args":{"damage":2}}]}

### ENTITY_DEATH
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the dead entity (never a player) | cancellable: no
Fires when a non-player living entity dies.
event values: EVENT_DAMAGE (NUMBER): final damage of the last hit; EVENT_DAMAGE_REASON (TEXT): last damage cause; EVENT_EXP_AMOUNT (NUMBER): experience that drops
notes:
- Damager = the last entity that hit it (if the last damage came from an entity). Shooter and projectile are set if that was a projectile. Victim (both kinds) = the dead entity.
- PLAYER_KILL_MOB, PLAYER_KILL_MOB_BY_PROJECTILE, ENTITY_DEATH_BY_PROJECTILE or ENTITY_DEATH_BY_ANOTHER_ENTITY fire before this event for the same death.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_DEATH","nodes":[{"action":"VARIABLE_INCREMENT","args":{"variable":{"var":"mobs_dead"}}}]}

### ENTITY_DEATH_BY_PROJECTILE
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the dead entity (can be a player) | cancellable: yes
Fires when an entity dies and its last damage was a projectile (shot by anyone).
event values: EVENT_DAMAGE_REASON (TEXT): last damage cause
notes:
- Killer (`"target":"KILLER"`, `%killer%`) = the PROJECTILE, not the shooter. Victim (both kinds) = the dead entity.
- It also fires when the dead entity is a player.
- Cancelling cancels the game's death event. Test it in your world before you rely on it.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_DEATH_BY_PROJECTILE","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","target":"ALL","args":{"texts":"§7%entity% was shot dead"}}]}

### ENTITY_DEATH_BY_ANOTHER_ENTITY
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the dead entity (can be a player) | cancellable: yes
Fires when an entity dies and its last damage was a direct hit by a non-player entity (a mob).
event values: EVENT_DAMAGE_REASON (TEXT): last damage cause
notes:
- Killer = the mob that hit last. Victim (both kinds) = the dead entity.
- It also fires when the dead entity is a player (killed by a mob).
- Cancelling cancels the game's death event. Test it in your world before you rely on it.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_DEATH_BY_ANOTHER_ENTITY","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","target":"ALL","args":{"texts":"§7%killer% killed %entity%"}}]}

### ENTITY_KILL_PLAYER
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the killed player | cancellable: no
Fires when a player died and the last entity that damaged them in the last 10 seconds was not a player (a mob, or a projectile not shot by a player). It runs 5 ticks after the death, right after PLAYER_DEATH.
event values: EVENT_DAMAGE_REASON (TEXT): the last damage cause of the player
notes:
- Event entity (`DEFAULT_ENTITY`, `%entity%`) and killer = the killing entity (for a skeleton arrow: the arrow). Victim (both kinds) = the player.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_KILL_PLAYER","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","target":"ALL","args":{"texts":"§c%player% was killed by %entity%"}}]}

### ENTITY_KNOCKBACK_BY_ENTITY
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the entity that is pushed (can be a player) | cancellable: no
Fires when an entity is knocked back by an attack of another entity.
event values: EVENT_ACCELERATION (VECTOR): the knockback velocity; EVENT_DAMAGE (NUMBER): damage of the entity's last damage; EVENT_FINAL_DAMAGE (NUMBER): final damage of the last damage; EVENT_DAMAGE_REASON (TEXT): last damage cause
notes:
- Damager = the attacker. Shooter and projectile are set for projectiles. Victim (both kinds) = the pushed entity.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_KNOCKBACK_BY_ENTITY","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","target":"ALL","args":{"texts":"§7%damager% pushed %entity%"}}]}

### ENTITY_SPAWN
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the spawned entity | cancellable: yes
Fires when any entity appears in the world: mobs (natural, spawn egg, spawner, code), dropped items, projectiles, experience orbs, vehicles and so on.
event values: EVENT_ENTITY_SPAWN_REASON (TEXT): spawn reason for creatures, for example `NATURAL`, `SPAWNER`, `SPAWNER_EGG`, `EGG`, `BREEDING`, `CUSTOM` (spawned by code), `DEFAULT`; for items, projectiles and other non-creatures it is `DEFAULT`
notes:
- Cancelling stops the spawn.
- Fires often (every dropped item and arrow).
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_SPAWN","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","target":"ALL","args":{"texts":"§7Spawned: %entity% (%value(EVENT_ENTITY_SPAWN_REASON)%)"}}]}

### ENTITY_REMOVE_FROM_WORLD
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the removed entity | cancellable: no
Fires when an entity is removed from the world for any reason: it died, was removed by code, despawned, was picked up, or its chunk was unloaded.
notes:
- No event values. The entity is leaving; do not try to change it.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_REMOVE_FROM_WORLD","nodes":[{"action":"VARIABLE_INCREMENT","args":{"variable":{"var":"removed_entities"}}}]}

### ENTITY_PATH_FIND
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the mob | cancellable: yes
Fires when a mob finds a path to a location.
event values: EVENT_BLOCK (ITEM): the block at the path target; EVENT_BLOCK_LOCATION (LOCATION): center of the path target block
notes:
- Mobs search paths often. Keep the line short.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_PATH_FIND","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_TARGET
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the mob that chooses a target | cancellable: yes
Fires when a mob chooses a new target to follow or attack, or forgets its target.
event values: EVENT_ENTITY_TARGET_REASON (TEXT): for example `CLOSEST_PLAYER`, `TARGET_ATTACKED_ENTITY`, `TARGET_DIED`, `FORGOT_TARGET`, `TEMPT`, `CLOSEST_ENTITY`, `CUSTOM`, `UNKNOWN`
notes:
- Plain victim (`sel:"VICTIM"`, `%victim%`) = the new target (nothing when the target is lost). Node `"target":"VICTIM"` is empty here.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_TARGET","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_TELEPORT
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the teleporting entity (not players) | cancellable: yes
Fires when a non-player entity teleports: an enderman or shulker teleports, or the server teleports it.
event values: EVENT_OLD_LOCATION (LOCATION): position before; EVENT_NEW_LOCATION (LOCATION): position after
notes:
- Teleports made by code actions do not fire it.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_TELEPORT","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_RESURRECT
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the entity that would die (can be a player) | cancellable: yes
Fires when an entity takes deadly damage and the game checks for a Totem of Undying. If the entity has no totem, the event starts already cancelled.
event values: EVENT_DAMAGE_REASON (TEXT): the last damage cause
notes:
- Killer = the entity whose hit was the last damage (if any). Victim (both kinds) = the entity.
- Cancelling it makes the totem fail (the entity dies).
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_RESURRECT","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","target":"ALL","args":{"texts":"§6%entity% used a totem!"}}]}

### ENTITY_REGAIN_HEALTH
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the healed entity (can be a player) | cancellable: yes
Fires when an entity regains health: natural regeneration, food saturation, potions, effects.
event values: EVENT_REGAIN_HEALTH_AMOUNT (NUMBER): health points regained (1 = half a heart); EVENT_REGAIN_HEALTH_REASON (TEXT): `REGEN`, `SATIATED`, `EATING`, `ENDER_CRYSTAL`, `MAGIC`, `MAGIC_REGEN`, `WITHER_SPAWN`, `WITHER`, `CUSTOM`
notes:
- Cancel it to turn off natural regeneration (check the reason `SATIATED`).
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_REGAIN_HEALTH","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_EXPLODE
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the exploding entity | cancellable: yes
Fires when an entity explodes: TNT, creeper, fireball, end crystal, wither skull, TNT minecart.
notes:
- No event values. Cancelling stops the block damage of the explosion.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_EXPLODE","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_ZAP
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the NEW entity that will replace the struck one | cancellable: yes
Fires when lightning strikes an entity and it turns into another entity (for example a villager into a witch).
notes:
- Plain victim (`sel:"VICTIM"`, `%victim%`) = the struck entity.
- A charged creeper is ENTITY_CREEPER_POWER_EVENT.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_ZAP","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_CREEPER_POWER_EVENT
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the creeper | cancellable: yes
Fires when a creeper becomes charged (lightning) or loses the charge.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_CREEPER_POWER_EVENT","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_VEHICLE_CREATE
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the new vehicle | cancellable: yes
Fires when a vehicle (boat, minecart) is placed or created in the world.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_VEHICLE_CREATE","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_ENTER_VEHICLE
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the entity that gets in (can be a player) | cancellable: yes
Fires when an entity gets into a vehicle (boat, minecart, horse, pig and so on).
notes:
- Plain victim (`sel:"VICTIM"`, `%victim%`) = the vehicle.
- When a player gets in, the selection is that player, so player actions work.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_ENTER_VEHICLE","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_ACTION_BAR","args":{"message":"§aYou are riding %victim%"}}]}

### ENTITY_EXIT_VEHICLE
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the entity that gets out (can be a player) | cancellable: yes
Fires when an entity leaves a vehicle.
notes:
- Plain victim (`sel:"VICTIM"`, `%victim%`) = the vehicle.
- Cancel it to keep a player in the vehicle.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_EXIT_VEHICLE","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_VEHICLE_BLOCK_COLLISION
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the vehicle | cancellable: no
Fires when a vehicle bumps into a block.
event values: EVENT_BLOCK (ITEM): the block as an item; EVENT_BLOCK_LOCATION (LOCATION): center of the block
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_VEHICLE_BLOCK_COLLISION","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","target":"ALL","args":{"texts":"§7Crash!"}}]}

Fires when a vehicle bumps into an entity.
notes:
- Plain victim (`sel:"VICTIM"`, `%victim%`) = the entity it bumped into.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_VEHICLE_ENTITY_COLLISION","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_VEHICLE_TAKE_DAMAGE
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the vehicle | cancellable: yes
Fires when a vehicle is damaged (hit by a player or mob, a projectile, an explosion).
event values: EVENT_DAMAGE (NUMBER): the damage
notes:
- Damager = the attacker, if any. Shooter and projectile are set for projectiles. Victim (both kinds) = the vehicle.
- `GAME_SET_DAMAGE_OF_EVENT` works here.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_VEHICLE_TAKE_DAMAGE","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_PROJECTILE_LAUNCH
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the projectile | cancellable: yes
Fires when any projectile is launched (by a player, a mob or a dispenser).
notes:
- Shooter (`sel:"SHOOTER"`, `%shooter%`) = who launched it (nothing for a dispenser). Projectile = the projectile.
- The same launch also fires ENTITY_SPAWN, and PLAYER_LAUNCH_PROJECTILE when a player shot it.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_PROJECTILE_LAUNCH","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","target":"ALL","args":{"texts":"§7%shooter% launched %entity%"}}]}

### ENTITY_PROJECTILE_HIT_BLOCK
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the world default (NOT the projectile) | cancellable: no
Fires when a projectile hits a block (and no entity).
event values: EVENT_BLOCK (ITEM): the hit block as an item; EVENT_BLOCK_LOCATION (LOCATION): center of the hit block; EVENT_BLOCK_FACE (TEXT): the side of the block that was hit
notes:
- The projectile and the shooter are not available in this event.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_PROJECTILE_HIT_BLOCK","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","target":"ALL","args":{"texts":"§7A projectile hit the %value(EVENT_BLOCK_FACE)% side of a block"}}]}

### ENTITY_PROJECTILE_HIT_ENTITY
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the projectile | cancellable: no
Fires when a projectile hits an entity, before the damage is dealt.
event values: EVENT_DAMAGE (NUMBER): damage of the HIT entity's last damage (usually an earlier hit); EVENT_DAMAGE_REASON (TEXT): last damage cause of the hit entity
notes:
- Damager and projectile = the projectile. Shooter = who shot it. Victim (both kinds) = the hit entity.
- The damage itself comes later: use ENTITY_DAMAGE_ANOTHER_ENTITY for the real damage.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_PROJECTILE_HIT_ENTITY","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","target":"ALL","args":{"texts":"§7%shooter% hit %victim%"}}]}

### ENTITY_SHOOT_BOW
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the shooter (a mob or a player) | cancellable: yes
Fires when an entity shoots a bow or crossbow.
event values: EVENT_ITEM (ITEM): the bow or crossbow
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_SHOOT_BOW","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_INTERACT
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: none | cancellable: yes
This event never fires. It exists only so old code still loads. Do not use it.
event values: EVENT_BLOCK (ITEM): never set; EVENT_BLOCK_LOCATION (LOCATION): never set
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_INTERACT","nodes":[]}

### ENTITY_CHANGE_BLOCK
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the entity that changes the block | cancellable: yes
Fires when an entity changes a block: an enderman picks up or places a block, a falling block starts to fall or lands, a silverfish enters or leaves a block, a wither or the dragon breaks blocks, a sheep eats grass, a zombie breaks a door, a ravager breaks crops.
event values: EVENT_BLOCK (ITEM): the block BEFORE the change; EVENT_BLOCK_LOCATION (LOCATION): center of the block
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_CHANGE_BLOCK","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_BLOCK_FORM
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the entity that forms the block | cancellable: yes
Fires when an entity creates a block: a snow golem leaves snow, Frost Walker boots make ice.
event values: EVENT_BLOCK (ITEM): the block before it changes; EVENT_BLOCK_LOCATION (LOCATION): center of the block
notes:
- The same change also fires PLOT_BLOCK_FORM.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_BLOCK_FORM","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_BLOCK_STARTED_FALLING
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the entity | cancellable: yes
Fires when an entity changes a block into air. Mostly this is a block that starts to fall (sand, gravel, anvil), but every entity block change to air counts: an enderman picks up a block, a zombie breaks a door, a wither breaks blocks.
event values: EVENT_BLOCK (ITEM): the block before the change; EVENT_BLOCK_LOCATION (LOCATION): center of the block
notes:
- Cancel it to make sand and gravel float.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_BLOCK_STARTED_FALLING","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_BLOCK_FINISHED_FALLING
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the entity | cancellable: yes
Fires when an entity changes a block into a non-air block. Mostly this is a falling block that lands, but other entity block changes count too (an enderman places a block, a silverfish enters stone).
event values: EVENT_BLOCK (ITEM): the block before the change (for a landing block usually air); EVENT_BLOCK_LOCATION (LOCATION): center of the landing place
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_BLOCK_FINISHED_FALLING","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","target":"ALL","args":{"texts":"§7A block landed."}}]}

### ENTITY_BREAK_DOOR
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the entity (a zombie or similar) | cancellable: yes
Fires when a mob breaks a door (zombies on hard difficulty, vindicators).
event values: EVENT_BLOCK (ITEM): the door as an item; EVENT_BLOCK_LOCATION (LOCATION): center of the door block
notes:
- The same break also fires ENTITY_CHANGE_BLOCK and ENTITY_BLOCK_STARTED_FALLING.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_BREAK_DOOR","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_HANGING_BREAK
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the hanging entity (painting, item frame, lead knot) | cancellable: yes
Fires when a painting, item frame or similar hanging entity breaks.
event values: EVENT_HANGING_BREAK_REASON (TEXT): `ENTITY`, `EXPLOSION`, `OBSTRUCTION`, `PHYSICS`, `DEFAULT`
notes:
- Cancel it to protect paintings and item frames.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_HANGING_BREAK","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_PICKUP_ITEM
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the mob that picks up (never a player) | cancellable: yes
Fires when a non-player entity picks up an item from the ground (zombies, foxes, allays and so on). Players use PLAYER_PICKUP_ITEM.
event values: EVENT_ITEM (ITEM): the picked up item stack
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_PICKUP_ITEM","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_ITEM_MERGE
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: one player of the world (empty or the last spawned entity if no player is there) | cancellable: yes
Fires when two dropped item stacks on the ground merge into one.
event values: EVENT_ITEM (ITEM): the item stack that merges into the other
notes:
- Event entity (`DEFAULT_ENTITY`, `%entity%`) = the merging item (when a player is in the world). Plain victim (`sel:"VICTIM"`, `%victim%`) = the item it merges into.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_ITEM_MERGE","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_ITEM_DESPAWN
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the world default (not the item) | cancellable: yes
Fires when a dropped item despawns after lying on the ground for 5 minutes.
event values: EVENT_ITEM (ITEM): the item stack
notes:
- Cancel it to keep the item on the ground.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_ITEM_DESPAWN","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_EXPERIENCE_ORB_MERGE

Fires when two experience orbs merge into one.
notes:
- The other orb is not available.

### ENTITY_VILLAGER_ACQUIRE_TRADE

Fires when a villager gets a new trade offer (for example it levels up). It fires once for each new offer.

### ENTITY_VILLAGER_REPLENISH_TRADE
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the villager | cancellable: yes (but cancelling has no effect)
Fires when a player makes a trade that uses up the last use of a villager offer (the offer becomes sold out).
notes:
- `GAME_CANCEL_EVENT` does nothing here; the trade always happens.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_VILLAGER_REPLENISH_TRADE","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","target":"ALL","args":{"texts":"§cA trader is sold out!"}}]}

### ENTITY_SHEEP_REGROW_WOOL
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the sheep | cancellable: yes
Fires when a sheep regrows its wool after eating grass.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_SHEEP_REGROW_WOOL","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_SHEEP_DYE_WOOL
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the sheep | cancellable: yes
Fires when a sheep is dyed.
notes:
- The player who dyed it is not available.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_SHEEP_DYE_WOOL","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

### ENTITY_FIREWORK_EXPLODE
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the firework rocket | cancellable: yes
Fires when a firework rocket explodes.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_FIREWORK_EXPLODE","nodes":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","target":"ALL","args":{"texts":"§eBoom!"}}]}

### ENTITY_EXPLOSION_PRIME
event | category: ENTITY_EVENT | default target: the world default (NOT the exploding entity) | cancellable: yes
Fires when an entity is about to explode: a creeper finished its fuse, TNT or a fireball is about to explode.
notes:
- The exploding entity is not available here. Use ENTITY_EXPLODE if you need it.
- Cancelling stops the explosion.
example: {"kind":"event","event":"ENTITY_EXPLOSION_PRIME","nodes":[{"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}]}

## Game values

A game value is written as `{"val":"<ID>"}` or `{"val":"<ID>","sel":"<SEL>"}`. It is read at the moment the node runs. It fits every argument type except `VARIABLE`.

How `sel` works:

- `sel` is used only by values marked `sel: allowed` (categories ENTITY_STATISTIC, LOCATION_VALUE, ITEM_VALUE). For values marked `sel: not used` (EVENT_VALUE, WORLD) it is ignored.
- No `sel`: the value is read for the target the node is running for right now. An action runs once per target of its selection, so each target gets its own value. In a condition it is the target being tested. This is the normal choice.
- Valid `sel` strings. Write them exactly like this, in UPPER CASE:
  - `CURRENT`: the target the node is running for (the same as no `sel`);
  - `DEFAULT`: the default target of the event (the starting selection, see the event catalog);
  - `DEFAULT_ENTITY`: the event entity (`%entity%`);
  - `KILLER`, `DAMAGER`, `SHOOTER`, `PROJECTILE`: these entities of the event;
  - `VICTIM`: the plain victim of the event (`%victim%`, see the event catalog);
  - `LAST_LIVING_ENTITY`: the last living entity spawned in the world (by code or by the game).
- Any other text (also lower case `current`) means `DEFAULT`, not `CURRENT`. The server does not warn about a bad `sel`.
- If the chosen entity does not exist in this event (for example `KILLER` in PLAYER_JOIN), the value gives its "no target" result written in the entry.
- In placeholders `%value(ID)%` and `%value(ID, SEL)%` the rules differ: without SEL the value is read for the DEFAULT target of the event, and `CURRENT` there also means the default target. To get the value of each target, put the game value itself into the argument instead of a placeholder.
- If reading a value fails (the entry says "reading it is an error"), the action fails and the players see "error executing ..."; inside `%value(...)%` the result is just empty text.

Other rules:

- Event values (`EVENT_*`) belong to the event of the line. A function sees the event of its caller. A loop sees the event of the line that ran `GAME_START_LOOP`.
- "Nothing" means no value: the argument acts as if it was empty (a text part is skipped, a location argument has no location).
- A decimal game value put directly into a TEXT argument prints like `20.0`. `%var(name)%` prints whole numbers without `.0`, so to print nicely, save the value with `VARIABLE_SET` and print `%var(name)%`.
- LOCATION and ITEM values printed as text give a long technical string. Use them in LOCATION or ITEM arguments, not in messages.

### CURRENT_HEALTH
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: NUMBER | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Health of the target in health points (20 = 10 hearts, 1 = half a heart). 0 if the target is not a living entity or there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_HEALTH"}

### CURRENT_MAX_HEALTH
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: NUMBER | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Maximum health of the target (its max health attribute). 0 if the target is not a living entity or there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_MAX_HEALTH"}

### CURRENT_LAST_DAMAGE_CAUSE
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: TEXT | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Cause of the last damage the target took, for example `FALL`, `ENTITY_ATTACK`, `PROJECTILE`, `LAVA`, `VOID`. Empty text if there is no target. If the target was never damaged, reading it is an error and the action fails.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_LAST_DAMAGE_CAUSE"}

### CURRENT_FOOD_LEVEL
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: NUMBER | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Food level of a player, 0..20. 0 for non-players or no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_FOOD_LEVEL"}

### CURRENT_SATURATION_LEVEL
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: NUMBER | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Saturation of a player (decimal, 0..20). 0 for non-players or no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_SATURATION_LEVEL"}

### CURRENT_EXHAUSTION_VALUE
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: NUMBER | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Exhaustion of a player (decimal; at 4 the player loses saturation or food). -1 for non-players or no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_EXHAUSTION_VALUE"}

### CURRENT_XP_LEVEL
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: NUMBER | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Experience level of a player. 0 for non-players or no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_XP_LEVEL"}

### CURRENT_XP
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: NUMBER | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Progress of a player to the next level as a fraction from 0 to 1 (0.5 = half the bar). It is NOT a percent. 0 for non-players or no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_XP"}

### CURRENT_ARMOR_POINTS
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: NUMBER | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Armor points of the target (its armor attribute, 0..30, 2 = one armor icon). 0 if the target is not a living entity or there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_ARMOR_POINTS"}

### CURRENT_FIRE_TICKS
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: NUMBER | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Ticks until the target stops burning. 0 or less means it does not burn. 0 if there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_FIRE_TICKS"}

### CURRENT_AIR_REMAINING
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: NUMBER | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Air left under water in ticks (300 = full for players). 0 if the target is not a living entity or there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_AIR_REMAINING"}

### CURRENT_AIR_MAXIMUM_VALUE
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: NUMBER | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Maximum air in ticks (300 for players by default). -1 if the target is not a living entity or there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_AIR_MAXIMUM_VALUE"}

### CURRENT_HELD_SLOT
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: NUMBER | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Selected hotbar slot of a player, 0..8 (0 = left). 0 for non-players or no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_HELD_SLOT"}

### CURRENT_PING
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: NUMBER | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Ping of a player in milliseconds. Nothing for non-players or no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_PING"}

### CURRENT_VELOCITY
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: VECTOR | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Current velocity of the target in blocks per tick (movement from outside forces such as knockback or falling). Nothing if there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_VELOCITY"}

### CURRENT_NAME
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: TEXT | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Name of the target: the player name for players; for other entities the custom name if it has one, else the default name of the entity type. Empty text if there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_NAME","sel":"VICTIM"}

### CURRENT_UUID
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: TEXT | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
UUID of the target as text. Empty text if there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_UUID"}

### CURRENT_ENTITY_TYPE
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: TEXT | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Entity type of the target in upper case, for example `PLAYER`, `ZOMBIE`, `COW`, `ARMOR_STAND`. Some types use old names: `ITEM` is `DROPPED_ITEM`, `ZOMBIFIED_PIGLIN` is `PIG_ZOMBIE`, `MOOSHROOM` is `MUSHROOM_COW`, `SNOW_GOLEM` is `SNOWMAN`, `TNT` is `PRIMED_TNT`, `FIREWORK_ROCKET` is `FIREWORK`, `END_CRYSTAL` is `ENDER_CRYSTAL`, `ARROW` is `TIPPED_ARROW`, `LIGHTNING_BOLT` is `LIGHTNING`, `FISHING_BOBBER` is `FISHING_HOOK`, `LEASH_KNOT` is `LEASH_HITCH`, `EYE_OF_ENDER` is `ENDER_SIGNAL`, `EXPERIENCE_BOTTLE` is `THROWN_EXP_BOTTLE`; oak, spruce, birch, jungle, acacia and dark oak boats are `BOAT`; `CHEST_MINECART` is `MINECART_CHEST` (also `MINECART_FURNACE`, `MINECART_TNT`, `MINECART_HOPPER`, `MINECART_COMMAND`, `MINECART_MOB_SPAWNER`). Empty text if there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_ENTITY_TYPE","sel":"DEFAULT_ENTITY"}

### CURRENT_INVENTORY_TITLE
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: TEXT | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Title of the inventory window the player has open (for menus made by code: the title the code gave). Nothing for non-players or no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_INVENTORY_TITLE"}

### CURRENT_INVENTORY_TYPE
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: TEXT | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Type of the window the player has open, for example `CHEST`, `FURNACE`, `WORKBENCH`, `HOPPER`, `ANVIL`, `MERCHANT`. `CRAFTING` when no window is open. Empty text for non-players or no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_INVENTORY_TYPE"}

### CURRENT_INVENTORY_SLOTS
game value | category: ENTITY_STATISTIC | returns: NUMBER | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Number of slots of the window the player has open (27 for a small chest; 5 when no window is open). 0 for non-players or no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_INVENTORY_SLOTS"}

### CURRENT_LOCALE_OF_PLAYER

Language set in the player's game client, for example `en_us`, `ru_ru`. Empty text for non-players or no target.

### EVENT_ENDERMAN_ESCAPE_REASON
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: TEXT | sel: not used
ENTITY_ENDER_MAN_ESCAPE: `RUNAWAY`, `INDIRECT`, `CRITICAL_HIT`, `STARE` or `DROWN`. Empty text in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_ENDERMAN_ESCAPE_REASON"}

### EVENT_ENTITY_TARGET_REASON
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: TEXT | sel: not used
ENTITY_TARGET: reason, for example `CLOSEST_PLAYER`, `TARGET_ATTACKED_ENTITY`, `TARGET_DIED`, `FORGOT_TARGET`, `TEMPT`, `CUSTOM`. Empty text in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_ENTITY_TARGET_REASON"}

### EVENT_IGNITE_REASON
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: TEXT | sel: not used
PLOT_BLOCK_IGNITE: `FLINT_AND_STEEL`, `LAVA`, `SPREAD`, `LIGHTNING`, `FIREBALL`, `ENDER_CRYSTAL`, `EXPLOSION` or `ARROW`. Empty text in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_IGNITE_REASON"}

### EVENT_CAULDRON_WATER_LEVEL_CHANGE_REASON
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: TEXT | sel: not used
PLOT_CAULDRON_LEVEL_CHANGE: reason, for example `BUCKET_FILL`, `BUCKET_EMPTY`, `BOTTLE_FILL`, `BOTTLE_EMPTY`, `EVAPORATE`, `NATURAL_FILL`, `UNKNOWN`. Empty text in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_CAULDRON_WATER_LEVEL_CHANGE_REASON"}

### EVENT_CAULDRON_WATER_LEVEL_CHANGE_OLD_LEVEL
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
PLOT_CAULDRON_LEVEL_CHANGE: level before (0 = empty .. 3 = full). 0 in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_CAULDRON_WATER_LEVEL_CHANGE_OLD_LEVEL"}

### EVENT_CAULDRON_WATER_LEVEL_CHANGE_NEW_LEVEL
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
PLOT_CAULDRON_LEVEL_CHANGE: level after (0 = empty .. 3 = full). 0 in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_CAULDRON_WATER_LEVEL_CHANGE_NEW_LEVEL"}

### EVENT_ARMOR_STAND_EQUIPMENT_SLOT
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: TEXT | sel: not used
PLAYER_MANIPULATE_ARMOR_STAND: slot of the armor stand: `HAND`, `OFF_HAND`, `FEET`, `LEGS`, `CHEST` or `HEAD`. Empty text in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_ARMOR_STAND_EQUIPMENT_SLOT"}

### EVENT_ARMOR_STAND_ITEM
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: ITEM | sel: not used
PLAYER_MANIPULATE_ARMOR_STAND: the item on the armor stand. Nothing in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_ARMOR_STAND_ITEM"}

### EVENT_UNLEASH_REASON
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: TEXT | sel: not used
ENTITY_UNLEASH: `DISTANCE`, `HOLDER_GONE`, `PLAYER_UNLEASH` or `UNKNOWN`. Empty text in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_UNLEASH_REASON"}

### EVENT_FURNACE_SOURCE_ITEM
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: ITEM | sel: not used
PLOT_FURNACE_SMELT: the item being smelted. Nothing in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_FURNACE_SOURCE_ITEM"}

### EVENT_FURNACE_RESULT_ITEM
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: ITEM | sel: not used
PLOT_FURNACE_SMELT: the smelting result. Nothing in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_FURNACE_RESULT_ITEM"}

### EVENT_FURNACE_BURN_TIME
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
PLOT_FURNACE_BURN: burn time of the fuel in ticks. 0 in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_FURNACE_BURN_TIME"}

### EVENT_BREWING_STAND_FUEL_POWER
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
PLOT_BREWING_STAND_FUEL: how many brews the fuel gives. 0 in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_BREWING_STAND_FUEL_POWER"}

### EVENT_RESOURCE_PACK_STATUS
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: TEXT | sel: not used
PLAYER_RESOURCE_PACK: the new status, for example `ACCEPTED`, `DOWNLOADED`, `SUCCESSFULLY_LOADED`, `DECLINED`, `FAILED_DOWNLOAD`. Empty text in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_RESOURCE_PACK_STATUS"}

### EVENT_CREATIVE_COIN_TRANSACTION_AMOUNT
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
PLAYER_GOLD_TRANSACTION: number of creative coins. 0 in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_CREATIVE_COIN_TRANSACTION_AMOUNT"}

### EVENT_CREATIVE_COIN_TRANSACTION_NAME
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: TEXT | sel: not used
PLAYER_GOLD_TRANSACTION: the transaction name (`none` if the action had no name). Empty text in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_CREATIVE_COIN_TRANSACTION_NAME"}

### EVENT_CREATIVE_COIN_TRANSACTION_DISPLAY_NAME
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: TEXT | sel: not used
PLAYER_GOLD_TRANSACTION: the display name of the transaction (the name if no display name was given). Empty text in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_CREATIVE_COIN_TRANSACTION_DISPLAY_NAME"}

### EVENT_POINTS_PURCHASE_AMOUNT
game value | category: EVENT_VALUE | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
PLAYER_BUY_POINTS: number of bought points. -1 in other events.
example: {"val":"EVENT_POINTS_PURCHASE_AMOUNT"}

### CURRENT_LOCATION
game value | category: LOCATION_VALUE | returns: LOCATION | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Position of the target's feet with its yaw and pitch. Nothing if there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_LOCATION","sel":"VICTIM"}

### CURRENT_EYE_LOCATION
game value | category: LOCATION_VALUE | returns: LOCATION | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Position of the target's eyes with the view direction (yaw and pitch). Nothing if the target is not a living entity or there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_EYE_LOCATION"}

### CURRENT_EYE_VIEW
game value | category: LOCATION_VALUE | returns: VECTOR | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
View direction of the target as a vector of length 1. Nothing if the target is not a living entity or there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_EYE_VIEW"}

### CURRENT_PLAYER_FACING_VALUE
game value | category: LOCATION_VALUE | returns: TEXT | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Compass side the target looks to, from its yaw: `South`, `West`, `North` or `East` (written exactly like this, first letter upper case). Empty text if there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_PLAYER_FACING_VALUE"}

### CURRENT_TARGET_BLOCK_LOCATION
game value | category: LOCATION_VALUE | returns: LOCATION | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Location (block corner, whole numbers) of the first non-air block the target looks at, up to 120 blocks away; if there is no such block, the last block on the line. In PLAYER_BLOCK_BREAK it is the broken block. Nothing if the target is not a living entity or there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_TARGET_BLOCK_LOCATION"}

### CURRENT_TARGET_BLOCK_FACE
game value | category: LOCATION_VALUE | returns: TEXT | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Side of the looked-at block that faces the target: `UP`, `DOWN`, `NORTH`, `SOUTH`, `EAST` or `WEST` (up to 120 blocks). Empty text if no block is looked at, the target is not a living entity, or there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_TARGET_BLOCK_FACE"}

### CURRENT_BODY_ROTATION
game value | category: LOCATION_VALUE | returns: NUMBER | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Yaw of the target in degrees from 0 to 360 (0 = south, 90 = west, 180 = north, 270 = east). 0 if there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_BODY_ROTATION"}

### CURRENT_CENTER_OF_HITBOX
game value | category: LOCATION_VALUE | returns: LOCATION | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Middle of the target's hitbox: the feet position plus half of the height. Nothing if there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_CENTER_OF_HITBOX"}

### CURRENT_BLOCK_ABOVE
game value | category: LOCATION_VALUE | returns: LOCATION | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Location (block corner) of the block one above the block at the target's feet (for a player: the block at head height). Nothing if there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_BLOCK_ABOVE"}

### CURRENT_BLOCK_UNDER
game value | category: LOCATION_VALUE | returns: LOCATION | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Location (block corner) of the block under the block at the target's feet: the block the target stands on. Nothing if there is no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_BLOCK_UNDER"}

### CURRENT_COMPASS_TARGET
game value | category: LOCATION_VALUE | returns: LOCATION | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Location the player's compass points to. Nothing for non-players or no target.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_COMPASS_TARGET"}

### MAIN_HAND_ITEM
game value | category: ITEM_VALUE | returns: ITEM | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Copy of the item in the target's main hand (air if empty). Nothing if the target is not a living entity or there is no target.
example: {"val":"MAIN_HAND_ITEM"}

### OFFHAND_ITEM
game value | category: ITEM_VALUE | returns: ITEM | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Copy of the item in the target's off hand (air if empty). Nothing if the target is not a living entity or there is no target.
example: {"val":"OFFHAND_ITEM"}

### GET_HELMET_ITEM
game value | category: ITEM_VALUE | returns: ITEM | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Copy of the target's helmet (air if none). Nothing if the target is not a living entity or there is no target.
example: {"val":"GET_HELMET_ITEM"}

### GET_CHESTPLATE_ITEM
game value | category: ITEM_VALUE | returns: ITEM | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Copy of the target's chestplate (air if none). Nothing if the target is not a living entity or there is no target.
example: {"val":"GET_CHESTPLATE_ITEM"}

### GET_LEGGINGS_ITEM
game value | category: ITEM_VALUE | returns: ITEM | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Copy of the target's leggings (air if none). Nothing if the target is not a living entity or there is no target.
example: {"val":"GET_LEGGINGS_ITEM"}

### GET_BOOTS_ITEM
game value | category: ITEM_VALUE | returns: ITEM | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Copy of the target's boots (air if none). Nothing if the target is not a living entity or there is no target.
example: {"val":"GET_BOOTS_ITEM"}

### ENTITY_AS_ITEM
game value | category: ITEM_VALUE | returns: ITEM | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
The target as an item: a dropped item gives its item stack; a thrown potion its potion; a firework its rocket; an arrow `minecraft:arrow`; a snowball, egg or experience bottle its item; a minecart its minecart item; an armor stand `minecraft:armor_stand`; a falling block its block; other entities their spawn egg; players and entities without a spawn egg give `minecraft:dirt`. If there is no target, reading it is an error and the action fails.
example: {"val":"ENTITY_AS_ITEM","sel":"DEFAULT_ENTITY"}

### ITEM_FRAME_ITEM
game value | category: ITEM_VALUE | returns: ITEM | sel: allowed (default: the current target)
Copy of the item in an item frame. Air for other entities or no target.
example: {"val":"ITEM_FRAME_ITEM","sel":"DEFAULT_ENTITY"}

### PLOT_ONLINE
game value | category: WORLD | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
Number of players in this world, including players who are coding or building.
example: {"val":"PLOT_ONLINE"}

### PLOT_LIKES
game value | category: WORLD | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
Number of likes (`/like`) of this world.
example: {"val":"PLOT_LIKES"}

### PLOT_UNIQUE_JOINS
game value | category: WORLD | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
Number of different players who have ever visited this world.
example: {"val":"PLOT_UNIQUE_JOINS"}

### PLOT_VARIABLES_COUNT
game value | category: WORLD | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
Number of variables that exist in this world now (normal and list variables).
example: {"val":"PLOT_VARIABLES_COUNT"}

### PLOT_POINTS
game value | category: WORLD | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
Rating points of this world.
example: {"val":"PLOT_POINTS"}

### PLOT_TIME
game value | category: WORLD | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
Time of day in the world in ticks, 0..23999 (0 = sunrise, 6000 = noon, 18000 = midnight).
example: {"val":"PLOT_TIME"}

### PLOT_WEATHER_DURATION
game value | category: WORLD | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
Ticks until the weather of the world changes.
example: {"val":"PLOT_WEATHER_DURATION"}

### CURRENT_UNIX_TIME
game value | category: WORLD | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
Current real time in milliseconds since 1 January 1970 (UTC). Use it to measure time: (end - start) / 1000 = seconds.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_UNIX_TIME"}

### CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
game value | category: WORLD | returns: TEXT | sel: not used
Current real date and time of the server as text: `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.f`, the fraction has 1 to 3 digits with trailing zeros removed, for example `2026-11-19 14:05:09.42`.
example: {"val":"CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"}

### PLOT_BLOCKS_LIMIT_REMAIN
game value | category: WORLD | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
How many more blocks code may change in this second (the limit is 5000 and it is refilled every second).
example: {"val":"PLOT_BLOCKS_LIMIT_REMAIN"}

### PLOT_ACTION_COUNTER
game value | category: WORLD | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
Number of actions the code has run in the current limit window (see the action limit in the guide). Useful to watch the load.
example: {"val":"PLOT_ACTION_COUNTER"}

### PLOT_ID
game value | category: WORLD | returns: NUMBER | sel: not used
Id of this world (plot). Another world of the same owner uses it as `target_plot_id` to send data here.
example: {"val":"PLOT_ID"}

## Player actions

### PLAYER_MESSAGE
kind: action | category: PLAYER_COMMUNICATION | targets: any selected entity (players see it)
Sends one chat message to every selected entity. All values of `texts` are joined into one message.
args:
- texts (TEXT, up to 27 values): message parts. Numbers, locations, items, lists and game values are turned into text. Default: empty → nothing is sent.
markers:
- separator: 0 = join values with no separator (default), 1 = join with a space, 2 = join with a new line
notes:
- The joined message is cut at 1024 characters.
- Counts toward the plot message limit; if the limit is hit, the program stops.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":["Hello,","%player%!"]},"switches":{"separator":1}}

### PLAYER_SEND_TEXT_COMPONENT
kind: action | category: PLAYER_COMMUNICATION | targets: players
Sends one chat message built from text components (JSON chat text with colors, hover and click events) to every selected player.
args:
- components (COMPONENT, up to 27 values): message parts. A component value is a JSON chat component (`{"comp":{"text":"Hi"}}`). Plain text, numbers and variables are added as plain text parts. Default: empty → nothing is sent.
markers:
- separator: 0 = join parts with no separator (default), 1 = join with a space, 2 = join with a new line
notes:
- Click action `run_command` is replaced with `suggest_command` (the plot cannot run commands as the player).
- If the component JSON cannot be parsed, it is shown as plain text.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_SEND_TEXT_COMPONENT","args":{"components":[{"comp":"{\"text\":\"Click me\",\"color\":\"gold\",\"clickEvent\":{\"action\":\"open_url\",\"value\":\"https://mineland.net\"}}"}]},"switches":{"separator":0}}

### PLAYER_DIALOGUE
kind: action | category: PLAYER_COMMUNICATION | targets: any selected entity (players see it)
Sends several chat messages one after another with a pause between them. The first message is sent at once.
args:
- messages (TEXT, up to 27 values): one chat message per value, in order. Default: empty → nothing is sent.
- delay (NUMBER): pause between messages in ticks (20 ticks = 1 second). Default: 20.
notes:
- Message number i (from 0) is sent after i × delay ticks.
- A message is skipped if the target left the plot world in the meantime.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_DIALOGUE","args":{"messages":["Hi!","Welcome to my game."],"delay":40}}

### PLAYER_CLEAR_CHAT
kind: action | category: PLAYER_COMMUNICATION | targets: any selected entity (players see it)
Clears the chat window of every selected player by sending 256 empty lines.
notes:
- Counts toward the plot message limit.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_CLEAR_CHAT"}

### PLAYER_SOUND
kind: action | category: PLAYER_COMMUNICATION | targets: players
Plays a sound that only the selected player hears.
args:
- sound (TEXT): sound id, for example `minecraft:entity.experience_orb.pickup` or `entity.experience_orb.pickup`. Required; empty → nothing.
- volume (NUMBER): volume. Higher volume is heard from farther away. Default: 1. A negative value becomes 1.
- pitch (NUMBER): pitch, normal range 0.5–2. Default: 1. A negative value becomes 1.
- location (LOCATION): where the sound comes from. Default: the player's own position.
markers:
- category: sound category (only changes which volume slider of the player applies): 0 = master (default), 1 = music, 2 = records (jukebox/note blocks), 3 = weather, 4 = blocks, 5 = hostile mobs, 6 = friendly mobs, 7 = players, 8 = ambient, 9 = voice
notes:
- Color codes are removed from the id and it is lower-cased. Old (pre-1.13) sound names are converted when possible.
- An id that is not a valid namespaced key is ignored. A valid but unknown id (for example a resource pack sound) is still sent.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_SOUND","args":{"sound":"minecraft:entity.experience_orb.pickup","volume":1,"pitch":1.5},"switches":{"category":0}}

### PLAYER_STOP_SOUND
kind: action | category: PLAYER_COMMUNICATION | targets: players
Stops sounds that the selected player hears right now.
args:
- sounds (TEXT, up to 27 values): sound ids to stop. Default: empty → stops all vanilla sounds.
notes:
- With no ids, only vanilla sounds are stopped; custom resource pack sounds keep playing.
- Stopping all sounds uses the plot element limit; when it is used up, nothing happens.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_STOP_SOUND","args":{"sounds":"minecraft:music_disc.cat"}}

### PLAYER_TITLE
kind: action | category: PLAYER_COMMUNICATION | targets: players
Shows a big title and a subtitle in the middle of the player's screen.
args:
- title (TEXT): big top line. Default: empty.
- subtitle (TEXT): smaller line below. Default: empty.
- duration (NUMBER): how long the title stays, in ticks. Default: 30.
- fadein (NUMBER): fade-in time in ticks. Default: 5.
- fadeout (NUMBER): fade-out time in ticks. Default: 5.
notes:
- Counts toward the plot message limit.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_TITLE","args":{"title":"§6Round 1","subtitle":"Fight!","duration":40,"fadein":10,"fadeout":10}}

### PLAYER_ACTION_BAR
kind: action | category: PLAYER_COMMUNICATION | targets: players
Shows a text line above the player's hotbar.
args:
- message (TEXT): text to show. Required; empty → nothing.
notes:
- Counts toward the plot message limit.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_ACTION_BAR","args":{"message":"Coins: %var(coins)%"}}

### PLAYER_OPEN_LINK_BOOK
kind: action | category: PLAYER_COMMUNICATION | targets: players
Opens a book with a clickable link to a website for the player.
args:
- link (TEXT): the web address. `http://` is added if it does not start with `http`.
notes:
- Skipped for players who have a server menu open.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_OPEN_LINK_BOOK","args":{"link":"https://mineland.net"}}

### PLAYER_OPEN_BOOK
kind: action | category: PLAYER_COMMUNICATION | targets: players
Opens a written book for the player. Each text value is one page.
args:
- texts (TEXT, up to 27 values): pages, in order. Default: empty → nothing opens.
notes:
- Skipped for players who have a server menu open.
- If the selection contains a non-player entity, the action stops at that entity.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_OPEN_BOOK","args":{"texts":["Page one","Page two"]}}

### PLAYER_GIVE_ITEMS
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players
Adds every item from `items` to the player's inventory, like picking items up.
args:
- items (ITEM, up to 36 values): items to give, with their amounts. Empty values are skipped.
notes:
- Large amounts are split into full stacks. The amount is limited to what a whole inventory could hold.
- Items that do not fit are lost (not dropped).
example: {"action":"PLAYER_GIVE_ITEMS","args":{"items":[{"item":"minecraft:diamond","count":3}]}}

### PLAYER_GIVE_VAR_ITEMS
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players | hidden in menu
Old variant of PLAYER_GIVE_ITEMS for items stored in variables. Works the same way: each value that resolves to an item is added to the inventory.
args:
- items (ITEM, up to 36 values): items or variables that hold items. Values that are not items are skipped.
notes:
- Items that do not fit are lost.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_GIVE_VAR_ITEMS","args":{"items":[{"var":"reward"}]}}

### PLAYER_SET_ITEMS
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players
Puts items into exact inventory slots. The position of a value in the list is the slot.
args:
- hotbar (ITEM, up to 9 values): hotbar slots 0–8. Value index 0 → slot 0. Use `null` for an empty slot.
- main (ITEM, up to 27 values): main inventory slots 9–35. Value index 0 → slot 9. Use `null` for an empty slot.
markers:
- state: 0 = empty slots count: every slot without an item is cleared (default), 1 = empty slots are ignored: only slots with an item change
notes:
- With state 0, every slot you do not fill is cleared, including slots past the end of a short list. Passing only `hotbar` with state 0 clears the whole main inventory.
- Armor and off hand are not touched.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_SET_ITEMS","args":{"hotbar":[{"item":"minecraft:iron_sword"},null,{"item":"minecraft:bread","count":16}]},"switches":{"state":1}}

### PLAYER_SET_VAR_ITEMS
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players | hidden in menu
Old variant of PLAYER_SET_ITEMS that takes any values (usually variables). Values that are not items count as empty slots.
args:
- hotbar (ANY, up to 9 values): hotbar slots 0–8, by position. `null` = empty slot.
- main (ANY, up to 27 values): main inventory slots 9–35, by position. `null` = empty slot.
markers:
- state: 0 = empty slots count: every slot without an item is cleared (default), 1 = empty slots are ignored
notes:
- Same slot rules as PLAYER_SET_ITEMS.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_SET_VAR_ITEMS","args":{"hotbar":[{"var":"weapon"}]},"switches":{"state":1}}

### PLAYER_SET_ENDER_CHEST_ITEMS
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players
Replaces the whole content of the player's ender chest on this plot.
args:
- items (ITEM, up to 27 values): ender chest slots 0–26, by position. `null` = empty slot.
markers:
- state: 0 = keep positions: empty values stay empty slots (default), 1 = drop empty values: items are packed to the start
notes:
- The ender chest is always cleared first, in both modes.
- The ender chest is saved per plot.
- Counts toward the plot inventory save/load limit.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_SET_ENDER_CHEST_ITEMS","args":{"items":[{"item":"minecraft:diamond"}]},"switches":{"state":0}}

### PLAYER_SET_ARMOR
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players and other living entities
Sets the armor slots. Any item or block can go into the helmet slot and is visible on the head.
args:
- helmet (ITEM): head slot.
- chestplate (ITEM): chest slot.
- leggings (ITEM): legs slot.
- boots (ITEM): feet slot.
markers:
- state: 0 = empty slots count: an armor slot without an item is cleared (default), 1 = empty slots are ignored: only given slots change
example: {"action":"PLAYER_SET_ARMOR","args":{"helmet":{"item":"minecraft:iron_helmet"},"boots":{"item":"minecraft:iron_boots"}},"switches":{"state":1}}

### PLAYER_SET_VAR_ARMOR
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players and other living entities | hidden in menu
Old variant of PLAYER_SET_ARMOR that takes any values (usually variables holding items).
args:
- helmet_var (ANY): head slot.
- chestplate_var (ANY): chest slot.
- leggings_var (ANY): legs slot.
- boots_var (ANY): feet slot.
markers:
- state: 0 = empty slots count: a slot without an item is cleared (default), 1 = empty slots are ignored
notes:
- A value that does not resolve to an item counts as empty.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_SET_VAR_ARMOR","args":{"helmet_var":{"var":"hat"}},"switches":{"state":1}}

### PLAYER_SET_ITEM_IN_HAND
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players and other living entities
Puts an item into the main hand or the off hand.
args:
- item (ITEM): item to hold. Default: empty → the hand is cleared.
markers:
- state: 0 = main hand (default), 1 = off hand
example: {"action":"PLAYER_SET_ITEM_IN_HAND","args":{"item":{"item":"minecraft:shield"}},"switches":{"state":1}}

### PLAYER_SET_CURSOR_ITEM
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players
Puts an item on the player's mouse cursor (the item being dragged in an open inventory).
args:
- item (ITEM): item for the cursor. Default: empty → the cursor is cleared.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_SET_CURSOR_ITEM","args":{"item":{"item":"minecraft:apple"}}}

### PLAYER_REMOVE_ITEMS
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players
Removes the given items from the player's inventory.
args:
- items (ITEM, up to 36 values): items to remove. The item amount is how many to remove.
notes:
- Items are matched by type and data, not by stack size.
- If the player has fewer items than the given amount, none of that item is removed.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_REMOVE_ITEMS","args":{"items":[{"item":"minecraft:gold_ingot","count":5}]}}

### PLAYER_REMOVE_VAR_ITEMS
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players | hidden in menu
Old variant of PLAYER_REMOVE_ITEMS that takes any values (usually variables holding items).
args:
- items (ANY, up to 36 values): items or variables with items. Values that are not items are skipped.
notes:
- Same rules as PLAYER_REMOVE_ITEMS.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_REMOVE_VAR_ITEMS","args":{"items":[{"var":"price"}]}}

### PLAYER_CLEAR_INVENTORY
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players and other living entities
Clears the whole inventory of the player: hotbar, main inventory, armor and off hand.
notes:
- Also clears the player's 2x2 crafting grid when no other inventory is open.
- For a non-player living entity it clears its equipment.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_CLEAR_INVENTORY"}

### PLAYER_CLEAR_ENDER_CHEST_INVENTORY
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players
Clears the player's ender chest on this plot.
notes:
- Counts toward the plot inventory save/load limit.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_CLEAR_ENDER_CHEST_INVENTORY"}

### PLAYER_SET_SLOT
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players
Changes which hotbar slot the player holds.
args:
- slot (NUMBER): hotbar slot 0–8 (0 is the leftmost). Default: 0.
notes:
- Values outside 0–8 are clamped into that range.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_SET_SLOT","args":{"slot":4}}

### PLAYER_GIVE_RANDOM_ITEM
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players
Gives the player one random item from the list. Each player gets their own random pick.
args:
- items (ITEM, up to 36 values): items to choose from. Empty values are skipped. Default: empty → nothing.
notes:
- The chosen item is given with its full amount. Items that do not fit are lost.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_GIVE_RANDOM_ITEM","args":{"items":[{"item":"minecraft:diamond"},{"item":"minecraft:emerald","count":2}]}}

### PLAYER_SAVE_INVENTORY
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players
Saves a copy of the player's whole inventory (hotbar, main, armor, off hand) for this plot. PLAYER_LOAD_INVENTORY brings it back.
notes:
- One save per player per plot; a new save replaces the old one.
- Saving an empty inventory deletes the save.
- The save is written in the background.
- Counts toward the plot inventory save/load limit.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_SAVE_INVENTORY"}

### PLAYER_LOAD_INVENTORY
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players
Replaces the player's whole inventory with the copy saved by PLAYER_SAVE_INVENTORY. Items go back to the same slots.
notes:
- Loading happens in the background, so the inventory changes a moment later, not in the same tick.
- Nothing happens if the player left the plot before loading finished.
- If reading the save fails, the inventory is not changed.
- Counts toward the plot inventory save/load limit.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_LOAD_INVENTORY"}

### PLAYER_SET_ITEM_COOLDOWN
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players
Puts a cooldown on an item type, like the ender pearl cooldown. The player cannot use items of that type until it ends.
args:
- item (ITEM): the item whose type gets the cooldown. Required.
- cooldown (NUMBER): cooldown in ticks (20 ticks = 1 second). Default: 0.
notes:
- The cooldown applies to every item of the same type (material).
- Clamped to 0–72000 ticks (1 hour). 0 removes the cooldown.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_SET_ITEM_COOLDOWN","args":{"item":{"item":"minecraft:ender_pearl"},"cooldown":100}}

### PLAYER_GET_ITEM_FROM_PLAYER_INVENTORY
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players (first selected entity only)
Saves the item from one slot of the player's inventory into a variable.
args:
- slot (NUMBER): inventory slot: 0–8 hotbar, 9–35 main inventory, 36–39 armor (boots, leggings, chestplate, helmet), 40 off hand. Default: 0.
- variable (VARIABLE): variable that receives the item. Required.
notes:
- Only the first entity of the selection is used. If it is not a player, nothing happens.
- An empty slot gives an air item.
- Use slot numbers 0–40 only.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_GET_ITEM_FROM_PLAYER_INVENTORY","args":{"slot":0,"variable":{"var":"held"}}}

### PLAYER_SET_ITEM_TO_PLAYER_INVENTORY
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players
Puts one item into one or more slots of the player's inventory.
args:
- variable (ANY): item from a variable. If it holds an item, it is used instead of `item`.
- item (ITEM): item to put. Used when `variable` is not an item.
- slots (NUMBER, up to 27 values): target slots (0–8 hotbar, 9–35 main, 36–39 armor, 40 off hand). Required.
notes:
- If neither `variable` nor `item` is an item, the slots are cleared.
- Slot numbers outside the inventory are ignored. A text value that is not a number means slot 0.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_SET_ITEM_TO_PLAYER_INVENTORY","args":{"item":{"item":"minecraft:arrow","count":64},"slots":[8,17]}}

### PLAYER_SET_VAR_WITH_ITEM_COOLDOWN
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players | hidden in menu
Old variant of PLAYER_SET_ITEM_COOLDOWN where the item comes from a variable.
args:
- variable (ANY): item (usually a variable holding an item) whose type gets the cooldown. Required.
- cooldown (NUMBER): cooldown in ticks. Default: 0.
notes:
- Clamped to 0–72000 ticks. Applies to every item of the same type.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_SET_VAR_WITH_ITEM_COOLDOWN","args":{"variable":{"var":"ability_item"},"cooldown":60}}

### PLAYER_GET_ITEM_COOLDOWN
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players
Saves the remaining cooldown of an item type into a variable, in ticks.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): variable that receives the number. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item whose type is checked. Required.
notes:
- 0 means no cooldown.
- With several selected players the variable ends up with the value of the last one.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_GET_ITEM_COOLDOWN","args":{"variable":{"var":"cd"},"item":{"item":"minecraft:ender_pearl"}}}

### PLAYER_GET_VAR_WITH_ITEM_COOLDOWN
kind: action | category: PLAYER_INVENTORY | targets: players | hidden in menu
Old variant of PLAYER_GET_ITEM_COOLDOWN where the item comes from a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): variable that receives the remaining cooldown in ticks. Required.
- item (ANY): item (usually a variable holding an item) whose type is checked. Required.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_GET_VAR_WITH_ITEM_COOLDOWN","args":{"variable":{"var":"cd"},"item":{"var":"ability_item"}}}

### PLAYER_OPEN_MENU
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MENU | targets: players
Opens a custom menu (a chest-like inventory) with the given items. Use the click events of the plot to react to clicks.
args:
- items (ITEM, up to 27 values): menu slots by position, starting at slot 0. `null` = empty slot.
- name (TEXT): menu title. Default: the normal title of the chosen inventory type.
markers:
- separator: inventory type: 0 = chest, 3 rows (default), 1 = dispenser, 2 = dropper, 3 = furnace, 4 = enchanting table, 5 = brewing stand, 6 = anvil, 7 = beacon, 8 = hopper, 9 = shulker box
notes:
- Items beyond the size of the chosen type are dropped (for example a hopper has 5 slots).
- To get a bigger chest, use PLAYER_EXPAND_MENU or PLAYER_ADD_LINE_IN_THE_MENU after opening.
- Skipped for players who have a server menu open.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_OPEN_MENU","args":{"items":[null,null,null,null,{"item":"minecraft:emerald"}],"name":"Shop"},"switches":{"separator":0}}

### PLAYER_EXPAND_MENU
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MENU | targets: players
Makes the player's open chest menu bigger by whole rows and adds items after the old content.
args:
- items (ITEM, up to 27 values): items placed after the old content, by position. `null` = empty slot.
- number (NUMBER): how many rows (9 slots each) to add. Default: 0. A negative value counts as positive.
notes:
- Works only when a chest-type inventory is open.
- The menu is limited to 6 rows (54 slots). Items that do not fit are dropped.
- The menu is closed and opened again with the same title.
- Skipped for players who have a server menu open.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_EXPAND_MENU","args":{"items":[{"item":"minecraft:arrow"}],"number":1}}

### PLAYER_SET_ITEM_IN_THE_MENU
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MENU | targets: players
Puts one item into the given slots of the inventory the player has open.
args:
- item (ITEM): item to put. Default: empty → the slots are cleared.
- numbers (NUMBER, up to 27 values): slot numbers of the open inventory, starting at 0. Required.
notes:
- Slot numbers outside the open inventory are ignored.
- It changes the real inventory that is open, for example a real chest opened with PLAYER_OPEN_CONTAINER_FROM_WORLD.
- Skipped for players who have a server menu open.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_SET_ITEM_IN_THE_MENU","args":{"item":{"item":"minecraft:barrier"},"numbers":[0,8]}}

### PLAYER_GET_ITEM_FROM_MENU
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MENU | targets: players
Saves the item from one slot of the open inventory into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): variable that receives the item. Required.
- number (NUMBER): slot number in the open inventory, starting at 0. Default: 0.
notes:
- An empty slot gives an air item.
- The slot must exist in the open inventory.
- With several selected players the variable ends up with the value of the last one.
- Skipped for players who have a server menu open.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_GET_ITEM_FROM_MENU","args":{"variable":{"var":"clicked"},"number":13}}

### PLAYER_SET_NAME_OF_MENU
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MENU | targets: players
Changes the title of the inventory the player has open. Items stay the same.
args:
- name (TEXT): new title. Default: the normal title of that inventory type.
notes:
- Needs an open inventory. Nothing happens if the player has no inventory open.
- The menu is closed and opened again as a new code menu with the same items.
- Skipped for players who have a server menu open.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_SET_NAME_OF_MENU","args":{"name":"Page 2"}}

### PLAYER_ADD_LINE_IN_THE_MENU
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MENU | targets: players
Adds one row of 9 items to the top or the bottom of the open menu.
args:
- items (ITEM, up to 9 values): the new row, by position. `null` = empty slot.
markers:
- separator: 0 = add the row at the top: old items move down (default), 1 = add the row at the bottom
notes:
- If the menu becomes bigger, it is reopened as a chest menu (max 6 rows). Items pushed past slot 53 are lost.
- Needs an open inventory.
- Skipped for players who have a server menu open.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_ADD_LINE_IN_THE_MENU","args":{"items":[{"item":"minecraft:black_stained_glass_pane"}]},"switches":{"separator":1}}

### PLAYER_DELETE_LINES_IN_THE_MENU
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MENU | targets: players
Removes rows of 9 slots from the top or the bottom of the open menu.
args:
- number (NUMBER): how many rows to remove. Default: 0. A negative value counts as positive.
markers:
- separator: 0 = remove rows from the top (default), 1 = remove rows from the bottom
notes:
- Works only if the open menu has at least 2 rows (18 slots). At least 1 row always stays.
- The menu is closed and opened again as a chest menu with the same title.
- Do not remove more rows than the menu has when removing from the top (it causes an error).
- Skipped for players who have a server menu open.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_DELETE_LINES_IN_THE_MENU","args":{"number":1},"switches":{"separator":1}}

### PLAYER_CLOSE_INVENTORY
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MENU | targets: players
Closes the inventory the player has open.
notes:
- Does nothing if no inventory is open (it does not close the chat).
- Skipped for players who have a server menu open.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_CLOSE_INVENTORY"}

### PLAYER_OPEN_CONTAINER_FROM_WORLD
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MENU | targets: players
Opens the inventory of a block in the world for the player, for example a chest, barrel, furnace or hopper.
args:
- location (LOCATION): position of the block. Required.
markers:
- state: 0 = open the real inventory: changes are kept (default), 1 = open a copy: changes are not saved to the block
notes:
- Double chests open as one big inventory.
- Anvil: opens an anvil screen (not linked to that block).
- Enchanting table and crafting table: open their screen.
- Ender chest: opens the player's own ender chest (state 1 opens a copy of it).
- Other blocks do nothing.
- Skipped for players who have a server menu open.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_OPEN_CONTAINER_FROM_WORLD","args":{"location":{"loc":[10,64,10,0,0]}},"switches":{"state":0}}

### PLAYER_OPEN_ENTITY_INV_FROM_WORLD
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MENU | targets: players
Finds an entity with an inventory near a location (for example a chest minecart or a donkey) and opens its inventory for the player.
args:
- location (LOCATION): center of the search. Required.
- radius (NUMBER): search radius in blocks. Default: 0.1. Clamped to -100…100.
markers:
- state: 0 = open the real inventory: changes are kept (default), 1 = open a copy: changes are not saved to the entity
notes:
- The first entity found that has an inventory is used. Players count too.
- In copy mode, entities whose inventory type cannot be copied (for example players) are skipped.
- Skipped for players who have a server menu open.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_OPEN_ENTITY_INV_FROM_WORLD","args":{"location":{"loc":[10,64,10,0,0]},"radius":2},"switches":{"state":0}}

### PLAYER_TELEPORT
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MOVEMENT | targets: any selected entity
Teleports the selected entities to a location.
args:
- location (LOCATION): destination. Required.
markers:
- mode: 0 = use position and view direction of the location (default), 1 = use only the coordinates, keep own view direction, 2 = use only the view direction (yaw/pitch), keep own position
notes:
- Passengers riding the entity are ejected first.
- The location is moved inside the plot if it is outside.
- Counts toward the plot teleport limit; if the limit is hit, the program stops.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_TELEPORT","args":{"location":{"loc":[0.5,65,0.5,90,0]}},"switches":{"mode":0}}

### PLAYER_TURN_TO_THE_LOCATION
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MOVEMENT | targets: any selected entity
Turns the entity so it looks at a location. Its position does not change.
args:
- location (LOCATION): point to look at. Required.
notes:
- Nothing happens if the location is exactly the entity's position.
- Done with a teleport, so passengers are ejected.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_TURN_TO_THE_LOCATION","args":{"location":{"loc":[0,70,0,0,0]}}}

### PLAYER_RANDOM_TELEPORT
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MOVEMENT | targets: any selected entity
Teleports each selected entity to one random location from the list. Each entity gets its own random pick.
args:
- locations (LOCATION, up to 36 values): possible destinations. Required.
markers:
- mode: 0 = use position and view direction of the location (default), 1 = use only the coordinates, keep own view direction, 2 = use only the view direction, keep own position
notes:
- Counts toward the plot teleport limit.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_RANDOM_TELEPORT","args":{"locations":[{"loc":[10,64,10,0,0]},{"loc":[-10,64,-10,180,0]}]},"switches":{"mode":0}}

### PLAYER_TELEPORT_SEQUENCE
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MOVEMENT | targets: any selected entity
Teleports the entity through all locations one by one, with a pause between steps. The code after this action does not wait for it.
args:
- locations (LOCATION, up to 27 values): route, in order. Required.
- delay (NUMBER): pause between teleports in ticks. Default: 20. Minimum 1.
markers:
- mode: 0 = use position and view direction of each location (default), 1 = use only the coordinates, keep the view direction the entity had at the start, 2 = use only the view direction, keep the position the entity had at the start
notes:
- The first teleport happens right away.
- The route stops for an entity that died or left the plot world.
- If the total number of teleports of all entities is too large, the program stops.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_TELEPORT_SEQUENCE","args":{"locations":[{"loc":[0,65,0,0,0]},{"loc":[5,65,0,0,0]},{"loc":[10,65,0,0,0]}],"delay":10},"switches":{"mode":0}}

### PLAYER_LAUNCH_UPWARDS
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MOVEMENT | targets: any selected entity
Sets the entity's velocity straight up (or down for a negative value).
args:
- multiplier (NUMBER): vertical speed. Default: 0.
notes:
- Clamped to -10…10, and the final speed is capped at 4 blocks per tick.
- It replaces the current velocity; it does not add to it.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_LAUNCH_UPWARDS","args":{"multiplier":1.5}}

### PLAYER_LAUNCH_FORWARD
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MOVEMENT | targets: any selected entity
Sets the entity's velocity in the direction it is looking.
args:
- multiplier (NUMBER): speed along the view direction. A negative value launches backwards. Default: 0.
notes:
- Clamped to -10…10. Each velocity component is capped at 4 (the vector is scaled down to fit).
- It replaces the current velocity.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_LAUNCH_FORWARD","args":{"multiplier":2}}

### PLAYER_LAUNCH_TOWARD
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MOVEMENT | targets: any selected entity
Sets the entity's velocity to the given x, y and z values (world directions, not view direction).
args:
- x (NUMBER): speed along X. Default: 0.
- y (NUMBER): speed along Y (up). Default: 0.
- z (NUMBER): speed along Z. Default: 0.
notes:
- Each value is clamped to -10…10, then the vector is scaled down so no component is above 4.
- It replaces the current velocity.
- The values are blocks per tick (a walking player moves about 0.2, an arrow about 3).
- To launch along a VECTOR variable, split it first with VARIABLE_GET_COORDINATES_OF_VECTOR and give the parts as x, y, z (section 14.7).
example: {"action":"PLAYER_LAUNCH_TOWARD","args":{"x":1,"y":0.5,"z":0}}

### PLAYER_LAUNCH_TOLOC
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MOVEMENT | targets: any selected entity
Launches the entity toward a location. The velocity is the difference between the location and the entity's position.
args:
- location (LOCATION): target point. Required.
notes:
- The velocity is the whole distance per tick: a target 3 blocks away gives speed 3 blocks per tick, which flies far past it. Only the cap (no component above 4) limits it. For a controlled speed, build the vector yourself (VARIABLE_CREATE_VECTOR_BETWEEN_LOCATIONS, VARIABLE_SET_LENGTH_OF_VECTOR, VARIABLE_GET_COORDINATES_OF_VECTOR, PLAYER_LAUNCH_TOWARD).
- It replaces the current velocity.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_LAUNCH_TOLOC","args":{"location":{"loc":[20,70,0,0,0]}}}

### PLAYER_RIDE_ENTITY
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MOVEMENT | targets: any selected entity
Makes the selected entity ride another entity, found by its name or UUID.
args:
- name (TEXT): UUID of the entity to ride, or its name (case does not matter). Required.
notes:
- The ride starts 2 ticks later.
- An entity cannot ride itself. Ender dragons cannot ride or be ridden.
- Only entities in the plot world are searched. If several have the same name, the first one found is used.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_RIDE_ENTITY","args":{"name":"Horse"}}

### PLAYER_KICK
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MOVEMENT | targets: players
Kicks the player from the plot: they are sent to the Creative+ lobby. They are not disconnected from the server.
notes:
- The plot owner cannot be kicked; they get a message instead.
- Some players cannot be kicked; they get a message instead.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_KICK"}

### PLAYER_MOVE_TO_ANOTHER_PLOT
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MOVEMENT | targets: players
Asks the player to move to another plot (game). The player sees a confirmation; if they accept, they join that plot.
args:
- id (TEXT): id of the target plot, as shown to players. Required.
notes:
- The plot info is loaded in the background, so the confirmation appears a moment later.
- If the plot is not found, the confirmation shows "unknown game".
- Skipped for players who have a server menu open.
example: {"action":"PLAYER_MOVE_TO_ANOTHER_PLOT","args":{"id":"12345"}}

### ENTITY_MOVE_TO_LOCATION
kind: action | category: PLAYER_MOVEMENT | targets: mobs in the selection
Makes a mob walk to a location using normal mob pathfinding.
args:
- location (LOCATION): where to walk. Required.
- speed (NUMBER): walking speed multiplier. Default: 1. Clamped to 0.01–10.
notes:
- Only mobs move. Players and other entities are skipped, so select mobs first.
