- power (NUMBER): explosion power, whole number, clamped to -5..5. Default: 4 (normal TNT).
- fuse (NUMBER): ticks until the explosion (20 ticks = 1 second). Default: 0 (explodes at once).
markers:
- glowing: 0 = no glow (default), 1 = glowing outline
- gravity: 0 = has gravity (default), 1 = no gravity
notes:
- Remember to set `fuse`: the default 0 explodes immediately.
- Counts toward the plot entity limit.
example: {"action":"GAME_SPAWN_TNT","args":{"locations":{"loc":[0,70,0,0,0]},"power":2,"fuse":60}}

### GAME_SPAWN_VEHICLE
kind: action | category: GAME_ENTITY_SPAWNING | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Spawns a minecart or a boat at every given location.
args:
- locations (LOCATION, up to 9 values): one vehicle per location. Required.
- item (ITEM): the vehicle type. Any boat or raft item = that boat. `minecart` = minecart. `chest_minecart`/chest/trapped chest/ender chest = chest minecart. `hopper_minecart`/hopper = hopper minecart. `furnace_minecart`/furnace = furnace minecart. `tnt_minecart`/TNT = TNT minecart. `command_block_minecart`/command blocks = command block minecart. Spawner or any spawn egg = spawner minecart. Other items → nothing.
- name (TEXT): name shown above the vehicle. Default: no name.
- speed (NUMBER): maximum speed, clamped to 0–1.9. Default: the normal speed.
- block (ITEM): a block item shown inside a minecart (minecarts only). Non-block items are ignored.
markers:
- glowing: 0 = no glow (default), 1 = glowing outline
- gravity: 0 = has gravity (default), 1 = no gravity
- invulnerable: 0 = can be broken (default), 1 = invulnerable
- slow: 0 = an empty minecart does not slow down (default), 1 = an empty minecart slows down (minecarts only)
- silent: 0 = makes sounds (default), 1 = silent
notes:
- Counts toward the plot entity limit.
example: {"action":"GAME_SPAWN_VEHICLE","args":{"locations":{"loc":[3.5,64,3.5,90,0]},"item":{"item":"minecraft:minecart"},"block":{"item":"minecraft:pumpkin"}},"switches":{"invulnerable":1}}

### GAME_SPAWN_XP_ORB
kind: action | category: GAME_ENTITY_SPAWNING | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Spawns an experience orb at every given location.
args:
- locations (LOCATION, up to 18 values): one orb per location. Required.
- xp (NUMBER): experience points in the orb, whole number. Negative becomes 0. Default: not set (the orb keeps its spawn value).
- name (TEXT): name shown above the orb. Default: no name.
markers:
- gravity: 0 = has gravity (default), 1 = floats in place
notes:
- Counts toward the plot entity limit.
example: {"action":"GAME_SPAWN_XP_ORB","args":{"locations":{"loc":[0.5,65,0.5,0,0]},"xp":50}}

### GAME_SPAWN_LIGHTNING
kind: action | category: GAME_ENTITY_SPAWNING | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Strikes lightning at a location.
args:
- location (LOCATION): where the lightning strikes. Required.
markers:
- separator: 0 = real lightning (damages and can set fire) (default), 1 = visual only (no damage, no fire)
example: {"action":"GAME_SPAWN_LIGHTNING","args":{"location":{"loc":[10,64,10,0,0]}},"switches":{"separator":1}}

### GAME_SPAWN_DISPLAY_TEXT
kind: action | category: GAME_ENTITY_SPAWNING | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Spawns a text display entity (a floating text "visualizer") at a location.
args:
- texts (TEXT, up to 27 values): the shown text. All values are joined with the separator. Required: if the joined text is empty, nothing is spawned.
- text (TEXT): the NAME of the display (a hidden custom name). It is not shown; use it to find the display later, for example with `IF_ENTITY_NAME_EQUALS`. Default: no name.
- location (LOCATION): where the display appears. Required.
markers:
- separator: 0 = join values with no separator (default), 1 = join with a space, 2 = join with a new line
notes:
- It is a real entity of the world: it stays until it is removed (for example with `ENTITY_REMOVE`). Change it later with the `ENTITY_SET_DISPLAY_*` actions.
- It faces the direction of the location's yaw and pitch (it does not turn to the player). Use `ENTITY_SET_DISPLAY_BILLBOARD` to change that.
- The text is cut at about 1024 characters.
- Counts toward the plot entity limit. It becomes the last spawned entity (`SELECT_LAST_SPAWNED_MOB`).
example: {"action":"GAME_SPAWN_DISPLAY_TEXT","args":{"texts":["§6Welcome!","§7Have fun"],"text":"welcome_sign","location":{"loc":[0.5,66,0.5,180,0]}},"switches":{"separator":2}}

### GAME_SPAWN_DISPLAY_BLOCK
kind: action | category: GAME_ENTITY_SPAWNING | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Spawns a block display entity (a block "visualizer" without collision) at a location.
args:
- location (LOCATION): where the display appears (its corner, not its center). Required.
- name (TEXT): the NAME of the display (a hidden custom name), used to find it later. Default: no name.
- block (BLOCK): the shown block, given as a block item. Non-block items → nothing. Block state from the item is kept if the item has it.
notes:
- A real entity of the world: it stays until removed. Change it later with the `ENTITY_SET_DISPLAY_*` actions.
- The block fills the cube from `location` to `location` + 1 on every axis. Scale grows it from that corner, rotations turn it around that corner, translation moves it together with the corner.
- Counts toward the plot entity limit. It becomes the last spawned entity (`SELECT_LAST_SPAWNED_MOB`).
example: {"action":"GAME_SPAWN_DISPLAY_BLOCK","args":{"location":{"loc":[2,65,2,0,0]},"name":"gem","block":{"item":"minecraft:diamond_block"}}}

### GAME_SPAWN_DISPLAY_ITEM
kind: action | category: GAME_ENTITY_SPAWNING | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Spawns an item display entity (an item "visualizer" floating in place) at a location.
args:
- location (LOCATION): where the display appears (its center). Required.
- name (TEXT): the NAME of the display (a hidden custom name), used to find it later. Default: no name.
- item (ITEM): the shown item. Required.
notes:
- A real entity of the world: it stays until removed. Change it later with the `ENTITY_SET_DISPLAY_*` actions.
- Counts toward the plot entity limit. It becomes the last spawned entity (`SELECT_LAST_SPAWNED_MOB`).
example: {"action":"GAME_SPAWN_DISPLAY_ITEM","args":{"location":{"loc":[0.5,66,0.5,0,0]},"name":"trophy","item":{"item":"minecraft:golden_apple"}}}

### GAME_CANCEL_EVENT
kind: action | category: GAME_CODE_UTILITY | targets: the current event (works even with an empty selection)
Cancels the event that started this line, for example stops a block break, a chat message or damage.
notes:
- Works only in events marked cancellable. In other events nothing happens.
- It must run while the event is still happening: after `GAME_WAIT` it has no effect.
- A later GAME_RETURN_EVENT undoes it.
example: {"action":"GAME_CANCEL_EVENT"}

### GAME_RETURN_EVENT
kind: action | category: GAME_CODE_UTILITY | targets: the current event (works even with an empty selection)
Un-cancels the event that started this line (the event happens normally again).
notes:
- Works only in cancellable events, before any `GAME_WAIT`.
example: {"action":"GAME_RETURN_EVENT"}

### GAME_SET_ITEMSTACK_OF_EVENT
kind: action | category: GAME_CODE_UTILITY | targets: the current event (runs once per selected target)
Replaces the item of the current event with another item.
args:
- item (ITEM): the new item. Required; empty → nothing.
notes:
- What is replaced depends on the event: `PLAYER_CRAFT_ITEM` = craft result; `PLAYER_CLICK_INVENTORY` = the clicked slot; `PLAYER_DRAG_INVENTORY` = the cursor; `PLAYER_CONSUME_ITEM` = the eaten item; `PLAYER_DROP_ITEM` = the dropped item; `PLAYER_DAMAGE_ITEM`, `PLAYER_FOOD_LEVEL_CHANGE`, `PLAYER_LEASH_ENTITY` = the item in the held hotbar slot; player click events = the item in the used hand; `PLAYER_FISHING` = item data of the used item; `PLAYER_ITEM_MEND` = the mended item in the hand; `PLAYER_MANIPULATE_ARMOR_STAND` = the clicked armor stand slot; `PLAYER_SWAP_HANDS` = the item that goes to the off hand; `ENTITY_PICKUP_ITEM` (not for players) and `ENTITY_INVENTORY_PICKUP_ITEM` = the picked item; `ENTITY_ITEM_DESPAWN`, `ENTITY_WITCH_CONSUME_POTION`, `ENTITY_WITCH_THROW_POTION` = item data only; `ENTITY_ITEM_MERGE` = the merged item, 1 tick later; `PLOT_BLOCK_BREW` = every filled bottle slot; `PLOT_BLOCK_DISPENSE` = the dispensed item; `PLOT_BREWING_STAND_FUEL` and `PLOT_FURNACE_BURN` = the fuel slot; `PLOT_FURNACE_SMELT` = the result; `PLOT_INVENTORY_MOVE_ITEM` = the moved item.
- In `PLAYER_PICKUP_ITEM` it does nothing.
- In other events it does nothing.
- `PLOT_*` events usually have an empty selection. Select a target first (for example `SELECT_RANDOM_PLAYER`), or this action does not run.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_ITEMSTACK_OF_EVENT","args":{"item":{"item":"minecraft:golden_carrot"}}}

### GAME_SET_DAMAGE_OF_EVENT
kind: action | category: GAME_CODE_UTILITY | targets: the current event (runs once per selected target)
Changes the damage of the current event.
args:
- damage (NUMBER): new damage (2 = one heart). A negative value becomes 1. Default: 1.
notes:
- Damage events (`PLAYER_TAKE_DAMAGE`, `PLAYER_DAMAGE_PLAYER`, `ENTITY_TAKE_DAMAGE` and the other damage events): sets the damage before armor.
- `PLAYER_DAMAGE_ITEM`: sets the durability loss of the item (whole number).
- `ENTITY_VEHICLE_TAKE_DAMAGE`: sets the damage to the vehicle.
- `ENTITY_KNOCKBACK_BY_ENTITY`: changes the damage of the pushed entity's last damage.
- In other events nothing happens.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_DAMAGE_OF_EVENT","args":{"damage":10}}

### GAME_SET_BLOCK
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once, with the first selected target)
Places one block type at every given location.
args:
- variable (ITEM): a variable that holds a block item. If it is set, it is used instead of `block`.
- block (BLOCK): the block item to place. A water bucket places water, a lava bucket places lava. Empty (and no `variable`) → air (removes the blocks).
- data (NUMBER): old (1.12) block data value 0–15, for example a rotation. Default: the normal block state.
- locations (LOCATION, up to 36 values): where to place the block. Required.
notes:
- An item that is not a block (for example a sword) → nothing.
- Limit: 5000 blocks per second per plot for all block actions together. When the budget is used up, the remaining locations are skipped.
- Placement updates neighbours (water flows, sand falls).
- If the data value gives an invalid block, the normal block state is used.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_BLOCK","args":{"block":{"item":"minecraft:gold_block"},"locations":[{"loc":[0,64,0,0,0]},{"loc":[1,64,0,0,0]}]}}

### GAME_FILL_REGION_BLOCKS
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once, with the first selected target)
Fills the box between two corners with one block type.
args:
- block (BLOCK): the block item. A water bucket = water, a lava bucket = lava. Empty → air (clears the box).
- pos1 (LOCATION): first corner. Required.
- pos2 (LOCATION): opposite corner. Default: pos1 (one block).
- data (NUMBER): old (1.12) block data value 0–15. Default: the normal block state.
notes:
- Both corners must be inside the plot, otherwise nothing happens.
- The box must fit in the block budget (5000 blocks per second per plot). A box with more blocks than the budget left is skipped. A box bigger than 5000 blocks never works.
- Fill, copy and tree actions share a limit of world operations. Going over it stops the current run of the line.
- No physics: sand does not fall, water does not flow at once.
example: {"action":"GAME_FILL_REGION_BLOCKS","args":{"block":{"item":"minecraft:glass"},"pos1":{"loc":[0,64,0,0,0]},"pos2":{"loc":[9,64,9,0,0]}}}

### GAME_BREAK_BLOCK
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Breaks blocks as if a player mined them. The blocks drop their items.
args:
- locations (LOCATION, up to 36 values): blocks to break. Required.
- tool (ITEM): the tool used. It decides the drops (for example silk touch or fortune). Default: no tool (normal drops).
notes:
- Uses the block budget (5000 blocks per second per plot). Over the budget, blocks are skipped.
example: {"action":"GAME_BREAK_BLOCK","args":{"locations":{"loc":[0,64,0,0,0]},"tool":{"item":"minecraft:diamond_pickaxe"}}}

### GAME_COPY_BLOCKS
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Copies a box of blocks (air included) to another place.
args:
- pos1 (LOCATION): first corner of the source box. Required.
- pos2 (LOCATION): opposite corner of the source box. Required.
- from (LOCATION): a reference point of the source. Required.
- paste (LOCATION): where the reference point lands. Required. Every block moves by `paste - from`.
notes:
- If you set `from` = the lowest corner of the box, `paste` is the lowest corner of the copy.
- pos1, pos2 and paste must be inside the plot, otherwise nothing happens. Copied blocks that fall outside the plot or the world height are skipped.
- Chest contents, sign text and other block data are copied too.
- Block budget: a copy of up to 5000 blocks uses its size; 5001–50000 blocks counts as 5000 (it needs the full budget of the second); more than 50000 blocks never works.
- Shares the world operation limit with fill and tree actions; going over it stops the current run of the line.
example: {"action":"GAME_COPY_BLOCKS","args":{"pos1":{"loc":[0,64,0,0,0]},"pos2":{"loc":[4,68,4,0,0]},"from":{"loc":[0,64,0,0,0]},"paste":{"loc":[20,64,0,0,0]}}}

### GAME_FILL_HOLDER
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Adds items to a container block (chest, barrel, hopper, furnace, shulker box and so on).
args:
- location (LOCATION): the container. Required; not a container → nothing.
- items (ITEM, up to 36 values): items to add, with their amounts. They stack with items already inside. Default: empty → nothing.
notes:
- If the container is full, the items that do not fit are lost.
example: {"action":"GAME_FILL_HOLDER","args":{"location":{"loc":[2,64,2,0,0]},"items":[{"item":"minecraft:bread","count":8},{"item":"minecraft:iron_sword"}]}}

### GAME_REMOVE_HOLDER_ITEMS
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Removes items from a container block.
args:
- location (LOCATION): the container. Required.
- items (ITEM, up to 36 values): items to remove. The amount of each item is how many to remove (from any slots). Default: empty → nothing.
example: {"action":"GAME_REMOVE_HOLDER_ITEMS","args":{"location":{"loc":[2,64,2,0,0]},"items":{"item":"minecraft:bread","count":4}}}

### GAME_SET_HOLDER_ITEMS
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Puts one item into the given slots of a container block, replacing what was there.
args:
- location (LOCATION): the container. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item to put. Empty → the slots are cleared.
- variable (ITEM): a variable that holds an item. If set, it is used instead of `item`.
- slots (NUMBER, up to 36 values): slot numbers, starting at 0 (a single chest has slots 0–26). Required.
notes:
- Slots outside the container size are skipped.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_HOLDER_ITEMS","args":{"location":{"loc":[2,64,2,0,0]},"item":{"item":"minecraft:emerald"},"slots":[0,13]}}

### GAME_EMPTY_HOLDER
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Removes all items from container blocks.
args:
- locations (LOCATION, up to 18 values): containers to clear. Locations without a container are skipped.
example: {"action":"GAME_EMPTY_HOLDER","args":{"locations":[{"loc":[2,64,2,0,0]},{"loc":[4,64,2,0,0]}]}}

### GAME_CHANGE_SIGN
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Changes one line on the front side of a sign.
args:
- location (LOCATION): the sign. Required; not a sign → nothing.
- text (TEXT): new text of the line. Required.
- line (NUMBER): line number 0–3 (0 = top). Values outside are clamped. Default: 0.
notes:
- Text that looks like an IP address or a domain (for example `play.server.net`) is replaced with an empty line.
example: {"action":"GAME_CHANGE_SIGN","args":{"location":{"loc":[0,65,3,0,0]},"text":"§aScore: %var(score)%","line":1}}

### GAME_GET_BLOCK_NAME
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Saves the custom name of a block (a renamed chest, furnace, hopper and so on) into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): variable that receives the name. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the block. Required; a block that cannot have a name → the variable is not changed.
example: {"action":"GAME_GET_BLOCK_NAME","args":{"variable":{"var":"chest_name"},"location":{"loc":[2,64,2,0,0]}}}

### GAME_SET_BLOCK_NAME
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Renames blocks that have an inventory. The name is shown as the title when a player opens it.
args:
- text (TEXT): the new name. Required.
- locations (LOCATION, up to 36 values): blocks to rename. Blocks that cannot have a name are skipped.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_BLOCK_NAME","args":{"text":"§6Treasure","locations":{"loc":[2,64,2,0,0]}}}

### GAME_GET_ITEM_FROM_CONTAINER
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Saves the item from one slot of a container block into a variable.
args:
- output (VARIABLE): variable that receives the item. Required.
- slot (NUMBER): slot number, starting at 0. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the container. Required.
notes:
- An empty slot gives an air item.
- A slot number outside the container makes the action fail with an error.
example: {"action":"GAME_GET_ITEM_FROM_CONTAINER","args":{"output":{"var":"item"},"slot":0,"location":{"loc":[2,64,2,0,0]}}}

### GAME_SET_ITEMS_IN_ENDER_CHEST
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: players (their ender chest)
Puts one item into the given slots of each selected player's ender chest.
args:
- item (ITEM): the item to put. Empty (and no `variable`) → the slots are cleared.
- variable (ITEM): a variable that holds an item. If set, it is used instead of `item`.
- slots (NUMBER, up to 27 values): slot numbers 0–26. Required. Others are skipped.
notes:
- The ender chest belongs to this plot and is saved for this plot.
- Counts toward the plot inventory save/load limit.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_ITEMS_IN_ENDER_CHEST","args":{"item":{"item":"minecraft:diamond","count":5},"slots":0}}

### GAME_GET_ITEM_FROM_ENDER_CHEST
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: players (their ender chest)
Saves the item from one slot of the selected player's ender chest into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): variable that receives the item. Required.
- slot (NUMBER): slot number 0–26. Required. Other numbers → the variable is not changed.
notes:
- With several selected players, each run overwrites the same variable; the last player wins.
example: {"action":"GAME_GET_ITEM_FROM_ENDER_CHEST","args":{"variable":{"var":"ec_item"},"slot":4}}

### GAME_GET_ITEMS_FROM_CONTAINER
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Saves all items of a container block into a list variable.
args:
- output_list (LIST): list variable that receives the items. Use a variable value. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the container. Required.
notes:
- The old content of the list is removed first.
- Empty slots are skipped, so list positions do not match slot numbers.
example: {"action":"GAME_GET_ITEMS_FROM_CONTAINER","args":{"output_list":{"var":"loot"},"location":{"loc":[2,64,2,0,0]}}}

### GAME_GET_LINE_FROM_SIGN
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Saves one line of a sign (front side) into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): variable that receives the text. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the sign. Required.
- number (NUMBER): line number 0–3 (0 = top). Required.
notes:
- Line numbers outside 0–3 make the action fail with an error.
example: {"action":"GAME_GET_LINE_FROM_SIGN","args":{"variable":{"var":"line"},"location":{"loc":[0,65,3,0,0]},"number":0}}

### GAME_GENERATE_TREE
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Grows a tree of the chosen type at a location.
args:
- location (LOCATION): the base of the tree. Required.
markers:
- type: 0 = oak (default), 1 = big oak, 2 = dark oak, 3 = swamp oak, 4 = birch, 5 = tall birch, 6 = acacia, 7 = spruce, 8 = tall spruce, 9 = giant spruce, 10 = jungle bush, 11 = small jungle tree, 12 = big jungle tree, 13 = jungle tree with cocoa, 14 = red mushroom, 15 = brown mushroom, 16 = chorus plant
notes:
- If the tree cannot grow there (no space or wrong ground), nothing happens.
- Uses the world operation limit (shared with fill and copy). Going over it stops the current run of the line.
example: {"action":"GAME_GENERATE_TREE","args":{"location":{"loc":[5,64,5,0,0]}},"switches":{"type":4}}

### GAME_SET_LOOT_TABLE
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Gives a container (chest, barrel and so on) a vanilla loot table, like dungeon chests.
args:
- location (LOCATION): the container. Required.
- key (TEXT): loot table id, for example `minecraft:chests/simple_dungeon`. Empty → the loot table is removed. Unknown id → nothing.
- seed (NUMBER): random seed, whole number. Default: 0.
notes:
- The loot appears when a player opens the container for the first time.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_LOOT_TABLE","args":{"location":{"loc":[2,64,2,0,0]},"key":"minecraft:chests/simple_dungeon","seed":12345}}

### GAME_SET_LOCK
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Locks a container (chest, furnace, barrel, hopper, beacon and so on) with a key name. Only a player who holds an item with exactly this custom name can open it.
args:
- location (LOCATION): the container. Required.
- key (TEXT): the key name. Color codes with `§` count as part of the name. Empty → the lock is removed.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_LOCK","args":{"location":{"loc":[2,64,2,0,0]},"key":"§6Gold Key"}}

### GAME_GET_BURN_TIME
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Saves the remaining fuel burn time of a furnace (also blast furnace and smoker) into a variable, in ticks.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): variable that receives the number. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the furnace. Required; not a furnace → the variable is not changed.
example: {"action":"GAME_GET_BURN_TIME","args":{"variable":{"var":"burn"},"location":{"loc":[3,64,0,0,0]}}}

Sets the remaining fuel burn time of a furnace.
args:

- time (NUMBER): burn time in ticks, whole number, clamped to 0–32767. Default: 0.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_BURN_TIME","args":{"location":{"loc":[3,64,0,0,0]},"time":1600}}

Sets the cooking progress of a furnace.

### GAME_GET_FUEL_LEVEL
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Saves the fuel level of a brewing stand (blaze powder charges left) into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): variable that receives the number. Required.

### GAME_SET_FUEL_LEVEL
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Sets the fuel level of a brewing stand.
args:
- location (LOCATION): the brewing stand. Required.
- level (NUMBER): fuel charges, whole number. Values below 1 become 1. Default: 1.
notes:
- The level cannot be set to 0 with this action.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_FUEL_LEVEL","args":{"location":{"loc":[4,64,0,0,0]},"level":20}}

### GAME_SET_NOTE
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Sets the note of a note block.
args:
- location (LOCATION): the note block. Required; not a note block → nothing.
- octave (NUMBER): octave 0, 1 or 2, whole number. Values outside are clamped. Default: 1.
markers:
- tone: 0 = G (default), 1 = A, 2 = B, 3 = C, 4 = D, 5 = E, 6 = F
- type: 0 = flat (default), 1 = natural, 2 = sharp
notes:
- The note block range is F♯ of octave 0 to F♯ of octave 2. In octave 2 only tone F with type sharp works; any other note in octave 2 makes the action fail with an error.
- B sharp becomes C and E sharp becomes F.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_NOTE","args":{"location":{"loc":[6,64,0,0,0]},"octave":1},"switches":{"tone":3,"type":1}}

### GAME_PLAY_NOTE
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Plays the current note of a note block with a chosen instrument and shows a note particle above it. The block is not changed.
args:
- location (LOCATION): the note block. Required; not a note block → nothing.
- instrument (TEXT): instrument name (case does not matter): `PIANO`, `BASS_DRUM`, `SNARE_DRUM`, `STICKS`, `BASS_GUITAR`, `FLUTE`, `BELL`, `GUITAR`, `CHIME`, `XYLOPHONE`, `IRON_XYLOPHONE`, `COW_BELL`, `DIDGERIDOO`, `BIT`, `BANJO`, `PLING`, `ZOMBIE`, `SKELETON`, `CREEPER`, `DRAGON`, `WITHER_SKELETON`, `PIGLIN`. Required; unknown name → nothing.
notes:
- The sound uses the "records" volume slider of the player.
example: {"action":"GAME_PLAY_NOTE","args":{"location":{"loc":[6,64,0,0,0]},"instrument":"BELL"}}

Saves the music disc in a jukebox into a variable as an item.

- location (LOCATION): the jukebox. Required; not a jukebox → the variable is not changed.
notes:

kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Puts a music disc into a jukebox.

### GAME_GET_BLOCK_FACE
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Saves the direction a block faces into a variable as text.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): variable that receives the text. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the block. Required.
notes:
- The result is a name like `NORTH`, `EAST`, `SOUTH`, `WEST`, `UP`, `DOWN`. Signs, banners and heads can give 16 directions, like `NORTH_NORTH_EAST`.
- A block without a direction → the variable is not changed.
example: {"action":"GAME_GET_BLOCK_FACE","args":{"variable":{"var":"facing"},"location":{"loc":[0,64,5,0,0]}}}

### GAME_SET_BLOCK_FACE
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Turns a block (stairs, furnace, piston, sign, head and so on) to a direction.
args:
- location (LOCATION): the block. Required.
- text (TEXT): direction name, case does not matter: `NORTH`, `EAST`, `SOUTH`, `WEST`, `UP`, `DOWN`, or a 16-way name like `NORTH_EAST`, `NORTH_NORTH_EAST` for signs, banners and heads. Required.
notes:
- Unknown names and directions the block does not support → nothing.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_BLOCK_FACE","args":{"location":{"loc":[0,64,5,0,0]},"text":"EAST"}}

### GAME_GET_SPAWNER_PARAMETER
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Saves one setting of a mob spawner block into a variable as a number.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): variable that receives the number. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the spawner. Required; not a spawner → the variable is not changed.
markers:
- parameter: 0 = current delay until the next spawn, in ticks (default), 1 = minimum spawn delay, 2 = maximum spawn delay, 3 = mobs per spawn, 4 = spawn range in blocks, 5 = maximum nearby entities, 6 = required player range in blocks
example: {"action":"GAME_GET_SPAWNER_PARAMETER","args":{"variable":{"var":"delay"},"location":{"loc":[8,64,0,0,0]}},"switches":{"parameter":3}}

### GAME_SET_SPAWNER_PARAMETER
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Changes one setting of a mob spawner block.
args:
- location (LOCATION): the spawner. Required.
- number (NUMBER): the new value, whole number. Negative becomes 0. Default: 1.
markers:
- parameter: 0 = current delay until the next spawn, in ticks (default), 1 = minimum spawn delay, 2 = maximum spawn delay, 3 = mobs per spawn, 4 = spawn range in blocks, 5 = maximum nearby entities, 6 = required player range in blocks
notes:
- Delays (0, 1, 2) are at least 5 ticks. The minimum delay is not set above the current maximum, and the maximum not below the current minimum: to raise both, set the maximum first.
- Mobs per spawn: more than 20 → nothing is changed.
- Spawn range is at most 20, required player range at most 30.
- Maximum nearby entities is at most the plot entity limit.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_SPAWNER_PARAMETER","args":{"location":{"loc":[8,64,0,0,0]},"number":4},"switches":{"parameter":3}}

### GAME_GET_SPAWNER_ENTITY_TYPE
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Saves the mob type of a spawner block into a variable as text.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): variable that receives the text. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the spawner. Required; not a spawner → the variable is not changed.
notes:
- The text is an upper-case type name like `ZOMBIE` or `SKELETON`. A few types use old 1.12 names, for example `PIG_ZOMBIE` for the zombified piglin.
- A spawner without a type gives empty text.
example: {"action":"GAME_GET_SPAWNER_ENTITY_TYPE","args":{"variable":{"var":"spawner_mob"},"location":{"loc":[8,64,0,0,0]}}}

### GAME_SET_SPAWNER_ENTITY_TYPE
kind: action | category: GAME_BLOCK_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Sets the mob type of a spawner block.
args:
- location (LOCATION): the spawner. Required.
- name (TEXT): type name like `ZOMBIE` or `zombie` (no `minecraft:` prefix). Old 1.12 names also work. Required.
notes:
- Unknown types, types that cannot be spawned, and ender dragon, player, painting, item frame, lightning bolt → nothing.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_SPAWNER_ENTITY_TYPE","args":{"location":{"loc":[8,64,0,0,0]},"name":"SKELETON"}}

### GAME_CREATE_EXPLOSION
kind: action | category: GAME_SPECIAL_EFFECTS | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Creates an explosion at every given location. It hurts and pushes entities nearby.
args:
- locations (LOCATION, up to 9 values): explosion centers. Required.
- power (NUMBER): explosion power, clamped to 0–5 (TNT is 4). Default: 1.
markers:
- fire: 0 = normal explosion (default), 1 = explosion that sets fire
- break: 0 = breaks blocks (default), 1 = does not break blocks
notes:
- The explosion has no source: it also hurts the player whose code created it. Power 2 or more next to a player can kill.
- Explosions have a rate limit per plot. Going over it stops the current run of the line.
example: {"action":"GAME_CREATE_EXPLOSION","args":{"locations":{"loc":[0,64,0,0,0]},"power":3},"switches":{"break":1}}

### GAME_PLAY_FIREWORK_EXPLOSION
kind: action | category: GAME_SPECIAL_EFFECTS | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Shows a firework explosion at every given location. Colors and shape come from the firework item.
args:
- locations (LOCATION, up to 9 values): explosion places. Default: empty → nothing.
- item (ITEM): a `minecraft:firework_rocket` item with explosions. Any other item → nothing.
notes:
- A rocket is spawned and blows up 1 tick later, so the explosion can damage entities very close to it.
example: {"action":"GAME_PLAY_FIREWORK_EXPLOSION","args":{"locations":{"loc":[0,70,0,0,0]},"item":{"item":"minecraft:firework_rocket","components":"{\"minecraft:fireworks\":{explosions:[{shape:\"star\",colors:[I;65280],has_twinkle:1b}]}}"}}}

### GAME_PLAY_PARTICLE_EFFECT
kind: action | category: GAME_SPECIAL_EFFECTS | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target) | hidden in menu
Shows particles at every given location to players within 40 blocks.
args:
- locations (LOCATION, up to 18 values): where to show the particles. Required.
- particle (PARTICLE): the particle. Required.
- count (NUMBER): particles per location, whole number 1–10. Default: 1.
- speed (NUMBER): particle speed, whole number 0–10. Default: 0.
- x (NUMBER): random spread, 0–20. Default: 0.
- y (NUMBER): accepted but ignored (the spread of `x` is used).
- z (NUMBER): accepted but ignored (the spread of `x` is used).
notes:
- The spread of `x` is used on all three axes.
- `speed` is a whole number: 0.5 becomes 0.
example: {"action":"GAME_PLAY_PARTICLE_EFFECT","args":{"locations":{"loc":[0.5,65,0.5,0,0]},"particle":{"particle":"minecraft:flame"},"count":10,"x":0.5}}

### GAME_CREATE_PARTICLE_LINE
kind: action | category: GAME_SPECIAL_EFFECTS | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target) | hidden in menu
Draws a line of particles between two locations, shown to players within 40 blocks.
args:
- particle (PARTICLE): the particle. Required.
- start_location (LOCATION): start of the line. Required.
- end_location (LOCATION): end of the line. Required.
notes:
- One particle every 0.5 block, at most 50 blocks long; a longer line stops after 50 blocks.
- The first particle is drawn at the middle of the line, then from 1 block after the start to the end.
example: {"action":"GAME_CREATE_PARTICLE_LINE","args":{"particle":{"particle":"minecraft:end_rod"},"start_location":{"loc":[0,65,0,0,0]},"end_location":{"loc":[10,65,0,0,0]}}}

### GAME_CREATE_HOLO
kind: action | category: GAME_SPECIAL_EFFECTS | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Creates a hologram (floating text lines) or updates the hologram with the same name.
args:
- loc (LOCATION): where the hologram is. Required.
- name (TEXT): the hologram id. Required. Placeholders are filled, so `%player%` in the name makes one hologram per player.
- texts (TEXT, up to 27 values): one line per value. `\n` inside a text also starts a new line. Default: empty → the name itself is shown as the only line.
notes:
- If a hologram with this name exists, its text is replaced and it moves to `loc`. Use this to update a hologram.
- At most 64 lines. The number of holograms per plot is limited; over the limit a new hologram is not created.
- A hologram is not an entity: entity selections and conditions do not see it.
- It stays until GAME_REMOVE_HOLO or until the code stops (the world leaves play mode or unloads). A code restart keeps it.
example: {"action":"GAME_CREATE_HOLO","args":{"loc":{"loc":[0.5,67,0.5,0,0]},"name":"top","texts":["§6§lTop players","§7Coins: %var(coins)%"]}}

### GAME_REMOVE_HOLO
kind: action | category: GAME_SPECIAL_EFFECTS | targets: the plot world (runs once per selected target)
Removes holograms by name.
args:
- names (TEXT, up to 18 values): ids of the holograms (the `name` used in GAME_CREATE_HOLO). Unknown names are ignored.
example: {"action":"GAME_REMOVE_HOLO","args":{"names":"top"}}

### GAME_CREATE_SCOREBOARD
kind: action | category: GAME_SCOREBOARD_MANIPULATION | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Creates a sidebar scoreboard with an id. Nobody sees it yet: show it to players with `PLAYER_SHOW_SCOREBOARD`.
args:
- name (TEXT): the scoreboard id. Required. Placeholders are filled, so `%player%` makes one scoreboard per player.
- display_name (TEXT): title shown on top of the sidebar. Hex colors like `&#ff8800` work. Default: the id.
notes:
- If a scoreboard with this id exists, nothing happens (the title is not changed). So it is safe to run it in `PLAYER_JOIN`.
- A player sees one code scoreboard at a time.
- It stays until GAME_REMOVE_SCOREBOARD or until the code stops (the world leaves play mode or unloads). A code restart keeps it.
example: {"action":"GAME_CREATE_SCOREBOARD","args":{"name":"main","display_name":"§e§lMY GAME"}}

### GAME_REMOVE_SCOREBOARD
kind: action | category: GAME_SCOREBOARD_MANIPULATION | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Deletes a scoreboard and hides it from every player who sees it.
args:
- name (TEXT): the scoreboard id. Unknown id → nothing.
example: {"action":"GAME_REMOVE_SCOREBOARD","args":{"name":"main"}}

### GAME_SET_SCOREBOARD_SCORE
kind: action | category: GAME_SCOREBOARD_MANIPULATION | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Adds a line to a scoreboard or changes its score. Lines are sorted by score, the highest on top.
args:
- name (TEXT): the scoreboard id. Unknown id → nothing.
- tag (TEXT): the line text. It is also the line id: the same text changes the same line. Required.
- score (NUMBER): the score (the red number), whole number. Default: 0.
notes:
- The shown text is cut to 40 characters.
- At most 256 lines; a new line over that removes the oldest line.
- Players who see the scoreboard get the change at once.
- To change the text of a line, remove the old line (GAME_REMOVE_SCOREBOARD_SCORE) and add a new one.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_SCOREBOARD_SCORE","args":{"name":"main","tag":"§fKills: §a%var(kills)%","score":3}}

### GAME_REMOVE_SCOREBOARD_SCORE
kind: action | category: GAME_SCOREBOARD_MANIPULATION | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Removes lines from a scoreboard, by text or by score.
args:
- name (TEXT): the scoreboard id. Unknown id → nothing.
- tag (TEXT): text of the line to remove. If set, `score` is ignored.
- score (NUMBER): without `tag`: removes EVERY line with this score. Default: 0.
example: {"action":"GAME_REMOVE_SCOREBOARD_SCORE","args":{"name":"main","score":3}}

### GAME_SET_SCOREBOARD_DISPLAY_NAME
kind: action | category: GAME_SCOREBOARD_MANIPULATION | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Changes the title of an existing scoreboard.
args:
- name (TEXT): the scoreboard id. Required; unknown id → nothing.
- display_name (TEXT): the new title. Hex colors like `&#ff8800` work. Required; empty → nothing.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_SCOREBOARD_DISPLAY_NAME","args":{"name":"main","display_name":"§c§lROUND 2"}}

### GAME_WEATHER
kind: action | category: GAME_WORLD_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once, with the first selected target)
Sets the weather of the plot world and how long it lasts.
args:
- duration (NUMBER): how long the weather lasts before it changes by itself, whole number, in the unit of the `state` marker. Default: 1000000.
markers:
- weather: 0 = clear (default), 1 = rain, 2 = thunderstorm
- state: unit of `duration`: 0 = ticks (default), 1 = seconds, 2 = minutes
notes:
- The weather changes by itself after the duration only if the weather cycle game rule is on (GAME_SET_GAME_RULE_VALUE, rule 8).
example: {"action":"GAME_WEATHER","args":{"duration":5},"switches":{"weather":1,"state":2}}

### GAME_TIME
kind: action | category: GAME_WORLD_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once, with the first selected target)
Sets the time of day of the plot world.
args:
- time (NUMBER): the time, whole number, in the unit of the `state` marker. Default: 1200.
markers:
- state: 0 = "hours": value × 1000 ticks (0 = sunrise, 6 = noon, 12 = sunset, 18 = midnight) (default), 1 = ticks (0–24000: 0 = sunrise, 6000 = noon, 13000 = night, 18000 = midnight)
notes:
- Only the time of day changes; values above one day wrap around (24000 ticks = 1 day).
- To keep the time fixed, turn off the day cycle (GAME_SET_GAME_RULE_VALUE, rule 6, value `false`).
example: {"action":"GAME_TIME","args":{"time":18000},"switches":{"state":1}}

### GAME_SET_GAME_RULE_VALUE
kind: action | category: GAME_WORLD_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once, with the first selected target)
Sets a game rule of the plot world.
args:
- value (TEXT): `true` or `false` (anything else counts as `false`); for rule 7 a whole number. Default: `true`.
markers:
- rule: 0 = fire spreads (`true` = on, `false` = off) (default), 1 = mobs change blocks (mob griefing), 2 = mobs drop loot, 3 = blocks drop items, 4 = entities drop items (minecarts, item frames…), 5 = natural health regeneration, 6 = day/night cycle, 7 = random tick speed (number 0–30), 8 = weather cycle
notes:
- Rule 7: a value that is not a number or not in 0–30 → nothing.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_GAME_RULE_VALUE","args":{"value":"false"},"switches":{"rule":6}}

### GAME_SET_WORLD_DIFFICULTY
kind: action | category: GAME_WORLD_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once, even with an empty selection)
Sets the difficulty of the plot world.
markers:
- state: 0 = peaceful (default), 1 = easy, 2 = normal, 3 = hard
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_WORLD_DIFFICULTY","switches":{"state":3}}

### GAME_SET_PVP_PARAMS
kind: action | category: GAME_WORLD_MANIPULATION | targets: the plot world (runs once, with the first selected target)
Changes the PvP settings of the plot world: knockback, hit delays and critical damage.
args:
- horizontal (NUMBER): horizontal knockback, 0–2. Server default about 0.4.
- vertical (NUMBER): vertical knockback, 0–2. Default about 0.4.
- vertical_limit (NUMBER): limit of vertical knockback, 0–2. Default about 0.4.
- extra_horizontal (NUMBER): extra horizontal knockback when the attacker sprints, 0–2. Default about 0.5.
- extra_vertical (NUMBER): extra vertical knockback when the attacker sprints, 0–2. Default about 0.1.
- player_hit_delay (NUMBER): ticks a player cannot be hurt again after a hit, whole number 1–100. Default about 18.
- mob_hit_delay (NUMBER): the same for mobs, 1–100. Default about 16.
- crit_multiplier (NUMBER): damage multiplier of critical hits, 0–10. Default about 1.5.
notes:
- Arguments you leave out keep the server default value (not the value set before).
- If no argument is set, the world goes back to the server defaults.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_PVP_PARAMS","args":{"horizontal":0.6,"player_hit_delay":10}}

### GAME_CREATE_BOSSBAR
kind: action | category: GAME_WORLD_MANIPULATION | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Creates a boss bar with an id. Nobody sees it yet: show it to players with `PLAYER_SHOW_BOSSBAR`.
args:
- name (TEXT): the bossbar id. Required. Placeholders are filled, so `%player%` makes one bossbar per player.
- title (TEXT): text shown above the bar. Default: empty.
markers:
- state: color: 0 = red (default), 1 = blue, 2 = yellow, 3 = green, 4 = pink, 5 = white, 6 = purple
- state1: style: 0 = solid bar (default), 1 = 6 segments, 2 = 10 segments, 3 = 12 segments, 4 = 20 segments
notes:
- If a bossbar with this id exists, nothing happens (title, color and style are not changed). So it is safe to run it in `PLAYER_JOIN`.
- A new bar is full (progress 100).
- A player sees one code bossbar at a time.
- It stays until GAME_REMOVE_BOSSBAR or until the code stops (the world leaves play mode or unloads). A code restart keeps it.
example: {"action":"GAME_CREATE_BOSSBAR","args":{"name":"timer","title":"§eTime left"},"switches":{"state":2,"state1":2}}

### GAME_REMOVE_BOSSBAR
kind: action | category: GAME_WORLD_MANIPULATION | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Deletes a bossbar and hides it from every player who sees it.
args:
- name (TEXT): the bossbar id. Unknown id → nothing.
example: {"action":"GAME_REMOVE_BOSSBAR","args":{"name":"timer"}}

### GAME_SET_BOSSBAR_TITLE
kind: action | category: GAME_WORLD_MANIPULATION | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Changes the title of an existing bossbar.
args:
- name (TEXT): the bossbar id. Unknown id → nothing.
- title (TEXT): the new title.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_BOSSBAR_TITLE","args":{"name":"timer","title":"§eTime left: §f%var(time)%"}}

### GAME_SET_BOSSBAR_COLOR
kind: action | category: GAME_WORLD_MANIPULATION | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Changes the color of an existing bossbar.
args:
- name (TEXT): the bossbar id. Unknown id → nothing.
markers:
- state: 0 = red (default), 1 = blue, 2 = yellow, 3 = green, 4 = pink, 5 = white, 6 = purple
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_BOSSBAR_COLOR","args":{"name":"timer"},"switches":{"state":0}}

### GAME_SET_BOSSBAR_STYLE
kind: action | category: GAME_WORLD_MANIPULATION | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Changes the style (segments) of an existing bossbar.
args:
- name (TEXT): the bossbar id. Unknown id → nothing.
markers:
- state: 0 = solid bar (default), 1 = 6 segments, 2 = 10 segments, 3 = 12 segments, 4 = 20 segments
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_BOSSBAR_STYLE","args":{"name":"timer"},"switches":{"state":4}}

### GAME_SET_BOSSBAR_PROGRESS
kind: action | category: GAME_WORLD_MANIPULATION | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Sets how full an existing bossbar is.
args:
- name (TEXT): the bossbar id. Unknown id → nothing.
- progress (NUMBER): fill in percent, 0–100 (values outside are clamped). Required.
example: {"action":"GAME_SET_BOSSBAR_PROGRESS","args":{"name":"timer","progress":75}}

## Variable actions

Rules that apply to every action in this section:
- An argument of type VARIABLE is an output. Its value MUST be a variable reference: `{"var":"score"}`. A text or a number there is rejected by the validator. LIST outputs (`output_list`) also take a variable reference.
- `"save": true` keeps the variable after the plot restarts. `"save": false` is a temporary game variable.
- The variable name can contain placeholders, for example `"name":"kills_%selected_uuid%"` gives every player their own variable. Color codes are removed from names.
- If the output variable does not exist, it is created. Writing replaces the old value and its type.
- The action runs once for every entity in the current selection (by default the event player). With 3 selected players, VARIABLE_INCREMENT adds 3 times. With an empty selection nothing happens.
- Reading a variable that does not exist gives nothing: a NUMBER argument then uses its default, a TEXT argument counts as not given.
- A NUMBER argument also accepts a text that holds a number, for example `"5"`. Arguments read as whole numbers cut the decimals (5.9 → 5) and need a whole-number text ("5", not "5.5").
- Numbers are stored as decimals. In text they are shown without trailing zeros and without exponent: 5 → `5`, 2.50 → `2.5`, 1000000 → `1000000`. The only exception is VARIABLE_SET with several values.
- A result that is not a finite number (NaN or infinity, for example the square root of -1) is not written: the variable keeps its old value. The same is true for a vector or location with such a coordinate.
- Texts are cut to 1024 characters when they are stored.
- Every LOCATION argument is moved inside the plot before use (X/Z into the plot borders, Y into the world height).
- An error inside an action (for example an index out of range) is reported in chat to the plot. The variable is not changed and the code goes on with the next block.
- Writing variables too fast hits a plot limit and stops the program.
- Very large items in a saved variable keep only the item type and amount.

### VARIABLE_SET
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_GENERAL | targets: none
Sets a variable. With one value, the variable gets that value with its own type (number, text, location, item, list and so on). With several values, they are joined into one text.
args:
- values (ANY, up to 27 values): the new value. Placeholders in texts are replaced. Values that resolve to nothing (for example a missing variable) are skipped. Default: empty → the variable becomes an empty text.
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
markers:
- separator: used only with several values: 0 = join with no separator (default), 1 = join with a space, 2 = join with a new line
notes:
- The argument keys are exactly `values` and `variable`.
- With several values, numbers are written in raw form: 5 becomes `5.0`. Locations and items become long technical text. To build a text from parts, use VARIABLE_COMBINE_TEXTS instead (it writes 5 as `5`).
- With several values the separator is also added after the last value (separator 1 leaves a space at the end).
- The joined text is cut at about 1024 characters.
- Setting a variable to a list or dictionary does not copy it: both variables then point to the same collection.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET","args":{"values":0,"variable":{"var":"score"}}}

### VARIABLE_SET_RAND_VALUE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_GENERAL | targets: none
Sets a variable to one random value from the list. The chosen value keeps its type.
args:
- values (ANY, up to 27 values): values to choose from. Values that resolve to nothing are skipped. Default: empty → the variable gets an empty value.
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
notes:
- The argument keys are exactly `values` and `variable`.
- Every value has the same chance.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_RAND_VALUE","args":{"values":["red","blue","green"],"variable":{"var":"team"}}}

### VARIABLE_GET_VARIABLE_NAMES
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_GENERAL | targets: none
Writes the names of all variables of the plot into a list variable.
args:
- output_list (LIST): output list variable. Required. Its old content is replaced.
notes:
- Includes saved and temporary variables, and list variables.
- Counts toward the plot element limit; if not enough is left, nothing happens.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_VARIABLE_NAMES","args":{"output_list":{"var":"all_vars"}}}

### VARIABLE_GET_RESULT_OF_RAY_TRACE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_GENERAL | targets: none
Casts a ray from a location in the direction of its yaw/pitch and writes what it hit into variables.
args:
- start (LOCATION): start point; its yaw and pitch give the direction. Required.
- width (NUMBER): thickness of the ray for hitting entities, in blocks. Default: 0. Clamped to 0–8.
- length (NUMBER): ray length in blocks. Default: 10. Maximum 256. 0 or less → nothing happens.
- entities (LIST): only entities whose name or UUID is in this list can be hit. Default: not given → any entity.
- block (VARIABLE): output: location of the hit block (its corner).
- face (VARIABLE): output: hit side of the block as a text: `UP`, `DOWN`, `NORTH`, `SOUTH`, `EAST` or `WEST`.
- uuid (VARIABLE): output: UUID of the hit entity, as a text.
- hit (VARIABLE): output: exact location of the hit point.
- blocks (BLOCK, up to 18 values): only these block types can be hit; other blocks are passed through. Use item values. Default: empty → every block.
markers:
- collision: 0 = blocks and entities (default), 1 = only blocks, 2 = only entities
- ignorePassable: 0 = passable blocks (grass, flowers) stop the ray (default), 1 = the ray goes through passable blocks
- ignoreFluids: 0 = water and lava stop the ray (default), 1 = the ray goes through fluids
notes:
- All outputs are optional. An output that has nothing (no hit, or no block / no entity hit) gets an empty value.
- Entities whose hitbox contains the start point (for example the player at their own eye location) are not hit.
- Only entities inside the plot can be hit.
- If `entities` is a variable that does not exist, the list is empty and no entity can be hit.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_RESULT_OF_RAY_TRACE","args":{"start":{"val":"CURRENT_EYE_LOCATION","sel":"CURRENT"},"length":50,"block":{"var":"ray_block"},"uuid":{"var":"ray_target"},"hit":{"var":"ray_hit"}},"switches":{"collision":0,"ignorePassable":1,"ignoreFluids":1}}

### VARIABLE_SET_ITEM
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_GENERAL | targets: none
Saves an item into a variable.
args:
- item (ITEM): the item. Default: empty → the variable gets an empty value.
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_ITEM","args":{"item":{"item":"minecraft:diamond_sword"},"variable":{"var":"weapon"}}}

### VARIABLE_GET_VALUE_FROM_ITEM
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Reads the name, durability or amount of an item into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item to read. Required; empty → nothing happens.
markers:
- state: 0 = name, as text (default), 1 = durability, as number, 2 = amount, as number
notes:
- Name: the custom name with its color codes if the item has one, otherwise the normal item name.
- Durability is the damage taken: 0 = new, higher = more worn. For items without durability it is the old 1.12 data value (for example a wool color).
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_VALUE_FROM_ITEM","args":{"variable":{"var":"amount"},"item":{"var":"held"}},"switches":{"state":2}}

### VARIABLE_SET_ITEM_TYPE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Changes the type (material) of an item and keeps its other data. Writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item to change. Default: empty → a new item of the type is made, amount 1.
- text (TEXT): new type, for example `diamond`, `minecraft:diamond` or `DIAMOND`. Required.
notes:
- An unknown type or a type that is not an item (for example `water`) → nothing happens.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_ITEM_TYPE","args":{"variable":{"var":"item"},"item":{"var":"item"},"text":"minecraft:golden_apple"}}

### VARIABLE_GET_ITEM_TYPE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Writes the type (material) of an item as a text, for example `DIAMOND_SWORD`.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item. Default: empty → `AIR`.
notes:
- The text is upper case without `minecraft:`.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_ITEM_TYPE","args":{"variable":{"var":"type"},"item":{"var":"held"}}}

### VARIABLE_GET_ITEM_LORE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Writes the lore lines (description) of an item into a list variable, one text per line.
args:
- output_list (LIST): output list variable. Required. Its old content is replaced.
- item (ITEM): the item. Required; empty → nothing happens.
notes:
- Lines keep their color codes (`§`).
- An item without lore gives an empty list.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_ITEM_LORE","args":{"output_list":{"var":"lore"},"item":{"var":"held"}}}

### VARIABLE_SET_ITEM_AMOUNT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Changes the amount of an item and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item. Required; empty → nothing happens.
- amount (NUMBER): new amount, whole number. Default: 1. Clamped to 0–6400.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_ITEM_AMOUNT","args":{"variable":{"var":"item"},"item":{"item":"minecraft:arrow"},"amount":16}}

### VARIABLE_SET_ITEM_DURABILITY
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Changes the durability of an item and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item. Required; empty → nothing happens.
- durability (NUMBER): damage taken, whole number: 0 = new, higher = more worn. Default: 0. Clamped to 0–32767.
notes:
- For items without durability, the number is used as an old 1.12 data value and changes the item variant, for example white wool with 14 becomes red wool. If there is no such variant, the item stays the same.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_ITEM_DURABILITY","args":{"variable":{"var":"sword"},"item":{"item":"minecraft:iron_sword"},"durability":100}}

### VARIABLE_SET_ITEM_LORE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Replaces one lore line of an item and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item. Required; empty → nothing happens.
- lore (TEXT): the new line. A new line character (`\n`) splits it into several lines that are inserted after it. Default: a single space.
- line (NUMBER): line number, starting at 1. Default: 1. Clamped to 1–256.
notes:
- If the lore is shorter than `line`, empty lines are added first.
- The lore holds at most 256 lines.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_ITEM_LORE","args":{"variable":{"var":"item"},"item":{"var":"item"},"lore":"§7Level: 5","line":2}}

### VARIABLE_SET_ITEM_NAME
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Sets the custom name of an item and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item. Required; empty → nothing happens.
- name (TEXT): the new name. Use `§` color codes. Default: a single space.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_ITEM_NAME","args":{"variable":{"var":"item"},"item":{"item":"minecraft:diamond_sword"},"name":"§bFrost Blade"}}

### VARIABLE_ADD_ITEM_LORE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Adds lines to the end of the lore of an item and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item. Required; empty → nothing happens.
- lore (TEXT): text to add. A new line character (`\n`) splits it into several lines. Default: one empty line.
notes:
- The lore holds at most 256 lines; extra lines are dropped.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_ADD_ITEM_LORE","args":{"variable":{"var":"item"},"item":{"var":"item"},"lore":"§7Right click to use"}}

### VARIABLE_REMOVE_ITEM_LORE_LINE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Removes one lore line from an item and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- out (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item. Required; empty → nothing happens.
- index (NUMBER): line number, starting at 1. Default: 1.
notes:
- If the item has no lore or the line does not exist, nothing happens (the output variable is not written).
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_REMOVE_ITEM_LORE_LINE","args":{"out":{"var":"item"},"item":{"var":"item"},"index":1}}

### VARIABLE_GET_BOOK_PAGE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Writes the text of one page of a book into a variable.
args:
- var (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- book (ITEM): a book and quill or a written book. Required.
- page (NUMBER): page number, starting at 1. Default: 0 (invalid, so always set it).
notes:
- Other items do nothing. A written book with no data does nothing.
- A page number that does not exist causes an action error.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_BOOK_PAGE","args":{"var":{"var":"page_text"},"book":{"var":"book"},"page":1}}

### VARIABLE_SET_ITEM_FLAGS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Hides or shows parts of the item tooltip and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item. Required; empty → nothing happens.
markers:
- enchantment: enchantments: 0 = hide (default), 1 = no change, 2 = show
- attributes: attributes (attack damage and so on): 0 = hide (default), 1 = no change, 2 = show
- unbreakable: the "Unbreakable" line: 0 = hide (default), 1 = no change, 2 = show
- potion_effects: extra tooltip (potion effects and other extra info): 0 = hide (default), 1 = no change, 2 = show
- destroys: "Can break" list: 0 = hide (default), 1 = no change, 2 = show
- placed_on: "Can be placed on" list: 0 = hide (default), 1 = no change, 2 = show
notes:
- Every marker defaults to 0 = hide. Set a marker to 1 for every part you do not want to change.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_ITEM_FLAGS","args":{"variable":{"var":"item"},"item":{"var":"item"}},"switches":{"enchantment":0,"attributes":0,"unbreakable":1,"potion_effects":1,"destroys":1,"placed_on":1}}

### VARIABLE_SET_BOOK_PAGE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Replaces the text of one existing page of a book and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- var (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- book (ITEM): a book and quill or a written book. Required.
- text (TEXT): new page text. Default: a single space.
- page (NUMBER): page number, starting at 1. Default: 0 (invalid, so always set it).
notes:
- The page must already exist; this action cannot add pages. A page number that does not exist causes an action error.
- Other items do nothing. A written book with no data does nothing.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_BOOK_PAGE","args":{"var":{"var":"book"},"book":{"var":"book"},"text":"Chapter 1","page":1}}

### VARIABLE_ADD_ENCHANTMENT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Adds an enchantment to an item and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item. Required; empty → nothing happens.
- enchant (TEXT): enchantment id, for example `sharpness` or `minecraft:sharpness`. Old names (like `DAMAGE_ALL`) are also accepted. Required.
- level (NUMBER): level, whole number. Default: 1.
notes:
- With level 0 or less the item also gets the enchantment glint.
- An unknown enchantment → nothing happens.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_ADD_ENCHANTMENT","args":{"variable":{"var":"sword"},"item":{"var":"sword"},"enchant":"minecraft:sharpness","level":5}}

### VARIABLE_REMOVE_ENCHANTMENT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Removes an enchantment from an item and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item. Required; empty → nothing happens.
- enchant (TEXT): enchantment id, for example `sharpness`. Required.
notes:
- An unknown enchantment → nothing happens.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_REMOVE_ENCHANTMENT","args":{"variable":{"var":"sword"},"item":{"var":"sword"},"enchant":"sharpness"}}

### VARIABLE_GET_COLOR_OF_ITEM
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Writes the color of leather armor, a potion or a map as a hex text, for example `#FF0000`.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item. Required; empty → nothing happens.
notes:
- The text is `#` and 6 upper-case hex digits.
- Other items, and potions or maps without a color, give `#FFFFFF`.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_COLOR_OF_ITEM","args":{"variable":{"var":"color"},"item":{"var":"armor"}}}

### VARIABLE_SET_COLOR_OF_ITEM
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Sets the color of leather armor, a potion or a map and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item. Required; empty → nothing happens.
- color (TEXT): hex color with 6 digits, for example `#FF8800`, `FF8800` or `0xFF8800`. Required.
notes:
- A color with fewer than 6 hex digits, or with letters that are not hex, causes an action error.
- Other items are written into the variable unchanged.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_COLOR_OF_ITEM","args":{"variable":{"var":"armor"},"item":{"item":"minecraft:leather_chestplate"},"color":"#FF0000"}}

### VARIABLE_SET_UNBREAKABLE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Makes an item unbreakable or breakable and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item. Required; empty → nothing happens.
markers:
- state: 0 = unbreakable (default), 1 = breakable
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_UNBREAKABLE","args":{"variable":{"var":"pickaxe"},"item":{"item":"minecraft:iron_pickaxe"}},"switches":{"state":0}}

### VARIABLE_GET_HEAD_OF_PLAYER
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Makes a player head item with a skin and writes it into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- text (TEXT): player name or UUID (state 0), or a skin texture value in base64 (state 1). Required.
markers:
- state: 0 = text is a player name or UUID (default), 1 = text is a skin texture value
notes:
- With state 0, an online player gives their skin at once.
- For an offline player the skin is loaded in the background. Until it is loaded, the head gets the default skin. Run the action again later to get the real skin.
- An invalid texture value → nothing happens.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_HEAD_OF_PLAYER","args":{"variable":{"var":"head"},"text":"%player%"},"switches":{"state":0}}

### VARIABLE_CREATE_CUSTOM_TAG
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Stores a custom value (tag) inside an item and writes the result into a variable. Use it to mark items, for example with an id or a price.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item. Required; empty → nothing happens.
- key (TEXT): tag name. Required.
- value (ANY): tag value. Required.
- path (TEXT): optional folder of the tag: names split by `/`, for example `shop/prices`. At most 5 parts. Default: empty → top level.
markers:
- type_of_value: 0 = store as text (default), 1 = store as number
notes:
- With type 0, a number value is stored in raw form: 5 becomes the text `5.0`.
- With type 1, a value that is not a number is not stored (the item is still written).
- A path with more than 5 parts → nothing happens.
- Read the tag with VARIABLE_GET_CUSTOM_TAG.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_CREATE_CUSTOM_TAG","args":{"variable":{"var":"item"},"item":{"var":"item"},"key":"price","value":100,"path":"shop"},"switches":{"type_of_value":1}}

### VARIABLE_GET_CUSTOM_TAG
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Reads a custom tag from an item (made with VARIABLE_CREATE_CUSTOM_TAG) into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item. Required; empty → nothing happens.
- key (TEXT): tag name. Default: empty → the whole tag folder is written as one text.
- path (TEXT): folder of the tag, names split by `/`. Default: empty → top level.
- default (ANY): value to write if the tag does not exist. Default: empty text.
notes:
- Number tags are written as numbers, text tags as texts.
- Use the same `path` as when the tag was created.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_CUSTOM_TAG","args":{"variable":{"var":"price"},"item":{"var":"item"},"key":"price","path":"shop","default":0}}

### VARIABLE_DELETE_CUSTOM_TAG
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_ITEM | targets: none
Removes a custom tag from an item and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- item (ITEM): the item. Required; empty → nothing happens.
- key (TEXT): tag name. Required.
- path (TEXT): folder of the tag, names split by `/`. Default: empty → top level.
notes:
- If the item has no custom tags or the path does not exist, nothing happens (the output variable is not written).
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_DELETE_CUSTOM_TAG","args":{"variable":{"var":"item"},"item":{"var":"item"},"key":"price","path":"shop"}}

### VARIABLE_COMBINE_TEXTS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Joins several values into one text and writes it into a variable. Best way to build a text from parts.
args:
- texts (TEXT, up to 27 values): parts, in order. Numbers, variables and game values are turned into text (5 → `5`). Default: empty → empty text.
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
markers:
- separator: 0 = join with no separator (default), 1 = join with a space, 2 = join with a new line
notes:
- The separator goes only between parts.
- The result is cut at 1024 characters.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_COMBINE_TEXTS","args":{"texts":["Score:",{"var":"score"}],"variable":{"var":"line"}},"switches":{"separator":1}}

### VARIABLE_PARSE_NUMBER
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Turns a text like `42` or `-3.5` into a number and writes it into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- text (TEXT): the text. Required.
notes:
- The whole text must look like `-12`, `12` or `12.5`: optional minus, digits, optional dot and digits. Spaces, a plus sign, a comma, `.5` or `1e3` do not work.
- If the text is not a number, the variable is not changed.
- A missing `text` causes an action error.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_PARSE_NUMBER","args":{"variable":{"var":"amount"},"text":{"val":"EVENT_CHAT_MESSAGE"}}}

### VARIABLE_REPEAT_TEXT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Repeats a text several times and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- text (TEXT): text to repeat. Required; empty → nothing happens.
- number (NUMBER): how many times, whole number. Default: 0 → empty text. A value below 0 or above 200 becomes 1.
notes:
- The result is cut at 1024 characters.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_REPEAT_TEXT","args":{"variable":{"var":"bar"},"text":"■","number":10}}

### VARIABLE_CUT_TEXT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Cuts a part out of a text, or keeps only a part, by character positions.
args:
- text (TEXT): the text. Required.
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- start (NUMBER): start position, counted from 0, included. Default: 0.
- end (NUMBER): end position, counted from 0, not included. Default: 1.
markers:
- mode: 0 = remove the characters from start to end, keep the rest (default), 1 = keep only the characters from start to end
notes:
- Example with `Hello`, start 1, end 3: mode 0 gives `Hlo`, mode 1 gives `el`.
- In mode 1 an `end` past the text length is reduced to the length.
- A position below 0, past the text length (mode 0), or start larger than end causes an action error.
- Color codes count as characters.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_CUT_TEXT","args":{"text":"Hello world","variable":{"var":"word"},"start":0,"end":5},"switches":{"mode":1}}

### VARIABLE_SPLIT_TEXT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Splits a text into parts by a separator and writes the parts into a list variable.
args:
- output_list (LIST): output list variable. Required. Its old content is replaced.
- input_text (TEXT): the text to split. Required.
- input_splitter (TEXT): the separator. It is plain text, not a regex. Default: a space.
notes:
- Empty parts at the end are dropped: `a,b,,` gives `a`, `b`.
- An empty separator splits the text into single characters.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SPLIT_TEXT","args":{"output_list":{"var":"words"},"input_text":"red,green,blue","input_splitter":","}}

### VARIABLE_GET_TEXT_LENGTH
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Writes the number of characters of a text into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- text (TEXT): the text. Required.
notes:
- Color codes count as characters (`§a` is 2). Some emoji count as 2.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_TEXT_LENGTH","args":{"variable":{"var":"len"},"text":{"var":"name"}}}

### VARIABLE_GET_SYMBOL_BY_INDEX
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Writes one character of a text into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- text (TEXT): the text. Required.
- index (NUMBER): position, counted from 0 (0 = first character). Default: 0.
notes:
- A position below 0 or past the end causes an action error.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_SYMBOL_BY_INDEX","args":{"variable":{"var":"first"},"text":"Hello","index":0}}

### VARIABLE_GET_INDEX_OF_SUBTEXT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Finds a smaller text inside a text and writes its position (counted from 0) into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- text (TEXT): the text to search in. Required.
- subtext (TEXT): the text to find. Required.
- index (NUMBER): position to start the search from. Default: 0.
markers:
- separator: 0 = first match: search forward from `index` (default), 1 = last match: search backward from `index`
notes:
- Not found → -1.
- The search is case sensitive.
- With separator 1 the search goes backward starting at `index`, so the default 0 only finds a match at position 0. Set `index` to a large number (for example 1024) to find the last match.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_INDEX_OF_SUBTEXT","args":{"variable":{"var":"pos"},"text":"a-b-c","subtext":"-","index":0},"switches":{"separator":0}}

### VARIABLE_REPLACE_TEXT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Replaces every match of a text with another text and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- text (TEXT): the text to change. Required.
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- replace_text (TEXT): what to find. Plain text, not a regex. Default: a single space.
- text_replace (TEXT): what to put instead. Default: empty → matches are removed.
markers:
- case: 0 = case sensitive (default), 1 = ignore case
- colors: 0 = keep colors (default), 1 = remove color codes from both texts first; the result has no colors
notes:
- All matches are replaced.
- Color removal deletes `§` or `&` together with the next character.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_REPLACE_TEXT","args":{"text":{"var":"msg"},"variable":{"var":"msg"},"replace_text":"bad","text_replace":"***"},"switches":{"case":1,"colors":0}}

### VARIABLE_REPLACE_TEXT_BY_REGEX
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Replaces matches of a regular expression and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- text (TEXT): the text to change. Required.
- regex (TEXT): regular expression, Java syntax. Required.
- replace (TEXT): replacement. `$1` inserts group 1, `$0` the whole match; write `\$` for a real dollar sign and `\\` for a backslash. Required (use an empty text to delete matches).
markers:
- replaces: 0 = replace all matches (default), 1 = replace only the first match
- ignore: ignore case: 0 = off (default), 1 = on
- multiline: `^` and `$` match at every line: 0 = off (default), 1 = on
- literal: the regex is plain text (special characters have no meaning): 0 = off (default), 1 = on
- unix: only `\n` ends a line: 0 = off (default), 1 = on
- comments: spaces and `#` comments are allowed in the regex: 0 = off (default), 1 = on
- dotall: `.` also matches a new line: 0 = off (default), 1 = on
- canon_eq: characters that look the same in Unicode count as equal: 0 = off (default), 1 = on
notes:
- An invalid regex → nothing happens.
- The work stops after 100 ms; then the result can be cut.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_REPLACE_TEXT_BY_REGEX","args":{"variable":{"var":"digits"},"text":"a1b22c333","regex":"[^0-9]","replace":""},"switches":{"replaces":0}}

### VARIABLE_GET_GROUP_OF_REGEX
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Finds a regular expression in a text and writes one group of the match into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- text (TEXT): the text to search. Required.
- regex (TEXT): regular expression, Java syntax. Required.
- group (NUMBER): group number: 0 = the whole match, 1 = the first `( )` group, and so on. Default: 0. Below 0 → nothing happens.
notes:
- If the regex matches several times, the LAST match is used.
- No match, a group number larger than the number of groups, or an invalid regex → empty text.
- The work stops after 100 ms.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_GROUP_OF_REGEX","args":{"variable":{"var":"num"},"text":"Level 12","regex":"Level (\\d+)","group":1}}

### VARIABLE_SET_TEXT_CASE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Changes the letter case of a text and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- text (TEXT): the text. Required.
markers:
- separator: 0 = UPPER CASE (default), 1 = lower case, 2 = swap case of every letter
notes:
- Upper case also changes color codes like `§a` to `§A`.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_TEXT_CASE","args":{"variable":{"var":"cmd"},"text":{"var":"cmd"}},"switches":{"separator":1}}

### VARIABLE_CLEAR_COLORS_FROM_TEXT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Removes color codes from a text and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- text (TEXT): the text. Required.
notes:
- Every `§` or `&` is removed together with the character after it, so `Tom & Jerry` becomes `Tom Jerry`.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_CLEAR_COLORS_FROM_TEXT","args":{"variable":{"var":"plain"},"text":"§aGreen §lbold"}}

### VARIABLE_GET_CHAR_OF_NUMBER
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Writes the character with the given Unicode code into a variable, for example 65 → `A`.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- number (NUMBER): Unicode code point, whole number. Default: 0.
notes:
- A code below 0 or above 1114111 causes an action error.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_CHAR_OF_NUMBER","args":{"variable":{"var":"heart"},"number":10084}}

### VARIABLE_GET_NUMBER_OF_CHAR
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Writes the Unicode code of the first character of a text into a variable, for example `A` → 65.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- text (TEXT): the text; only the first character is used. Required.
notes:
- An empty text causes an action error.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_NUMBER_OF_CHAR","args":{"variable":{"var":"code"},"text":"A"}}

### VARIABLE_CREATE_HEX_FROM_RGB
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Makes a hex color text like `#FF8800` from red, green and blue.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- red (NUMBER): red 0–255, whole number. Default: 1.
- green (NUMBER): green 0–255, whole number. Default: 1.
- blue (NUMBER): blue 0–255, whole number. Default: 1.
notes:
- Values outside 0–255 are clamped. The result is `#` and 6 upper-case hex digits.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_CREATE_HEX_FROM_RGB","args":{"variable":{"var":"color"},"red":255,"green":136,"blue":0}}

### VARIABLE_CREATE_HEX_FROM_HSB
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Makes a hex color text like `#FF8800` from hue, saturation and brightness (HSB / HSV).
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- hue (NUMBER): hue in degrees, whole number (0 = red, 120 = green, 240 = blue). Default: 1. Wraps around at 360.
- sat (NUMBER): saturation in percent 0–100, whole number. Default: 1. Clamped.
- bright (NUMBER): brightness in percent 0–100, whole number. Default: 1. Clamped.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_CREATE_HEX_FROM_HSB","args":{"variable":{"var":"color"},"hue":120,"sat":100,"bright":100}}

### VARIABLE_CREATE_HEX_FROM_HSL
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Makes a hex color text like `#FF8800` from hue, saturation and lightness (HSL).
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- hue (NUMBER): hue in degrees, whole number (0 = red, 120 = green, 240 = blue). Default: 1. Wraps around at 360.
- sat (NUMBER): saturation in percent 0–100, whole number. Default: 1. Clamped.
- light (NUMBER): lightness in percent 0–100, whole number (50 = pure color). Default: 1. Clamped.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_CREATE_HEX_FROM_HSL","args":{"variable":{"var":"color"},"hue":0,"sat":100,"light":50}}

### VARIABLE_SET_HOVER_TO_TEXT_COMPONENT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT_COMPONENT | targets: none
Adds a hover text (shown when the mouse is over the message) to a text component and writes it into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- component (COMPONENT): the text component, or a variable holding one. Required; a plain text value does not work.
- text (TEXT): the hover text. Use `§` color codes; `\n` makes a new line. Required.
notes:
- Show the result with PLAYER_SEND_TEXT_COMPONENT.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_HOVER_TO_TEXT_COMPONENT","args":{"variable":{"var":"msg"},"component":{"comp":"{\"text\":\"[Info]\",\"color\":\"aqua\"}"},"text":"§7Click to read the rules"}}

### VARIABLE_SET_CLICK_EVENT_TO_TEXT_COMPONENT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT_COMPONENT | targets: none
Adds a click action to a text component and writes it into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- component (COMPONENT): the text component, or a variable holding one. Required; a plain text value does not work.
- text (TEXT): the text to put into the chat box (separator 0) or the web address (separator 1). Required.
markers:
