- separator: 0 = put the text into the player's chat box (default), 1 = open a web link
notes:
- Color codes and control characters are removed from `text`.
- For a link, `http://` is added if the text does not start with `http`. An invalid link → nothing happens.
- Running a command on click is not possible; with separator 0 the text is only suggested.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_CLICK_EVENT_TO_TEXT_COMPONENT","args":{"variable":{"var":"msg"},"component":{"var":"msg"},"text":"https://mineland.net"},"switches":{"separator":1}}

### VARIABLE_COMBINE_TEXT_COMPONENTS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT_COMPONENT | targets: none
Joins several text components (and plain texts) into one text component and writes it into a variable.
args:
- components (COMPONENT, up to 27 values): parts, in order. Plain texts, numbers and variables are added as plain text parts. Default: empty → an empty component.
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
markers:
- separator: 0 = join with no separator (default), 1 = join with a space, 2 = join with a new line
notes:
- Each part keeps its own colors, hover and click actions.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_COMBINE_TEXT_COMPONENTS","args":{"components":["§7Visit",{"var":"link"}],"variable":{"var":"msg"}},"switches":{"separator":1}}

### VARIABLE_SUM
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Adds numbers and writes the sum into a variable: result = number + numbers[0] + numbers[1] + ...
args:
- numbers (NUMBER, up to 27 values): numbers to add. A value that is not a number counts as 0.
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- number (NUMBER): start value. Default: 0.
notes:
- To add to a variable, put the same variable into `number` (or use VARIABLE_INCREMENT).
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SUM","args":{"numbers":[{"var":"a"},{"var":"b"}],"variable":{"var":"total"},"number":0}}

### VARIABLE_DIFFERENCE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Subtracts numbers and writes the result into a variable: result = number - numbers[0] - numbers[1] - ...
args:
- numbers (NUMBER, up to 27 values): numbers to subtract. A value that is not a number counts as 0.
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- number (NUMBER): the value to subtract from. Default: 0.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_DIFFERENCE","args":{"numbers":10,"variable":{"var":"hp_left"},"number":{"var":"hp"}}}

### VARIABLE_PRODUCT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Multiplies numbers and writes the result into a variable: result = number × numbers[0] × numbers[1] × ...
args:
- numbers (NUMBER, up to 27 values): numbers to multiply by. A value that is not a number counts as 0. Required; empty → nothing happens.
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- number (NUMBER): start value. Default: 0, so the result is always 0 unless you set it.
notes:
- Always set `number`.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_PRODUCT","args":{"numbers":2,"variable":{"var":"price"},"number":{"var":"price"}}}

### VARIABLE_QUOTIENT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Divides numbers and writes the result into a variable: result = number ÷ numbers[0] ÷ numbers[1] ÷ ...
args:
- numbers (NUMBER, up to 27 values): divisors. A value that is not a number counts as 0.
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- number (NUMBER): the value to divide. Default: 0.
notes:
- The result is a decimal: 7 ÷ 2 = 3.5. Use VARIABLE_ROUND for a whole number.
- Division by 0 does not fail: the variable gets a random number between -32768 and 32768. Check the divisor before dividing.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_QUOTIENT","args":{"numbers":20,"variable":{"var":"seconds"},"number":{"var":"ticks"}}}

### VARIABLE_REMAINDER
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes the remainder of a division (modulo) into a variable: result = dividend % divisor.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- dividend (NUMBER): the number to divide, whole number. Default: 0.
- divisor (NUMBER): the number to divide by, whole number. Default: 0.
notes:
- Both numbers are cut to whole numbers first (7.9 → 7).
- The sign follows the dividend: -7 % 3 = -1.
- Divisor 0 → the variable is not changed.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_REMAINDER","args":{"variable":{"var":"is_odd"},"dividend":{"var":"n"},"divisor":2}}

### VARIABLE_INCREMENT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Adds a number to a variable (variable = variable + number).
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): the variable to change. Required.
- number (NUMBER): how much to add. Default: 1.
notes:
- A variable that does not exist counts as 0, so it becomes `number`.
- A variable that holds a text with a number (like `"5"`) is read as that number.
- A variable that holds something else (a text that is not a number, a location, a list) causes an action error.
- Runs once per selected entity: with 3 selected players it adds 3 times.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_INCREMENT","args":{"variable":{"var":"kills_%selected_uuid%","save":true},"number":1}}

### VARIABLE_DECREMENT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Subtracts a number from a variable (variable = variable - number).
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): the variable to change. Required.
- number (NUMBER): how much to subtract. Default: 1.
notes:
- A variable that does not exist counts as 0, so it becomes -`number`.
- A variable that holds a text with a number (like `"5"`) is read as that number.
- A variable that holds something else causes an action error.
- Runs once per selected entity.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_DECREMENT","args":{"variable":{"var":"lives"},"number":1}}

### VARIABLE_RANDOM_NUMBER
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes a random whole number between min and max (both included) into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- min (NUMBER): lowest value, whole number. Default: 0.
- max (NUMBER): highest value, whole number. Default: 0.
notes:
- Only whole numbers: decimals of min and max are cut. For a random decimal, divide the result.
- If min is larger than max, they are swapped.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_RANDOM_NUMBER","args":{"variable":{"var":"roll"},"min":1,"max":6}}

### VARIABLE_ROUND
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Rounds a number and writes it into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- number (NUMBER): the number to round. Default: 0.
- scale (NUMBER): digits after the dot to keep, whole number. Default: 0 → whole number. Clamped to -10…10. A negative scale rounds to tens (-1), hundreds (-2) and so on.
markers:
- state: 0 = normal rounding, half up (2.5 → 3, -2.5 → -2) (default), 1 = round down (floor: 2.7 → 2, -2.3 → -3), 2 = round up (ceil: 2.1 → 3)
notes:
- Example: 3.14159 with scale 2 gives 3.14.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_ROUND","args":{"variable":{"var":"avg"},"number":{"var":"avg"},"scale":1},"switches":{"state":0}}

### VARIABLE_ABSOLUTE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes the absolute value of a number (without minus sign) into a variable: -5 → 5.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- num (NUMBER): the number. Default: 0.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_ABSOLUTE","args":{"variable":{"var":"dist"},"num":{"var":"dx"}}}

### VARIABLE_MIN
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes the smaller of two numbers into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- num1 (NUMBER): first number. Default: 0.
- num2 (NUMBER): second number. Default: 0.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_MIN","args":{"variable":{"var":"hp"},"num1":{"var":"hp"},"num2":20}}

### VARIABLE_MAX
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes the larger of two numbers into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- num1 (NUMBER): first number. Default: 0.
- num2 (NUMBER): second number. Default: 0.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_MAX","args":{"variable":{"var":"coins"},"num1":{"var":"coins"},"num2":0}}

### VARIABLE_POW
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Raises a number to a power and writes it into a variable: result = num ^ degree.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- num (NUMBER): the base. Default: 0.
- degree (NUMBER): the power. Decimals work (0.5 = square root). Default: 0.
notes:
- A result that is not a real number (for example (-8) ^ 0.5) does not change the variable.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_POW","args":{"variable":{"var":"area"},"num":{"var":"side"},"degree":2}}

### VARIABLE_COS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes the cosine of an angle into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- num (NUMBER): angle in radians (not degrees). Default: 0.
notes:
- Convert degrees with VARIABLE_RADIANS first.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_COS","args":{"variable":{"var":"c"},"num":{"var":"angle_rad"}}}

### VARIABLE_SIN
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes the sine of an angle into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- num (NUMBER): angle in radians (not degrees). Default: 0.
notes:
- Convert degrees with VARIABLE_RADIANS first.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SIN","args":{"variable":{"var":"s"},"num":{"var":"angle_rad"}}}

### VARIABLE_TAN
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes the tangent of an angle into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- num (NUMBER): angle in radians (not degrees). Default: 0.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_TAN","args":{"variable":{"var":"t"},"num":0.5}}

### VARIABLE_ASIN
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes the arcsine of a number into a variable. The result is an angle in radians.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- num (NUMBER): a number from -1 to 1. Default: 0.
notes:
- A number outside -1…1 does not change the variable.
- Convert the result to degrees with VARIABLE_DEGREES.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_ASIN","args":{"variable":{"var":"a"},"num":0.5}}

### VARIABLE_ACOS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes the arccosine of a number into a variable. The result is an angle in radians.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- num (NUMBER): a number from -1 to 1. Default: 0.
notes:
- A number outside -1…1 does not change the variable.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_ACOS","args":{"variable":{"var":"a"},"num":0.5}}

### VARIABLE_ATAN
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes the arctangent of a number into a variable. The result is an angle in radians, from -π/2 to π/2.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- num (NUMBER): the number. Default: 0.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_ATAN","args":{"variable":{"var":"a"},"num":1}}

### VARIABLE_DEGREES
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Converts an angle from radians to degrees and writes it into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- num (NUMBER): angle in radians. Default: 0.
notes:
- The result can have a tiny floating-point error. Round it if you compare it.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_DEGREES","args":{"variable":{"var":"deg"},"num":{"var":"rad"}}}

### VARIABLE_LOG
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes the natural logarithm (base e) of a number into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- num (NUMBER): the number, above 0. Default: 0.
notes:
- 0 or a negative number does not change the variable.
- For another base use VARIABLE_COMMON_LOG.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_LOG","args":{"variable":{"var":"ln"},"num":10}}

### VARIABLE_COMMON_LOG
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes the logarithm of a number with a chosen base into a variable: result = log_base(argument).
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- argument (NUMBER): the number, whole number. Default: 0. A negative value becomes 1.
- base (NUMBER): the base, whole number. Default: 0. A negative value becomes 1.
notes:
- Both numbers are cut to whole numbers.
- Argument 0 or base 1 → the variable is not changed. Base 0 → 0.
- The result can have a tiny error: log of 1000 with base 10 gives 2.9999999999999996. Round it if you need a whole number.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_COMMON_LOG","args":{"variable":{"var":"digits"},"argument":100,"base":10}}

### VARIABLE_SQRT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes the square root of a number into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- num (NUMBER): the number, 0 or more. Default: 0.
notes:
- A negative number does not change the variable.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SQRT","args":{"variable":{"var":"root"},"num":16}}

### VARIABLE_EXP
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes e (2.718…) raised to the power of a number into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- num (NUMBER): the power. Default: 0.
notes:
- A too large power (more than about 709) does not change the variable.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_EXP","args":{"variable":{"var":"e"},"num":1}}

### VARIABLE_RADIANS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Converts an angle from degrees to radians and writes it into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- num (NUMBER): angle in degrees. Default: 0.
notes:
- Use it before VARIABLE_SIN, VARIABLE_COS and VARIABLE_TAN.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_RADIANS","args":{"variable":{"var":"rad"},"num":90}}

### VARIABLE_PERLINE_NOISE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes a smooth Perlin noise value for a location into a variable. Close locations give close values. Useful for terrain and random patterns.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the point (x, y, z are used). Required.
- seed (NUMBER): noise seed, whole number. The same seed always gives the same noise. Default: the seed of the plot world.
- frequency (NUMBER): scale; smaller = smoother, larger features. Default: 0.01. 0 or less becomes 1.
- octaves (NUMBER): number of noise layers, whole number 1–9. Default: 4. Outside 1–9 becomes 1.
- lacunarity (NUMBER): frequency change per layer. Default: 2. 0 or less becomes 1.
- persistence (NUMBER): strength change per layer. Default: 0.5. 0 or less becomes 1.
markers:
- normalized: 0 = normalized result, about -1…1 (default), 1 = raw sum of the layers
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_PERLINE_NOISE","args":{"variable":{"var":"height"},"location":{"val":"CURRENT_LOCATION","sel":"CURRENT"},"seed":12345,"frequency":0.05,"octaves":4},"switches":{"normalized":0}}

### VARIABLE_SIMPLEX_NOISE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes a smooth simplex noise value for a location into a variable. Works like VARIABLE_PERLINE_NOISE with a different noise type.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the point (x, y, z are used). Required.
- seed (NUMBER): noise seed, whole number. Default: the seed of the plot world.
- frequency (NUMBER): scale; smaller = smoother. Default: 0.01. 0 or less becomes 1.
- octaves (NUMBER): number of noise layers, whole number 1–9. Default: 4. Outside 1–9 → nothing happens.
- lacunarity (NUMBER): frequency change per layer. Default: 2. 0 or less becomes 2.
- persistence (NUMBER): strength change per layer. Default: 0.5. 0 or less becomes 1.
markers:
- separator: 0 = normalized result (default), 1 = raw sum of the layers
notes:
- The marker has no effect: the result is always normalized (about -1…1).
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SIMPLEX_NOISE","args":{"variable":{"var":"n"},"location":{"val":"CURRENT_LOCATION","sel":"CURRENT"},"seed":42,"frequency":0.02},"switches":{"separator":0}}

### VARIABLE_VORONOI_NOISE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_NUMBER | targets: none
Writes a Voronoi (cell) noise value for a location into a variable. The space is split into cells; each cell gets its own value.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the point (x, y, z are used). Required.
- noiseKey (NUMBER): noise seed, whole number. Default: the seed of the plot world. A value above 2147483647 becomes 1.
- noiseFrequency (NUMBER): cell density; smaller = bigger cells. Default: 0.01.
- displacement (NUMBER): how much the value differs between cells. Default: 4.
markers:
- separator: 0 = add the distance to the cell center to the value (default), 1 = only the cell value
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_VORONOI_NOISE","args":{"variable":{"var":"cell"},"location":{"val":"CURRENT_LOCATION","sel":"CURRENT"},"noiseKey":7,"noiseFrequency":0.05,"displacement":1},"switches":{"separator":1}}

### VARIABLE_SET_TIMESTAMP
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_GENERAL | targets: none | hidden in menu
Writes the current date and time as a text, like `2026-11-19 15:04:05.123`.
args:
- output (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
notes:
- The time is the server local time. For your own format use VARIABLE_SET_UNIX_TIME and VARIABLE_SET_TIMESTAMP_FROM_UNIX_TIME.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_TIMESTAMP","args":{"output":{"var":"now"}}}

### VARIABLE_SET_UNIX_TIME
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_GENERAL | targets: none | hidden in menu
Writes the current time as a number of MILLISECONDS since 1 January 1970 (UTC).
args:
- output (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
notes:
- The value is in milliseconds, not seconds: divide by 1000 for seconds.
- Use it to measure time: save it, later read it again and subtract.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_UNIX_TIME","args":{"output":{"var":"start_ms"}}}

### VARIABLE_SET_TIMESTAMP_FROM_UNIX_TIME
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_TEXT | targets: none
Turns a time in milliseconds since 1970 into a date/time text and writes it into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- unix (NUMBER): time in MILLISECONDS since 1 January 1970, whole number. Default: 0.
- format (TEXT): your own pattern, used only with separator 4. Java date pattern letters: `yyyy` year, `MM` month, `dd` day, `HH` hour, `mm` minute, `ss` second, `MMMM` month name. Default: `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss`.
- lang (TEXT): language for month and day names, for example `en` or `ru`. Default: `en`.
markers:
- separator: 0 = `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss` (default), 1 = `yyyy-MM-dd`, 2 = `HH:mm:ss`, 3 = `dd:HH:mm:ss`, 4 = the pattern from `format`
notes:
- The time zone is always Moscow (UTC+3).
- This formats a date, not a duration: `dd` is the day of the month. Because of the time zone, a duration like 90000 ms shows as `03:01:30` with format 2, not `00:01:30`. To show a duration, compute minutes and seconds with math actions.
- An invalid pattern causes an action error.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_TIMESTAMP_FROM_UNIX_TIME","args":{"variable":{"var":"date"},"unix":{"var":"start_ms"},"format":"dd MMMM yyyy, HH:mm","lang":"en"},"switches":{"separator":4}}

### VARIABLE_GET_VALUE_FROM_LOC
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_LOCATION | targets: none
Writes one coordinate or the rotation of a location into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the location. Default: not given → the location stored in `variable` itself is used; if it holds no location, nothing happens.
markers:
- state: 0 = X (default), 1 = Y, 2 = Z, 3 = yaw (horizontal rotation), 4 = pitch (vertical rotation)
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_VALUE_FROM_LOC","args":{"variable":{"var":"y"},"location":{"val":"CURRENT_LOCATION","sel":"CURRENT"}},"switches":{"state":1}}

### VARIABLE_GET_COORDINATES_OF_LOCATION
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_LOCATION | targets: none
Writes all parts of a location into separate variables: x, y, z, yaw and pitch.
args:
- location (LOCATION): the location. Required.
- x (VARIABLE): output for X. Optional.
- y (VARIABLE): output for Y. Optional.
- z (VARIABLE): output for Z. Optional.
- yaw (VARIABLE): output for yaw. Optional.
- pitch (VARIABLE): output for pitch. Optional.
notes:
- Only the outputs you give are written.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_COORDINATES_OF_LOCATION","args":{"location":{"val":"CURRENT_LOCATION","sel":"CURRENT"},"x":{"var":"px"},"y":{"var":"py"},"z":{"var":"pz"}}}

### VARIABLE_SET_LOCATION_VALUES
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_LOCATION | targets: none
Changes the coordinates and rotation of a location and writes the new location into a variable. Each part can be replaced or shifted.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the base location. Default: not given → the location stored in `variable` itself; if it holds no location, nothing happens.
- yaw (NUMBER): new yaw. Not given → yaw stays.
- pitch (NUMBER): new pitch. Not given → pitch stays.
- x (NUMBER): new X. Not given → X stays.
- y (NUMBER): new Y. Not given → Y stays.
- z (NUMBER): new Z. Not given → Z stays.
markers:
- yawState: 0 = set yaw to the value (=) (default), 1 = add the value to yaw (+=)
- pitchState: 0 = set pitch to the value (=) (default), 1 = add the value to pitch (+=)
- xState: 0 = set X to the value (=) (default), 1 = add the value to X (+=)
- yState: 0 = set Y to the value (=) (default), 1 = add the value to Y (+=)
- zState: 0 = set Z to the value (=) (default), 1 = add the value to Z (+=)
notes:
- To move a location 5 blocks up: give `y` = 5 and `yState` = 1.
- The stored yaw and pitch are rounded to 2 decimals.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_LOCATION_VALUES","args":{"variable":{"var":"spawn"},"location":{"var":"spawn"},"y":5},"switches":{"yState":1}}

### VARIABLE_LOCATIONS_DISTANCE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_LOCATION | targets: none
Writes the straight distance between two locations, in blocks, into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- loc1 (LOCATION): first location. Required.
- loc2 (LOCATION): second location. Required.
notes:
- The distance is 3D (X, Y and Z count). It is a decimal number.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_LOCATIONS_DISTANCE","args":{"variable":{"var":"dist"},"loc1":{"val":"CURRENT_LOCATION","sel":"CURRENT"},"loc2":{"loc":[0.5,64,0.5,0,0]}}}

### VARIABLE_ROUND_LOCATION
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_LOCATION | targets: none
Snaps a location to its block and writes it into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the location. Required.
markers:
- save_rotation: 0 = keep yaw and pitch (default), 1 = set yaw and pitch to 0
- type_of_coordinate: 0 = round X, Y and Z (default), 1 = round only X and Z, 2 = round only Y
- type_of_round: 0 = to the block center: whole number + 0.5 (default), 1 = to the block corner: whole number
notes:
- The coordinate is rounded down to its block first: 10.7 → 10.5 (center) or 10 (corner); -0.3 → -0.5 or -1.
- With center rounding, Y also gets + 0.5.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_ROUND_LOCATION","args":{"variable":{"var":"block_center"},"location":{"val":"CURRENT_LOCATION","sel":"CURRENT"}},"switches":{"save_rotation":1,"type_of_coordinate":1,"type_of_round":0}}

### VARIABLE_GET_DIRECTION_OF_LOCATION
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_LOCATION | targets: none
Writes the view direction of a location (from its yaw and pitch) as a vector of length 1 into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the location. Required.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_DIRECTION_OF_LOCATION","args":{"variable":{"var":"dir"},"location":{"val":"CURRENT_EYE_LOCATION","sel":"CURRENT"}}}

### VARIABLE_SET_DIRECTION_OF_LOCATION
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_LOCATION | targets: none
Sets the yaw and pitch of a location so it looks along a vector, and writes the location into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the location. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): the direction to look in. Required.
notes:
- The position does not change. Only the direction of the vector matters, not its length.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_DIRECTION_OF_LOCATION","args":{"variable":{"var":"loc"},"location":{"loc":[0.5,64,0.5,0,0]},"vector":{"vec":[1,0,0]}}}

### VARIABLE_TURN_LOCATION_TO_ANOTHER_LOCATION
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_LOCATION | targets: none
Turns a location so it looks at another location, and writes it into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the location to turn. Required.
- location2 (LOCATION): the point to look at. Required.
notes:
- The position of `location` does not change, only its yaw and pitch.
- If both locations are the same point, nothing happens.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_TURN_LOCATION_TO_ANOTHER_LOCATION","args":{"variable":{"var":"look"},"location":{"val":"CURRENT_LOCATION","sel":"CURRENT"},"location2":{"loc":[0,70,0,0,0]}}}

### VARIABLE_GET_BLOCK_FROM_LOCATION
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_LOCATION | targets: none
Writes the block at a location as an item into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- location (LOCATION): the block position. Required.
notes:
- The result is an item of the block type, amount 1. Block states (rotation and so on) are not kept.
- Air gives an air item. Water and lava give a bucket, fire gives flint and steel, portals give an ender pearl, frosted ice gives ice.
- Outside the plot territory nothing happens.
- To get the type name as text, use VARIABLE_GET_ITEM_TYPE on the result.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_BLOCK_FROM_LOCATION","args":{"variable":{"var":"block"},"location":{"loc":[10,63,10,0,0]}}}

### VARIABLE_GET_RANDOM_LOCATION_FROM_REGION
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_LOCATION | targets: none
Writes a random location inside the box between two corners into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- location (LOCATION): first corner. Its yaw and pitch are used for the result. Required.
- location2 (LOCATION): opposite corner. Required.
markers:
- state: 0 = round the coordinates to whole numbers (default), 1 = keep decimals
notes:
- Rounding goes to the nearest whole number (not to the block center). Add 0.5 with VARIABLE_SET_LOCATION_VALUES if you need the center.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_RANDOM_LOCATION_FROM_REGION","args":{"variable":{"var":"drop"},"location":{"loc":[0,64,0,0,0]},"location2":{"loc":[20,64,20,0,0]}},"switches":{"state":0}}

### VARIABLE_GET_CENTER_OF_REGION
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_LOCATION | targets: none
Writes the middle point between two locations into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- location (LOCATION): first location. Its yaw and pitch are used for the result. Required.
- location2 (LOCATION): second location. Required.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_CENTER_OF_REGION","args":{"variable":{"var":"center"},"location":{"loc":[0,64,0,0,0]},"location2":{"loc":[20,70,20,0,0]}}}

### VARIABLE_CREATE_VECTOR
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Makes a vector from x, y and z and writes it into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- x (NUMBER): X part. Default: 1.
- y (NUMBER): Y part (up). Default: 1.
- z (NUMBER): Z part. Default: 1.
notes:
- Missing parts are 1, not 0. Give all three.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_CREATE_VECTOR","args":{"variable":{"var":"up"},"x":0,"y":1,"z":0}}

### VARIABLE_CREATE_VECTOR_BETWEEN_LOCATIONS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Makes the vector from one location to another (location2 - location) and writes it into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- location (LOCATION): start point. Required.
- location2 (LOCATION): end point. Required.
notes:
- The length of the vector is the distance between the points.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_CREATE_VECTOR_BETWEEN_LOCATIONS","args":{"variable":{"var":"to_target"},"location":{"val":"CURRENT_LOCATION","sel":"CURRENT"},"location2":{"loc":[0,64,0,0,0]}}}

### VARIABLE_CREATE_VECTOR_WITH_BLOCK_FACE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Makes the vector of a block side name and writes it into a variable, for example `UP` → (0, 1, 0).
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- face (TEXT): side name: `UP`, `DOWN`, `NORTH` (-Z), `SOUTH` (+Z), `EAST` (+X), `WEST` (-X), `NORTH_EAST`, `NORTH_WEST`, `SOUTH_EAST`, `SOUTH_WEST`, `SELF` and similar. Case does not matter. Required.
notes:
- An unknown name or a missing `face` causes an action error.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_CREATE_VECTOR_WITH_BLOCK_FACE","args":{"variable":{"var":"normal"},"face":{"var":"ray_face"}}}

### VARIABLE_GET_COORDINATE_OF_VECTOR
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Writes one part (x, y or z) of a vector into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): the vector. Required.
markers:
- state: 0 = X (default), 1 = Y, 2 = Z
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_COORDINATE_OF_VECTOR","args":{"variable":{"var":"vy"},"vector":{"var":"dir"}},"switches":{"state":1}}

### VARIABLE_GET_COORDINATES_OF_VECTOR
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Writes the x, y and z parts of a vector into separate variables.
args:
- vector (VECTOR): the vector. Required.
- x (VARIABLE): output for X. Optional.
- y (VARIABLE): output for Y. Optional.
- z (VARIABLE): output for Z. Optional.
notes:
- Only the outputs you give are written.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_COORDINATES_OF_VECTOR","args":{"vector":{"var":"dir"},"x":{"var":"dx"},"y":{"var":"dy"},"z":{"var":"dz"}}}

### VARIABLE_SET_COORDINATE_OF_VECTOR
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Replaces one part (x, y or z) of a vector and writes the new vector into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): the vector. Required.
- value (NUMBER): new value of the part. Default: 1.
markers:
- state: 0 = X (default), 1 = Y, 2 = Z
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_COORDINATE_OF_VECTOR","args":{"variable":{"var":"dir"},"vector":{"var":"dir"},"value":0},"switches":{"state":1}}

### VARIABLE_GET_LENGTH_OF_VECTOR
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Writes the length of a vector into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): the vector. Required.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_LENGTH_OF_VECTOR","args":{"variable":{"var":"speed"},"vector":{"var":"velocity"}}}

### VARIABLE_SET_LENGTH_OF_VECTOR
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Changes the length of a vector and keeps its direction. Writes the new vector into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): the vector. Required.
- length (NUMBER): new length. Default: 1. A negative length flips the direction.
notes:
- A zero vector has no direction: the variable is not changed.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SET_LENGTH_OF_VECTOR","args":{"variable":{"var":"dir"},"vector":{"var":"dir"},"length":2}}

### VARIABLE_SUM_OF_VECTORS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Adds vectors and writes the result into a variable: result = vector + vectors[0] + vectors[1] + ...
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): the first vector. Required.
- vectors (VECTOR, up to 27 values): vectors to add. Values that are not vectors are skipped. Required; empty → nothing happens.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SUM_OF_VECTORS","args":{"variable":{"var":"v"},"vector":{"var":"v"},"vectors":[{"vec":[0,1,0]}]}}

### VARIABLE_DIFFERENCE_OF_VECTORS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Subtracts vectors and writes the result into a variable: result = vector - vectors[0] - vectors[1] - ...
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): the vector to subtract from. Required.
- vectors (VECTOR, up to 27 values): vectors to subtract. Values that are not vectors are skipped. Required; empty → nothing happens.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_DIFFERENCE_OF_VECTORS","args":{"variable":{"var":"v"},"vector":{"var":"a"},"vectors":[{"var":"b"}]}}

### VARIABLE_MULTIPLY_VECTOR_BY_NUMBER
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Multiplies every part of a vector by a number and writes the result into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): the vector. Required.
- number (NUMBER): the multiplier. Default: 1.
notes:
- A negative number flips the direction. To divide, multiply by 1/n (for example 0.5).
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_MULTIPLY_VECTOR_BY_NUMBER","args":{"variable":{"var":"push"},"vector":{"var":"dir"},"number":1.5}}

### VARIABLE_DIVIDE_VECTORS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Divides two vectors part by part and writes the result into a variable: (x1/x2, y1/y2, z1/z2).
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): the vector to divide. Required.
- vector2 (VECTOR): the divisor. Required.
notes:
- If any part of `vector2` is 0, the variable is not changed.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_DIVIDE_VECTORS","args":{"variable":{"var":"v"},"vector":{"vec":[4,6,8]},"vector2":{"vec":[2,2,2]}}}

### VARIABLE_SCALAR_PRODUCT_OF_VECTORS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Writes the dot product of two vectors (a number) into a variable: x1·x2 + y1·y2 + z1·z2.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): first vector. Required.
- vector2 (VECTOR): second vector. Required.
notes:
- For vectors of length 1: 1 = same direction, 0 = at a right angle, -1 = opposite.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_SCALAR_PRODUCT_OF_VECTORS","args":{"variable":{"var":"dot"},"vector":{"var":"look"},"vector2":{"var":"to_target"}}}

### VARIABLE_CROSS_PRODUCT_OF_VECTORS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Writes the cross product of two vectors into a variable. The result is at a right angle to both vectors.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): first vector. Required.
- vector2 (VECTOR): second vector. Required.
notes:
- The order matters: look × (0, 1, 0) points to the RIGHT of the look direction, (0, 1, 0) × look points to the LEFT. Looking south (0, 0, 1): look × (0, 1, 0) = (-1, 0, 0), west.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_CROSS_PRODUCT_OF_VECTORS","args":{"variable":{"var":"side"},"vector":{"var":"look"},"vector2":{"vec":[0,1,0]}}}

### VARIABLE_HADAMARD_PRODUCT_OF_VECTORS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Multiplies two vectors part by part and writes the result into a variable: (x1·x2, y1·y2, z1·z2).
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): first vector. Required.
- vector2 (VECTOR): second vector. Required.
notes:
- Useful to remove a part: multiply by (1, 0, 1) to drop Y.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_HADAMARD_PRODUCT_OF_VECTORS","args":{"variable":{"var":"flat"},"vector":{"var":"dir"},"vector2":{"vec":[1,0,1]}}}

### VARIABLE_ALIGN_VECTOR
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Turns a vector so it points along one axis (X, Y or Z) and writes it into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): the vector. Required.
markers:
- normalized: 0 = keep the original length (default), 1 = length 1
notes:
- The axis is the one with the largest value, compared WITH sign, not by size: (-5, 1, 0) gives a Y vector (0, 5.1, 0), not an X vector.
- If several parts are equal, X wins over Y, and Y wins over Z.
- The result keeps the sign of the chosen part: (-1, -2, -3) gives (-3.74, 0, 0).
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_ALIGN_VECTOR","args":{"variable":{"var":"axis"},"vector":{"var":"dir"}},"switches":{"normalized":1}}

### VARIABLE_GET_MID_POINT_BETWEEN_VECTORS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Writes the middle point of two vectors into a variable: (vector + vector2) / 2.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): first vector. Required.
- vector2 (VECTOR): second vector. Required.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_MID_POINT_BETWEEN_VECTORS","args":{"variable":{"var":"mid"},"vector":{"vec":[0,0,0]},"vector2":{"vec":[10,4,2]}}}

### VARIABLE_GET_ANGLE_BETWEEN_VECTORS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Writes the angle between two vectors into a variable, from 0 to 180 degrees.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): first vector. Required.
- vector2 (VECTOR): second vector. Required.
markers:
- state: 0 = degrees (default), 1 = radians
notes:
- In degrees the result can have a tiny error (for example 90.0000025). Round it if you compare it.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_ANGLE_BETWEEN_VECTORS","args":{"variable":{"var":"angle"},"vector":{"var":"look"},"vector2":{"var":"to_target"}},"switches":{"state":0}}

### VARIABLE_REFLECT_VECTOR
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Reflects a vector off a surface, like a ball bouncing off a wall, and writes it into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): the incoming vector. Required.
- vector2 (VECTOR): the surface normal (the direction the surface faces, for example (0, 1, 0) for a floor). Required.
- scale (NUMBER): the result is multiplied by this. Default: 1. Below 0.01 becomes 1.
notes:
- A zero `vector2` gives no result: the variable is not changed.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_REFLECT_VECTOR","args":{"variable":{"var":"bounce"},"vector":{"var":"velocity"},"vector2":{"vec":[0,1,0]},"scale":0.8}}

### VARIABLE_ROTATE_VECTOR_AROUND_ANOTHER_VECTOR
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Rotates a vector around another vector (the axis) by an angle and writes it into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): the vector to rotate. Required.
- vector2 (VECTOR): the rotation axis. Only its direction matters. Required.
- angle (NUMBER): rotation angle. Default: 1.
markers:
- state: unit of `angle`: 0 = degrees (default), 1 = radians
notes:
- The rotation follows the right-hand rule: counter-clockwise when you look from the tip of the axis toward its start.
- A zero axis gives no result: the variable is not changed.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_ROTATE_VECTOR_AROUND_ANOTHER_VECTOR","args":{"variable":{"var":"v"},"vector":{"var":"v"},"vector2":{"vec":[0,1,0]},"angle":15},"switches":{"state":0}}

### VARIABLE_ROTATE_VECTOR_AROUND_AXIS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Rotates a vector around the X, Y or Z axis by an angle and writes it into a variable.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): the vector to rotate. Required.
- angle (NUMBER): rotation angle. Default: 1.
markers:
- coordinate: axis: 0 = X (default), 1 = Y, 2 = Z
- state: unit of `angle`: 0 = degrees (default), 1 = radians
notes:
- The rotation follows the right-hand rule: counter-clockwise when you look from the positive end of the axis.
- To spin something around a player horizontally, use axis 1 (Y). A positive angle around Y turns east (+X) toward north (-Z): the opposite of a growing yaw. To turn a direction by yaw + 30, give -30.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_ROTATE_VECTOR_AROUND_AXIS","args":{"variable":{"var":"offset"},"vector":{"var":"offset"},"angle":10},"switches":{"coordinate":1,"state":0}}

### VARIABLE_GET_BLOCK_FACE_OF_VECTOR
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_VECTOR | targets: none
Writes the block side name that a vector points to into a variable, for example (0, 1, 0) → `UP`.
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): output variable. Required.
- vector (VECTOR): the vector. Required.
notes:
- A part counts only if it is above 0.3 or below -0.3 (the vector is not normalized first).
- Possible results: `UP`, `DOWN`, `NORTH`, `SOUTH`, `EAST`, `WEST`, `NORTH_EAST`, `NORTH_WEST`, `SOUTH_EAST`, `SOUTH_WEST`, `SELF`.
- `SELF` means no part counts, or the vector points up/down AND sideways at the same time.
example: {"action":"VARIABLE_GET_BLOCK_FACE_OF_VECTOR","args":{"variable":{"var":"facing"},"vector":{"var":"dir"}}}

## Collection actions

Rules for all LIST_*, DICT_* and SET_* actions:
- An argument that the action changes or writes into must be a variable: `{"var":"items"}`. A text value with the variable name also works. A literal `{"list":[...]}` there makes the action do nothing.
- An argument that is only read may be a variable or a literal (`{"list":[...]}`, `{"map":{...}}`, `{"set":[...]}`). A variable that does not exist reads as empty.
- Lists have their own name space: a list `x` and a normal variable `x` can both exist. Dicts and sets share the name space with normal variables: a dict or set action on a variable that holds something else replaces it with an empty dict or set.
- List and dict positions (indexes) start at 1.
- Two values are equal only when type and content are the same. Number `5` and text `"5"` are different. Text is case-sensitive.
- A list or dict put inside a list or dict is stored by reference, not copied. Changing the inner one changes it everywhere. Sets store copies.
- The size of a list, dict or set and its nesting are limited. A collection cannot contain itself.
- Very heavy work with collections does nothing until the world's budget refills.
- Like every action, it runs once per selected target. With an empty selection it does not run.

### LIST_CREATE_OR_CLEAR
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Clears the list variable and fills it with the given values in order. Creates the list if it does not exist.
args:
- list (LIST): the list variable to fill.
- values (ANY, up to 27 values): elements, in order. Default: empty → the list becomes empty.
notes:
- Text values get their placeholders replaced now. Empty slots and variables that do not exist are skipped.
example: {"action":"LIST_CREATE_OR_CLEAR","args":{"list":{"var":"colors"},"values":["red","green",3]}}

### LIST_GET_BY_INDEX
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes the element at position `index` into `output_var`.
args:
- list (LIST): the list. A variable or a literal list.
- index (NUMBER): position, starting at 1. Decimals are cut (2.7 → 2). Default: 0 (out of range).
- output_var (VARIABLE): where the element is written.
notes:
- Out of range (below 1 or above the size) writes the number 0.
- An inner list or dict is not copied: `output_var` then points to the same inner list, so changing it also changes the element.
example: {"action":"LIST_GET_BY_INDEX","args":{"list":{"var":"colors"},"index":1,"output_var":{"var":"first_color"}}}

### LIST_INSERT
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Inserts `value` at position `index`. Elements from that position move one place to the right.
args:
- list (LIST): the list variable to change.
- index (NUMBER): position, starting at 1. 1 or less inserts at the front; larger than the size inserts at the end. Default: 0 (front).
- value (ANY): the value to insert. Required; missing → nothing.
notes:
- Text is stored as written: placeholders in it are NOT replaced now. Use LIST_APPEND or a variable if you need the replaced text.
example: {"action":"LIST_INSERT","args":{"list":{"var":"queue"},"index":1,"value":{"var":"next_name"}}}

### LIST_REPLACE_BY_INDEX
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Replaces the element at position `index` with `value`.
args:
- list (LIST): the list variable to change.
- index (NUMBER): position, starting at 1. 1 or less replaces the first element. Default: 0 (first element).
- value (ANY): the new value. Required; missing → nothing.
notes:
- An index larger than the size gives an error and the list does not change. On an empty list it is always an error.
- Text is stored as written: placeholders in it are NOT replaced now.
example: {"action":"LIST_REPLACE_BY_INDEX","args":{"list":{"var":"scores","save":true},"index":2,"value":100}}

### LIST_REMOVE_BY_INDEX
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Removes the element at position `index`. Later elements move one place to the left.
args:
- list (LIST): the list variable to change.
- index (NUMBER): position, starting at 1. 1 or less removes the first element; larger than the size removes the last element. Default: 0 (first element).
notes:
- On an empty list nothing happens.
example: {"action":"LIST_REMOVE_BY_INDEX","args":{"list":{"var":"queue"},"index":1}}

### LIST_APPEND
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Adds the values to the end of the list, in order. Creates the list if it does not exist.
args:
- list (LIST): the list variable to change.
- values (ANY, up to 27 values): values to add. Default: empty → nothing is added.
notes:
- Text values get their placeholders replaced now. Empty slots and variables that do not exist are skipped.
example: {"action":"LIST_APPEND","args":{"list":{"var":"joined_players"},"values":"%player%"}}

### LIST_CONCAT
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Adds all elements of `appended_list` to the end of `main_list`. `appended_list` does not change.
args:
- main_list (LIST): the list variable to change.
- appended_list (LIST): the list to add. A variable or a literal list.
notes:
- If either list has 100000 or more elements, nothing happens.
- The size of `appended_list` counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"LIST_CONCAT","args":{"main_list":{"var":"all_items"},"appended_list":{"var":"new_items"}}}

### LIST_COPY
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Replaces the contents of `output_list` with the elements of `input_list`. The old elements of `output_list` are removed first.
args:
- input_list (LIST): the source list. A variable or a literal list.
- output_list (LIST): the list variable to write into.
notes:
- Inner lists and dicts are shared, not copied.
- If either list has 100000 or more elements, nothing happens. The size of `input_list` counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"LIST_COPY","args":{"input_list":{"var":"spawn_points","save":true},"output_list":{"var":"free_points"}}}

### LIST_SIZE
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes the number of elements of the list into `output`.
args:
- list (LIST): the list. A variable or a literal list. A list that does not exist has size 0.
- output (VARIABLE): where the number is written.
example: {"action":"LIST_SIZE","args":{"list":{"var":"queue"},"output":{"var":"queue_size"}}}

### LIST_SORT
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes the elements of `input_list` sorted into `output_list`. The old elements of `output_list` are removed. `input_list` does not change unless it is the same variable.
args:
- input_list (LIST): the list to sort. A variable or a literal list.
- output_list (LIST): the list variable to write into. May be the same variable as `input_list`.
markers:
- asc_sort: 0 = ascending, smallest first (default), 1 = descending, largest first
notes:
- Numbers are sorted by value. Texts are sorted by character code: case-sensitive (`B` before `a`) and `"10"` before `"9"`.
- The list must hold only numbers or only texts. Mixed types, locations, items or inner collections give an error and `output_list` does not change.
- If either list has 100000 or more elements, nothing happens. The size counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"LIST_SORT","args":{"input_list":{"var":"scores","save":true},"output_list":{"var":"scores_sorted"}},"switches":{"asc_sort":1}}

### LIST_TO_STRING
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes a text view of the list into `output`. Format: first line ``<Array `name` (size) >``, then one line ``#0 = `value` `` per element (counting from 0), last line ``</Array `name`>``.
args:
- list (LIST): the list. A variable or a literal list (then the name is empty).
- output (VARIABLE): where the text is written.
notes:
- Numbers are shown like `5.0`. Inner lists are shown as `[a, b]`, sets as `(a, b)`, dicts as `{k=v}`.
- The text is cut at 1024 characters. A list with 100000 or more elements gives nothing.
- For a short readable text, put the list variable into a TEXT value or `%var(name)%`: it prints as `[a, b, c]`.
example: {"action":"LIST_TO_STRING","args":{"list":{"var":"colors"},"output":{"var":"colors_text"}}}

### LIST_REMOVE
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Removes elements equal to `value` from the list.
args:
- list (LIST): the list variable to change.
- value (ANY): the value to remove. Text gets its placeholders replaced. Required; missing → nothing.
markers:
- remove_mode: 0 = remove the first match (default), 1 = remove the last match, 2 = remove all matches
notes:
- The list size counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"LIST_REMOVE","args":{"list":{"var":"joined_players"},"value":"%player%"},"switches":{"remove_mode":2}}

### LIST_INDEX_OF
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes the position (from 1) of `value` in the list into `output_var`. Writes 0 if the value is not in the list.
args:
- list (LIST): the list. A variable or a literal list.
- value (ANY): the value to find. Text gets its placeholders replaced. Required; missing → `output_var` does not change.
- output_var (VARIABLE): where the position is written.
markers:
- search_last: 0 = position of the first match (default), 1 = position of the last match
notes:
- The list size counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"LIST_INDEX_OF","args":{"list":{"var":"joined_players"},"value":"%player%","output_var":{"var":"join_place"}},"switches":{"search_last":0}}

### LIST_RANDOM
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes one random element of the list into `output_var`.
args:
- list (LIST): the list. A variable or a literal list.
- output_var (VARIABLE): where the element is written.
notes:
- If the list is empty, `output_var` does not change.
example: {"action":"LIST_RANDOM","args":{"list":{"list":["red","blue","green"]},"output_var":{"var":"random_team"}}}

### LIST_DISTINCT
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Removes repeated elements from the list. The first copy of each value stays; the order is kept.
args:
- list (LIST): the list variable to change.
notes:
- The list size counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"LIST_DISTINCT","args":{"list":{"var":"joined_players"}}}

### LIST_REVERSE
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Reverses the order of the elements of the list.
args:
- list (LIST): the list variable to change.
notes:
- The list size counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"LIST_REVERSE","args":{"list":{"var":"scores_sorted"}}}

### LIST_SHUFFLE
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Puts the elements of the list in a random order.
args:
- list (LIST): the list variable to change.
notes:
- The list size counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"LIST_SHUFFLE","args":{"list":{"var":"spawn_points"}}}

### LIST_SUBLIST
kind: action | category: LIST | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Keeps only the elements from `from_index` to `to_index` (both included), or removes exactly those elements.
args:
- list (LIST): the list variable to change.
- from_index (NUMBER): first position of the range, starting at 1. Default: 0 (invalid).
- to_index (NUMBER): last position of the range, included. Default: 0 (invalid).
markers:
- cut_range: 0 = keep only the range (default), 1 = remove the range from the list
notes:
- The range must be valid: 1 ≤ from_index ≤ to_index ≤ size. Otherwise nothing happens (no clamping).
- The list size counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"LIST_SUBLIST","args":{"list":{"var":"scores_sorted"},"from_index":1,"to_index":3},"switches":{"cut_range":0}}

### DICT_CREATE_OR_CLEAR
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_DICT | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Clears the dict variable and fills it with key → value pairs. Creates the dict if it does not exist.
args:
- dict (MAP): the dict variable to fill.
- keys (TEXT, up to 9 values): keys. Placeholders are replaced; numbers become text (`5` → `"5"`).
- values (ANY, up to 9 values): values. Text values get their placeholders replaced.
notes:
- Pairs are made by position: key 1 with value 1, key 2 with value 2, and so on. Extra keys or values without a partner are ignored.
- Empty slots and variables that do not exist are skipped, which shifts the pairing. Fill both lists without gaps.
- If a key is given twice, the last value wins.
example: {"action":"DICT_CREATE_OR_CLEAR","args":{"dict":{"var":"prices","save":true},"keys":["sword","bow"],"values":[50,80]}}

### DICT_PUT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_DICT | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes `value` into the dict under `key`. An existing key gets the new value and keeps its place in the order; a new key is added at the end.
args:
- dict (MAP): the dict variable to change. Created if it does not exist.
- key (TEXT): the key. Placeholders are replaced. Required; missing → nothing.
- value (ANY): the value. Text gets its placeholders replaced. Required; missing → nothing.
example: {"action":"DICT_PUT","args":{"dict":{"var":"kills","save":true},"key":"%player_uuid%","value":0}}

### DICT_GET_BY_KEY
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_DICT | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes the value stored under `key` into `output_var`.
args:
- dict (MAP): the dict. A variable or a literal dict.
- key (TEXT): the key. Exact match, case-sensitive.
- output_var (VARIABLE): where the value is written.
notes:
- If the key is not in the dict (or `key` is missing), the number 0 is written.
- An inner list or dict is not copied (see LIST_GET_BY_INDEX).
example: {"action":"DICT_GET_BY_KEY","args":{"dict":{"var":"prices","save":true},"key":"sword","output_var":{"var":"price"}}}

### DICT_REMOVE_BY_KEY
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_DICT | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Removes the pair with `key` from the dict. If there is no such key, nothing changes.
args:
- dict (MAP): the dict variable to change.
- key (TEXT): the key to remove.
example: {"action":"DICT_REMOVE_BY_KEY","args":{"dict":{"var":"kills","save":true},"key":"%player_uuid%"}}

### DICT_SIZE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_DICT | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes the number of pairs in the dict into `output`.
args:
- dict (MAP): the dict. A variable or a literal dict. A dict that does not exist has size 0.
- output (VARIABLE): where the number is written.
example: {"action":"DICT_SIZE","args":{"dict":{"var":"kills","save":true},"output":{"var":"players_counted"}}}

### DICT_KEYS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_DICT | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes all keys of the dict, as texts, into `output_list`. The old elements of `output_list` are removed first.
args:
- dict (MAP): the dict. A variable or a literal dict.
- output_list (LIST): the list variable to write into.
notes:
- Keys come in the order they were added.
- The dict size counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"DICT_KEYS","args":{"dict":{"var":"prices","save":true},"output_list":{"var":"shop_names"}}}

### DICT_VALUES
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_DICT | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes all values of the dict into `output_list`. The old elements of `output_list` are removed first.
args:
- dict (MAP): the dict. A variable or a literal dict.
- output_list (LIST): the list variable to write into.
notes:
- Values come in the order their keys were added.
- The dict size counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"DICT_VALUES","args":{"dict":{"var":"prices","save":true},"output_list":{"var":"shop_prices"}}}

### DICT_COPY
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_DICT | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Replaces the contents of `output_dict` with the pairs of `input_dict`. The old pairs of `output_dict` are removed first.
args:
- input_dict (MAP): the source dict. A variable or a literal dict.
- output_dict (MAP): the dict variable to write into.
notes:
- Inner lists and dicts are shared, not copied.
- If either dict has 100000 or more pairs, nothing happens. The size of `input_dict` counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"DICT_COPY","args":{"input_dict":{"var":"prices","save":true},"output_dict":{"var":"sale_prices"}}}

### DICT_MERGE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_DICT | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Adds all pairs of `merged_dict` to `main_dict`. When a key is in both, the value from `merged_dict` wins. `merged_dict` does not change.
args:
- main_dict (MAP): the dict variable to change.
- merged_dict (MAP): the dict to add. A variable or a literal dict.
notes:
- Existing keys keep their place in the order; new keys are added at the end.
- If either dict has 100000 or more pairs, nothing happens. The size of `merged_dict` counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"DICT_MERGE","args":{"main_dict":{"var":"prices","save":true},"merged_dict":{"map":{"shield":30}}}}

### DICT_TO_STRING
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_DICT | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes a text view of the dict into `output`. Format: first line ``<Dict `name` (size) >``, then one line ``#key = `value` `` per pair, last line ``</Dict `name`>``.
args:
- dict (MAP): the dict. A variable or a literal dict (then the name is empty).
- output (VARIABLE): where the text is written.
notes:
- Numbers are shown like `5.0`. The text is cut at 1024 characters. A dict with 100000 or more pairs gives nothing.
example: {"action":"DICT_TO_STRING","args":{"dict":{"var":"prices","save":true},"output":{"var":"prices_text"}}}

### DICT_FROM_LISTS
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_DICT | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Clears the dict and fills it with pairs made from two lists: element 1 of `keys_list` with element 1 of `values_list`, and so on.
args:
- dict (MAP): the dict variable to fill.
- keys_list (LIST): the keys. A variable or a literal list. Each element becomes text (`5` → `"5"`).
- values_list (LIST): the values. A variable or a literal list.
notes:
- Only as many pairs as the shorter list are made.
- If a key repeats, the last value wins.
- The number of pairs counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"DICT_FROM_LISTS","args":{"dict":{"var":"prices","save":true},"keys_list":{"var":"shop_names"},"values_list":{"var":"shop_prices"}}}

### DICT_KEY_BY_INDEX
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_DICT | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes the key of the pair at position `index` (in the order of adding, from 1) into `output_var`.
args:
- dict (MAP): the dict. A variable or a literal dict.
- index (NUMBER): position, starting at 1. Default: 0 (out of range).
- output_var (VARIABLE): where the key is written.
notes:
- Out of range writes the number 0.
- The index counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"DICT_KEY_BY_INDEX","args":{"dict":{"var":"prices","save":true},"index":1,"output_var":{"var":"first_key"}}}

### DICT_VALUE_BY_INDEX
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_DICT | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes the value of the pair at position `index` (in the order of adding, from 1) into `output_var`.
args:
- dict (MAP): the dict. A variable or a literal dict.
- index (NUMBER): position, starting at 1. Default: 0 (out of range).
- output_var (VARIABLE): where the value is written.
notes:
- Out of range writes the number 0.
- The index counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"DICT_VALUE_BY_INDEX","args":{"dict":{"var":"prices","save":true},"index":1,"output_var":{"var":"first_price"}}}

### DICT_KEYS_BY_VALUE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_DICT | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes every key whose value equals `value` into `output_list`, as texts. The old elements of `output_list` are removed first.
args:
- dict (MAP): the dict. A variable or a literal dict.
- value (ANY): the value to find. Text gets its placeholders replaced. Required; missing → `output_list` does not change.
- output_list (LIST): the list variable to write into.
notes:
- No match → `output_list` becomes empty.
- The dict size counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"DICT_KEYS_BY_VALUE","args":{"dict":{"var":"teams"},"value":"red","output_list":{"var":"red_players"}}}

### DICT_SORT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_DICT | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes the pairs of `input_dict` into `output_dict` in sorted order. The old pairs of `output_dict` are removed.
args:
- input_dict (MAP): the dict to sort. A variable or a literal dict.
- output_dict (MAP): the dict variable to write into. May be the same variable as `input_dict`.
markers:
- sort_by: 0 = sort by keys (default), 1 = sort by values
- asc_sort: 0 = ascending (default), 1 = descending
notes:
- By keys: keys that look like numbers are compared as numbers and come before other keys; other keys are compared as text (case-sensitive).
- By values: numbers first (by value), then texts, then other values (not ordered among themselves).
- Descending reverses the whole order, including these groups.
- If either dict has 100000 or more pairs, nothing happens. The size counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"DICT_SORT","args":{"input_dict":{"var":"kills","save":true},"output_dict":{"var":"kills_top"}},"switches":{"sort_by":1,"asc_sort":1}}

### SET_CREATE_OR_CLEAR
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_SETS | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Clears the set variable and fills it with the given values. Repeated values are stored once. Creates the set if it does not exist.
args:
- set (SET): the set variable to fill.
- values (ANY, up to 27 values): values. Default: empty → the set becomes empty.
notes:
- Text values get their placeholders replaced now. Empty slots and variables that do not exist are skipped.
- A set keeps the order of adding.
example: {"action":"SET_CREATE_OR_CLEAR","args":{"set":{"var":"visited"},"values":["spawn"]}}

### SET_ADD
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_SETS | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Adds the values to the set. Values already in the set are not added again. Creates the set if it does not exist.
args:
- set (SET): the set variable to change.
- values (ANY, up to 27 values): values to add. Default: empty → nothing is added.
notes:
- Text values get their placeholders replaced now.
example: {"action":"SET_ADD","args":{"set":{"var":"finished_players"},"values":"%player_uuid%"}}

### SET_REMOVE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_SETS | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Removes the values from the set. Values that are not in the set are skipped.
args:
- set (SET): the set variable to change.
- values (ANY, up to 27 values): values to remove. Text values get their placeholders replaced.
example: {"action":"SET_REMOVE","args":{"set":{"var":"finished_players"},"values":"%player_uuid%"}}

### SET_SIZE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_SETS | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes the number of values in the set into `output`.
args:
- set (SET): the set. A variable or a literal set. A set that does not exist has size 0.
- output (VARIABLE): where the number is written.
example: {"action":"SET_SIZE","args":{"set":{"var":"finished_players"},"output":{"var":"finished_count"}}}

### SET_COPY
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_SETS | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Replaces the contents of `output_set` with the values of `input_set`. The old values of `output_set` are removed first.
args:
- input_set (SET): the source set. A variable or a literal set.
- output_set (SET): the set variable to write into.
notes:
- Inner lists, dicts and sets are copied.
- If either set has 100000 or more values, nothing happens. The size of `input_set` counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"SET_COPY","args":{"input_set":{"var":"visited"},"output_set":{"var":"visited_backup"}}}

### SET_UNION
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_SETS | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Adds all values of `other_set` to `main_set`. `other_set` does not change.
args:
- main_set (SET): the set variable to change.
- other_set (SET): the second set. A variable or a literal set.
notes:
- If either set has 100000 or more values, nothing happens. The size of `other_set` counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"SET_UNION","args":{"main_set":{"var":"visited"},"other_set":{"set":["shop","arena"]}}}

### SET_INTERSECT
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_SETS | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Keeps in `main_set` only the values that are also in `other_set`. `other_set` does not change.
args:
- main_set (SET): the set variable to change.
- other_set (SET): the second set. A variable or a literal set.
notes:
- The size of `other_set` counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"SET_INTERSECT","args":{"main_set":{"var":"alive"},"other_set":{"var":"red_team"}}}

### SET_DIFFERENCE
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_SETS | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Removes from `main_set` every value that is in `other_set`. `other_set` does not change.
args:
- main_set (SET): the set variable to change.
- other_set (SET): the second set. A variable or a literal set.
notes:
- The size of `other_set` counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"SET_DIFFERENCE","args":{"main_set":{"var":"alive"},"other_set":{"var":"eliminated"}}}

### SET_TO_LIST
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_SETS | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes all values of the set into `output_list`, in the order of adding. The old elements of `output_list` are removed first.
args:
- set (SET): the set. A variable or a literal set.
- output_list (LIST): the list variable to write into.
notes:
- Inner lists, dicts and sets are copied.
- The set size counts toward the element budget.
example: {"action":"SET_TO_LIST","args":{"set":{"var":"finished_players"},"output_list":{"var":"finished_list"}}}

### SET_TO_STRING
kind: action | category: VARIABLE_SETS | targets: none (runs once per selected target)
Writes a text view of the set into `output`. Format: first line ``<Set `name` (size) >``, then one line ``#1 = `value` `` per value (counting from 1), last line ``</Set `name`>``.
args:
- set (SET): the set. A variable or a literal set (then the name is empty).
- output (VARIABLE): where the text is written.
notes:
- Numbers are shown like `5.0`. The text is cut at 1024 characters. A set with 100000 or more values gives nothing.
example: {"action":"SET_TO_STRING","args":{"set":{"var":"visited"},"output":{"var":"visited_text"}}}

## Conditions

Rules for all conditions:
- A condition is a bracket: `{"action":"IF_...","args":{...},"children":[ ... ]}`. `children` is required (it may be `[]`). The children run when the condition is TRUE.
- Each entry has a `Targets:` note. "all" = every target must pass. "any" = one passing target is enough. "first" = only the first target is checked. It also gives the result for an EMPTY selection (for example a loop with no players); this differs between conditions.
- `"not": true` flips the final result, after the targets are combined and after the empty-selection rule. Example: a condition that is TRUE on an empty selection is FALSE with `not`.
- `"elseIf": true` works the same for every condition: it is checked only when all conditions before it in the chain were FALSE (guide 5.4). `not` also works on an else-if.
- If the condition fails with an error (bad input), the result is FALSE (with `not`: TRUE) and the world gets an "error executing" message.
- `target`: IF_PLAYER, IF_GAME and IF_VARIABLE conditions use the player target list (default `SELECTION`). IF_ENTITY conditions (marked `entity condition`) use the entity target list (default `CURRENT`); in `PLAYER_*` events they test the event entity when the event has one.
- IF_VARIABLE conditions test values, not the targets. The targets only matter for placeholders and game values inside the arguments.
- Conditions that read the current event (event item, block, message, click) give FALSE in an event without that data and send everyone in the world an "incompatible event" chat warning. With `not` that becomes TRUE, so use them only in the listed events.

### IF_VARIABLE_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when `obj` is equal to at least one value in `objects`.
args:
- objects (ANY, up to 27 values): values to compare with. Text values get their placeholders replaced.
- obj (ANY): the value to check. Text written here is NOT read with placeholders (`%player%` stays as written). Put such text into `objects` or into a variable.
notes:
- Numbers are compared by value. Other values must have the same type and content: text is case-sensitive, number 5 and text "5" are different, items must match fully (also the amount).
- If `obj` is missing (for example a variable that does not exist), the result is TRUE only when `objects` is empty too.
- Targets: any (checked for each target until one passes). Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_EQUALS","args":{"obj":{"var":"team_%player_uuid%"},"objects":["red","blue"]},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You are in a team."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_NOT_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when `obj` is different from every value in `objects`.
args:
- objects (ANY, up to 27 values): values to compare with. Text values get their placeholders replaced.
- obj (ANY): the value to check. Text written here is NOT read with placeholders.
notes:
- Numbers here are also compared by their kind: a whole-number game value (for example `PLOT_ONLINE`) counts as different from the same number written as a JSON number. To compare numbers, use IF_VARIABLE_EQUALS with `"not": true` or IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER.
- If `obj` is missing, the result is TRUE only when `objects` is not empty.
- Targets: all (every target must have no equal value). Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_NOT_EQUALS","args":{"obj":{"var":"state"},"objects":"running"},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"The game has not started yet."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when `num1` compared with `num2` passes the check chosen by `state`.
args:
- num1 (NUMBER): the number to check. Missing or not a number → 0.
- num2 (NUMBER): the number to compare with. Missing or not a number → 0.
markers:
- state: 0 = num1 > num2 (default), 1 = num1 >= num2, 2 = num1 < num2, 3 = num1 <= num2
notes:
- Text is read as a number after placeholders, so `"%var(x)%"` works.
- Targets: first (only the first target is checked). Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_EASY_COMPARE_NUMBER","args":{"num1":{"var":"coins_%player_uuid%","save":true},"num2":100},"switches":{"state":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You can buy the sword."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_COMPARE_NUMBER
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when `number` is above `num1` and below `num2` (a range check). Each bound is optional.
args:
- number (NUMBER): the number to check.
- num1 (NUMBER): lower bound: `number` must be greater than it. Optional.
- num2 (NUMBER): upper bound: `number` must be less than it. Optional.
markers:
- state1: 0 = number > num1 (default), 1 = number >= num1
- state2: 0 = number < num2 (default), 1 = number <= num2
notes:
- A missing bound is not checked. If both bounds are missing, the result is FALSE.
- If `number` is missing or not a number, the result is TRUE only when both bounds are missing too.
- Targets: first. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_COMPARE_NUMBER","args":{"number":{"val":"CURRENT_HEALTH"},"num1":0,"num2":6},"switches":{"state1":0,"state2":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_ACTION_BAR","args":{"message":"§cLow health!"}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_TEXT_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when `text` is equal to at least one of `texts`.
args:
- texts (TEXT, up to 27 values): texts to compare with.
- text (TEXT): the text to check.
markers:
- case: 0 = case matters (default), 1 = ignore case
- colors: 0 = color codes matter (default), 1 = remove color codes before comparing
notes:
- If `text` is missing (for example a variable that does not exist), the result is TRUE only when `texts` is empty.
- Targets: any. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_TEXT_EQUALS","args":{"text":{"val":"EVENT_CHAT_MESSAGE"},"texts":["start","go"]},"switches":{"case":1,"colors":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Starting!"}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_TEXT_CONTAINS
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when `text` contains at least one of `texts` as a part. Example: `text` "Coding" contains "Cod".
args:
- texts (TEXT, up to 27 values): parts to look for.
- text (TEXT): the text to search in.
markers:
- case: 0 = case matters (default), 1 = ignore case
- colors: 0 = color codes matter (default), 1 = remove color codes before comparing
notes:
- If `text` is missing, the result is TRUE only when `texts` is empty.
- Targets: any. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_TEXT_CONTAINS","args":{"text":{"val":"EVENT_CHAT_MESSAGE"},"texts":"help"},"switches":{"case":1,"colors":0},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Type /spawn to go back."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_EXIST
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when a variable with this name exists (of any type, lists too).
args:
- variable (VARIABLE): the variable to check. Must be a variable value.
notes:
- The name is read with placeholders, so `kit_%player_uuid%` checks the current player's variable.
- Targets: first. Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_EXIST","not":true,"args":{"variable":{"var":"kit_%player_uuid%","save":true}},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"First visit!"}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_LOC_IN_RANGE
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when `loc_in_range` is inside the box between the corners `loc1` and `loc2`. The corners may be given in any order; the edges count as inside.
args:
- loc_in_range (LOCATION): the location to check.
- loc1 (LOCATION): first corner of the box.
- loc2 (LOCATION): second corner of the box.
notes:
- Exact coordinates are compared. A corner at block 10 means x up to 10.0, not 10.99. To include a whole block row, add 1 to the larger corner.
- Any location missing → FALSE. Locations outside the plot are pulled to the plot border first.
- Targets: any. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_LOC_IN_RANGE","args":{"loc_in_range":{"val":"CURRENT_LOCATION"},"loc1":{"loc":[10,60,10]},"loc2":{"loc":[20,70,20]}},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_ACTION_BAR","args":{"message":"You are in the arena."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_REGION_CONTAINS_ANOTHER_REGION
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
Compares two boxes: region 1 between `loc1` and `loc2`, region 2 between `loc3` and `loc4`.
args:
- loc1 (LOCATION): first corner of region 1.
- loc2 (LOCATION): second corner of region 1.
- loc3 (LOCATION): first corner of region 2.
- loc4 (LOCATION): second corner of region 2.
markers:
- state: 0 = TRUE when the regions overlap or touch (default), 1 = TRUE when region 2 is fully inside region 1
notes:
- For state 1, `loc1` must be the smaller corner (lowest x, y and z) and `loc2` the larger corner; this mode does not sort the corners. State 0 accepts corners in any order.
- Any location missing → FALSE.
- Targets: first. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_REGION_CONTAINS_ANOTHER_REGION","args":{"loc1":{"loc":[0,50,0]},"loc2":{"loc":[30,80,30]},"loc3":{"loc":[5,60,5]},"loc4":{"loc":[10,65,10]}},"switches":{"state":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Region 2 is inside region 1."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_TEXT_STARTS_WITH
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when `text` starts with at least one of `texts`. Example: `text` "!buy sword" starts with "!buy".
args:
- texts (TEXT, up to 27 values): beginnings to look for.
- text (TEXT): the text to check.
markers:
- case: 0 = case matters (default), 1 = ignore case
- colors: 0 = color codes matter (default), 1 = remove color codes before comparing
notes:
- `text` missing → FALSE.
- Targets: any. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_TEXT_STARTS_WITH","args":{"text":{"val":"EVENT_CHAT_MESSAGE"},"texts":"!buy"},"switches":{"case":1,"colors":0},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Opening the shop..."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_TEXT_ENDS_WITH
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when `text` ends with at least one of `texts`.
args:
- texts (TEXT, up to 27 values): endings to look for.
- text (TEXT): the text to check.
markers:
- case: 0 = case matters (default), 1 = ignore case
- colors: 0 = color codes matter (default), 1 = remove color codes before comparing
notes:
- `text` missing → FALSE.
- Targets: any. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_TEXT_ENDS_WITH","args":{"text":{"val":"EVENT_CHAT_MESSAGE"},"texts":"?"},"switches":{"case":0,"colors":0},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Good question!"}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_TEXT_EQUALS_REGEX
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when the texts match the regular expression `text` (Java regex). The WHOLE text must match, not a part: use `.*word.*` to look for a part.
args:
- texts (TEXT, up to 27 values): texts to test.
- text (TEXT): the regular expression.
markers:
- case: 0 = case matters (default), 1 = ignore case
- all_lines: 0 = at least one text must match (default), 1 = all texts must match
- color: 0 = keep color codes (default), 1 = remove color codes from the texts before testing (not from the expression)
notes:
- An invalid expression gives FALSE.
- Use simple expressions. A check that is too slow counts as not matching.
- `text` missing or `texts` empty → FALSE.
- Targets: any. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_TEXT_EQUALS_REGEX","args":{"text":"[0-9]{1,4}","texts":{"val":"EVENT_CHAT_MESSAGE"}},"switches":{"case":0,"all_lines":0,"color":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You typed a number."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_ITEM_IS_SOLID
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when the item is a solid block (a block that stops movement, for example stone, glass, stairs, slabs). Flowers, torches and plain items are not solid.
args:
- item (ITEM): the item to check.
notes:
- Some placeable things that were separate items in old Minecraft versions (for example doors and signs) count as not solid.
- `item` missing → TRUE.
- Targets: first. Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_ITEM_IS_SOLID","args":{"item":{"val":"MAIN_HAND_ITEM"}},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You hold a solid block."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_ITEM_IS_BLOCK
