kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when the item is a block that can be placed (stone, flowers, torches and so on), not a plain item like a sword.
args:
- item (ITEM): the item to check.
notes:
- Some placeable things that were separate items in old Minecraft versions (for example doors and signs) count as not a block.
- `item` missing → TRUE.
- Targets: first. Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_ITEM_IS_BLOCK","not":true,"args":{"item":{"val":"MAIN_HAND_ITEM"}},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"This is not a block."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_ITEM_HAS_ENCHANTMENT
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when the item has the enchantment. With `level`, the item's level must be at least `level`.
args:
- item (ITEM): the item to check.
- enchantment (TEXT): enchantment id, for example `sharpness` or `minecraft:sharpness`. Old names (for example `DAMAGE_ALL`) are converted.
- level (NUMBER): lowest level needed. Optional; missing → any level.
notes:
- `level` 0 is always TRUE, even without the enchantment.
- Unknown enchantment or missing item → FALSE.
- Targets: any. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_ITEM_HAS_ENCHANTMENT","args":{"item":{"val":"MAIN_HAND_ITEM"},"enchantment":"minecraft:sharpness","level":3},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Strong sword!"}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_ITEM_HAS_CUSTOM_TAG
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when the item has the custom tag `key` (tags made by VARIABLE_CREATE_CUSTOM_TAG). With `value`, the tag value must also be equal.
args:
- item (ITEM): the item to check.
- key (TEXT): the tag name.
- value (TEXT): the value to compare. Optional; empty → only the tag must exist.
- path (TEXT): path to nested tags, parts separated by `/`, at most 5 parts. Optional; empty → top level.
markers:
- type_of_value: 0 = compare as text, the tag must be a text tag (default), 1 = compare as a number, the tag must be a number tag
notes:
- A path part can itself be checked as a tag: put it into `key`.
- Missing or empty item → FALSE.
- Targets: any. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_ITEM_HAS_CUSTOM_TAG","args":{"item":{"val":"MAIN_HAND_ITEM"},"key":"weapon_id","value":"7"},"switches":{"type_of_value":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Weapon 7 equipped."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_EQUALS_ITEM
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when `item` is equal to at least one of `items`. The markers let you ignore parts of the item.
args:
- item (ITEM): the item to check. Missing or air → FALSE.
- items (ITEM, up to 18 values): items to compare with. Empty → FALSE.
markers:
- amount: 0 = compare the amount (default), 1 = ignore the amount
- durability: 0 = compare durability (default), 1 = ignore durability
- name: 0 = compare the name (default), 1 = ignore the name
- desc: 0 = compare the lore (default), 1 = ignore the lore
- metadata: 0 = compare all item data (default), 1 = ignore all item data (only the item type and amount are compared)
- ench: 0 = compare enchantments (default), 1 = ignore enchantments
- material: 0 = compare the item type (default), 1 = ignore the item type
- flags: 0 = compare item flags (default), 1 = ignore item flags
notes:
- Without ignores the items must match exactly, including the amount.
- Targets: first. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_EQUALS_ITEM","args":{"item":{"val":"MAIN_HAND_ITEM"},"items":{"item":"minecraft:diamond"}},"switches":{"amount":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You hold diamonds."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_TYPE_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when `value` has the type chosen by `state`.
args:
- value (ANY): the value to check, usually a variable.
markers:
- state: 0 = text (default), 1 = number, 2 = location, 3 = vector, 4 = potion, 5 = particle, 6 = item, 7 = text component
notes:
- Lists, dicts, sets and entity types match no option.
- `value` missing (for example a variable that does not exist) → FALSE.
- Targets: first. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_TYPE_EQUALS","args":{"value":{"var":"answer"}},"switches":{"state":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"The answer is a number."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_DICT_HAS_KEY
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when the dict has a pair with `key`.
args:
- dict (MAP): the dict. A variable or a literal dict. A dict that does not exist is empty.
- key (TEXT): the key. Exact match, case-sensitive.
notes:
- Targets: any. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_DICT_HAS_KEY","args":{"dict":{"var":"prices","save":true},"key":"sword"},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"The shop sells swords."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_SET_CONTAINS
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when the set contains `value`.
args:
- set (SET): the set. A variable or a literal set. A set that does not exist is empty.
- value (ANY): the value to find. Text gets its placeholders replaced.
notes:
- Same type and content needed: number 5 and text "5" are different.
- `value` missing → FALSE.
- Targets: any. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_SET_CONTAINS","args":{"set":{"var":"finished_players"},"value":"%player_uuid%"},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You already finished."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_LIST_CONTAINS
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when the list has at least one element equal to `value`.
args:
- list (LIST): the list. A variable or a literal list. A list that does not exist is empty.
- value (ANY): the value to find. Text gets its placeholders replaced.
notes:
- Same type and content needed: number 5 and text "5" are different.
- `value` missing → FALSE.
- Targets: any. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_LIST_CONTAINS","args":{"list":{"var":"joined_players"},"value":"%player%"},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You are in the queue."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_LIST_ELEMENT_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when the element at position `index` is equal to at least one of `values`.
args:
- list (LIST): the list. A variable or a literal list.
- index (NUMBER): position, starting at 1.
- values (ANY, up to 27 values): values to compare with. Text values get their placeholders replaced.
notes:
- Index out of range → FALSE.
- Same type and content needed: number 5 and text "5" are different.
- Targets: any. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_LIST_ELEMENT_EQUALS","args":{"list":{"var":"joined_players"},"index":1,"values":"%player%"},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You are first in the queue."}}]}

### IF_VARIABLE_LIST_IS_EMPTY
kind: condition | category: IF_VARIABLE | targets: none (tests values)
TRUE when the list has no elements. A list that does not exist counts as empty.
args:
- list (LIST): the list. A variable or a literal list.
notes:
- Targets: any. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_VARIABLE_LIST_IS_EMPTY","args":{"list":{"var":"queue"}},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Nobody is waiting."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_SNEAKING
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_TOGGLE | targets: players
TRUE when the player is sneaking (holding shift).
notes:
- Targets: all (a non-player target fails). Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_SNEAKING","children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You are sneaking."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_HOLDING_ITEM
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_INVENTORY_AND_ITEMS | targets: players
TRUE when the player holds one of `items` in the hand chosen by `state`.
args:
- items (ITEM, up to 27 values): items to look for. One match is enough.
markers:
- amount: 0 = compare the amount (default), 1 = ignore the amount
- durability: 0 = compare durability (default), 1 = ignore durability
- name: 0 = compare the name (default), 1 = ignore the name
- desc: 0 = compare the lore (default), 1 = ignore the lore
- state: 0 = any hand (default), 1 = main hand, 2 = off hand, 3 = both hands (the same item in each)
- metadata: 0 = compare all item data (default), 1 = ignore all item data (only the item type and amount are compared)
- ench: 0 = compare enchantments (default), 1 = ignore enchantments
- material: 0 = compare the item type (default), 1 = ignore the item type
- flags: 0 = compare item flags (default), 1 = ignore item flags
notes:
- Without ignores the held stack must match exactly, including the amount. Usually set `amount` to 1.
- With empty `items`: TRUE when the chosen hand is empty (for state 3: both hands).
- Targets: first (a non-player fails). Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_HOLDING_ITEM","args":{"items":{"item":"minecraft:blaze_rod"}},"switches":{"amount":1,"state":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Magic wand ready."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_HAS_ITEM
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_INVENTORY_AND_ITEMS | targets: players
TRUE when the player's inventory has the items. The count of each item is the minimum needed; several stacks add up.
args:
- items (ITEM, up to 36 values): items to look for.
markers:
- all: 0 = at least one of the items (default), 1 = all of the items
notes:
- Items must match exactly except the amount (name, lore and enchantments count).
- With empty `items`: FALSE for all = 0, TRUE for all = 1.
- Targets: all (a non-player fails). Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_HAS_ITEM","args":{"items":{"item":"minecraft:gold_ingot","count":10}},"switches":{"all":0},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You have 10 gold."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_LOOKING_AT
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_BLOCK_AND_WORLD | targets: players
TRUE when the block the player looks at is one of `blocks` OR is at one of `locations`, and it is not farther than `distance`.
args:
- blocks (ITEM, up to 9 values): block items to compare with. A water or lava bucket means water or lava.
- locations (LOCATION, up to 9 values): block positions to compare with.
- distance (NUMBER): largest distance in blocks from the player's feet. Default: 5.
markers:
- state: 0 = exact block type (default), 1 = ignore the block variant (all wool colors, all planks, logs, leaves, saplings, wooden slabs and stairs, signs, banners, beds, shulker boxes, and blocks that shared one id in old versions)
notes:
- The looked-at block is the first non-air block along the view, up to 120 blocks. Water counts as a block.
- With no `blocks`, the type test checks for air, so give `blocks` or `locations`.
- Targets: all (a non-player fails). Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_LOOKING_AT","args":{"blocks":{"item":"minecraft:target"},"distance":30},"switches":{"state":0},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You aim at the target."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_STANDING_ON
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_BLOCK_AND_WORLD | targets: players
TRUE when the block under the player is one of `blocks` OR is at one of `locations`.
args:
- blocks (ITEM, up to 18 values): block items to compare with. A water or lava bucket means water or lava.
- locations (LOCATION, up to 18 values): block positions to compare with.
markers:
- state: 0 = exact block type (default), 1 = ignore the block variant (all wool colors, all planks, logs, leaves, saplings, wooden slabs and stairs, signs, banners, beds, shulker boxes, and blocks that shared one id in old versions)
notes:
- "Under the player" is the block one below the block at the player's feet. On a slab, carpet or snow layer this is the block under that slab or carpet. In the air it is usually air.
- With no `blocks`, the type test checks for air.
- Targets: all (a non-player fails). Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_STANDING_ON","args":{"blocks":{"item":"minecraft:gold_block"}},"switches":{"state":0},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Checkpoint!"}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_NEAR
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_BLOCK_AND_WORLD | targets: players
TRUE when the player is within `range` blocks of at least one of `locations`.
args:
- locations (LOCATION, up to 27 values): centers.
- range (NUMBER): radius in blocks, measured from the player's feet in 3D. Default: 5.
notes:
- No locations → FALSE.
- Targets: first (only the first target is checked; a non-player fails). Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_NEAR","args":{"locations":{"loc":[0,64,0]},"range":3},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_ACTION_BAR","args":{"message":"You are near the spawn."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_WEARING
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_INVENTORY_AND_ITEMS | targets: players
TRUE when one of the player's armor pieces (helmet, chestplate, leggings, boots) is equal to one of `items`.
args:
- items (ITEM, up to 27 values): items to look for. One match is enough.
markers:
- amount: 0 = compare the amount (default), 1 = ignore the amount
- durability: 0 = compare durability (default), 1 = ignore durability
- name: 0 = compare the name (default), 1 = ignore the name
- desc: 0 = compare the lore (default), 1 = ignore the lore
- metadata: 0 = compare all item data (default), 1 = ignore all item data (only the item type and amount are compared)
- ench: 0 = compare enchantments (default), 1 = ignore enchantments
- material: 0 = compare the item type (default), 1 = ignore the item type
- flags: 0 = compare item flags (default), 1 = ignore item flags
notes:
- Armor wear (durability) is compared too; set `durability` to 1 for worn armor.
- A player with no armor: TRUE only when `items` is empty.
- Targets: first (a non-player fails). Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_WEARING","args":{"items":{"item":"minecraft:diamond_helmet"}},"switches":{"durability":1,"ench":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Nice helmet."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_NAME_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_ANOTHER | targets: players
TRUE when the target's name is equal to one of `name`. For a non-player, its custom name (or type name) is used.
args:
- name (TEXT, up to 36 values): names to compare with.
markers:
- case: 0 = case matters (default), 1 = ignore case
notes:
- Targets: all (every target needs a matching name). Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_NAME_EQUALS","args":{"name":["Steve","Alex"]},"switches":{"case":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Welcome back, builder!"}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_BLOCKING
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_TOGGLE | targets: players
TRUE when the player blocks with a raised shield.
notes:
- Targets: all (a non-player fails). Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_BLOCKING","children":[{"action":"PLAYER_ACTION_BAR","args":{"message":"Blocked!"}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_ITEM_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_INVENTORY_AND_ITEMS | targets: the current event
TRUE when the item of the current event is equal to one of `items`.
args:
- items (ITEM, up to 27 values): items to compare with. One match is enough.
markers:
- amount: 0 = compare the amount (default), 1 = ignore the amount
- durability: 0 = compare durability (default), 1 = ignore durability
- name: 0 = compare the name (default), 1 = ignore the name
- desc: 0 = compare the lore (default), 1 = ignore the lore
- metadata: 0 = compare all item data (default), 1 = ignore all item data (only the item type and amount are compared)
- ench: 0 = compare enchantments (default), 1 = ignore enchantments
- material: 0 = compare the item type (default), 1 = ignore the item type
- flags: 0 = compare item flags (default), 1 = ignore item flags
notes:
- Works in events with an item: PLAYER_RIGHT_CLICK, PLAYER_LEFT_CLICK, PLAYER_BLOCK_INTERACT (item in hand), PLAYER_CLICK_INVENTORY (clicked item), PLAYER_DRAG_INVENTORY (dragged item), PLAYER_CONSUME_ITEM, PLAYER_DROP_ITEM, PLAYER_PICKUP_ITEM, PLAYER_BREAK_ITEM, PLAYER_DAMAGE_ITEM, PLAYER_ITEM_MEND, PLAYER_MANIPULATE_ARMOR_STAND, PLAYER_SWAP_HANDS, and other events that give an `item` value. Other events: FALSE plus an "incompatible event" warning.
- Event item is empty (for example a click with an empty hand): TRUE only when `items` is empty.
- Targets: first. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_ITEM_EQUALS","args":{"items":{"item":"minecraft:compass"}},"switches":{"amount":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Opening the game menu..."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_CURSOR_ITEM_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_INVENTORY_AND_ITEMS | targets: players
TRUE when the item the player carries on the mouse cursor (in an open inventory) is equal to one of `items`.
args:
- items (ITEM, up to 27 values): items to compare with. One match is enough.
markers:
- amount: 0 = compare the amount (default), 1 = ignore the amount
- durability: 0 = compare durability (default), 1 = ignore durability
- name: 0 = compare the name (default), 1 = ignore the name
- desc: 0 = compare the lore (default), 1 = ignore the lore
- metadata: 0 = compare all item data (default), 1 = ignore all item data (only the item type and amount are compared)
- ench: 0 = compare enchantments (default), 1 = ignore enchantments
- material: 0 = compare the item type (default), 1 = ignore the item type
- flags: 0 = compare item flags (default), 1 = ignore item flags
notes:
- Empty cursor: TRUE only when `items` is empty.
- Targets: first (a non-player fails). Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_CURSOR_ITEM_EQUALS","args":{"items":{"item":"minecraft:emerald"}},"switches":{"amount":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You picked up an emerald."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_HAS_ITEM_COOLDOWN
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_INVENTORY_AND_ITEMS | targets: players
TRUE when the item type of `item` is on cooldown for the player (like an ender pearl right after a throw).
args:
- item (ITEM): the item whose type is checked. Only the type matters. Missing → FALSE.
notes:
- Targets: any (non-player targets are skipped). Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_HAS_ITEM_COOLDOWN","args":{"item":{"item":"minecraft:ender_pearl"}},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_ACTION_BAR","args":{"message":"Pearl is recharging."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_LEFT_OR_RIGHT_CLICK
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_INVENTORY_AND_ITEMS | targets: the current event
TRUE when the inventory click of the current event was the chosen mouse button. Shift-clicks count too.
markers:
- state: 0 = left click (default), 1 = right click
notes:
- Works only in PLAYER_CLICK_INVENTORY. In any other event it is FALSE (no warning).
- Targets: first. Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_LEFT_OR_RIGHT_CLICK","switches":{"state":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Right click: sell one."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_BLOCK_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_BLOCK_AND_WORLD | targets: the current event
TRUE when the block of the current event is one of `blocks` OR is at one of `locations`.
args:
- blocks (ITEM, up to 18 values): block items to compare with. A water or lava bucket means water or lava.
- locations (LOCATION, up to 18 values): block positions to compare with.
markers:
- state: 0 = exact block type (default), 1 = ignore the block variant (all wool colors, all planks, logs, leaves, saplings, wooden slabs and stairs, signs, banners, beds, shulker boxes, and blocks that shared one id in old versions)
notes:
- Works in events with a block: PLAYER_BLOCK_PLACE, PLAYER_BLOCK_BREAK, PLAYER_BLOCK_DAMAGE, PLAYER_BLOCK_INTERACT and PLAYER_RIGHT_CLICK (clicked block), block events of the plot (PLOT_*), ENTITY_CHANGE_BLOCK, and other events that give a `block` value. Other events: FALSE plus an "incompatible event" warning.
- The event has no block (for example a click in the air): TRUE only when both `blocks` and `locations` are empty.
- With no `blocks`, the type test checks for air.
- Targets: all (the same block for every target). Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_BLOCK_EQUALS","args":{"blocks":[{"item":"minecraft:diamond_ore"},{"item":"minecraft:deepslate_diamond_ore"}]},"switches":{"state":0},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Diamonds!"}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_CLICKED_SLOT_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_INVENTORY_AND_ITEMS | targets: the current event
TRUE when the clicked slot of the current inventory event is one of `slots`.
args:
- slots (NUMBER, up to 27 values): slot numbers inside the clicked inventory. A chest starts at 0 (top left). In the player's own inventory 0–8 is the hotbar and 9–35 the main part.
markers:
- state: 0 = at least one dragged slot is in `slots` (default), 1 = every dragged slot is in `slots`
notes:
- Works in PLAYER_CLICK_INVENTORY and PLAYER_DRAG_INVENTORY. In any other event it is FALSE (no warning).
- `state` is used only in PLAYER_DRAG_INVENTORY. In PLAYER_CLICK_INVENTORY the one clicked slot must be in `slots`.
- In a drag event with empty `slots` it is FALSE.
- Targets: all. Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_CLICKED_SLOT_EQUALS","args":{"slots":[11,15]},"switches":{"state":0},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You chose a kit."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_SLOT_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_INVENTORY_AND_ITEMS | targets: players
TRUE when the player's selected hotbar slot is one of `slots`. 0 = the leftmost slot, 8 = the rightmost.
args:
- slots (NUMBER, up to 9 values): hotbar slot numbers 0–8.
notes:
- Targets: all (a non-player fails). Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_SLOT_EQUALS","args":{"slots":0},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_ACTION_BAR","args":{"message":"Slot 1 selected."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_OPEN_TITLE_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_INVENTORY_AND_ITEMS | targets: players
TRUE when the title of the inventory the player has open is equal to one of `texts`.
args:
- texts (TEXT, up to 27 values): titles to compare with.
markers:
- case: 0 = case matters (default), 1 = ignore case
- colors: 0 = color codes matter (default), 1 = remove color codes before comparing
notes:
- For a menu opened by code, the title given in that action is used. For a vanilla container without a custom name the title is its translation key (for example `container.chest`).
- Targets: any (checked per target; a non-player before a match fails). Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_OPEN_TITLE_EQUALS","args":{"texts":"Shop"},"switches":{"case":0,"colors":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You are in the shop."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_VOTED

TRUE when the player has liked (voted for) this plot.

### IF_PLAYER_MESSAGE_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_ANOTHER | targets: the current event
TRUE when the chat message of the current event is equal to one of `name` (the whole message).
args:
- name (TEXT, up to 36 values): messages to compare with. The key is `name` even though it holds messages.
markers:
- case: 0 = case matters (default), 1 = ignore case
notes:
- Works in PLAYER_CHAT. Other events: FALSE plus an "incompatible event" warning.
- To look for a part of the message, use IF_VARIABLE_TEXT_CONTAINS with the game value `EVENT_CHAT_MESSAGE`.
- Targets: all. Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_MESSAGE_EQUALS","args":{"name":["hi","hello"]},"switches":{"case":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Hello, %player%!"}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_BLOCK_INTERACT_ACTION_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_ANOTHER | targets: the current event
TRUE when the click type of the current event is the one chosen by `state`.
markers:
- state: 0 = right click on a block or air (default), 1 = left click on a block or air, 2 = right click on air, 3 = right click on a block, 4 = left click on air, 5 = left click on a block, 6 = physical (stepping on a pressure plate, tripwire or farmland)
notes:
- Works in PLAYER_BLOCK_INTERACT, PLAYER_RIGHT_CLICK and PLAYER_LEFT_CLICK. Other events: FALSE plus an "incompatible event" warning.
- Targets: first. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_BLOCK_INTERACT_ACTION_EQUALS","switches":{"state":6},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You stepped on a plate."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_EQUIPMENT_SLOT_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_ANOTHER | targets: the current event
TRUE when the hand used in the current click event is the one chosen by `state`.
markers:
- state: 0 = main hand (default), 1 = off hand
notes:
- Works in PLAYER_BLOCK_INTERACT, PLAYER_RIGHT_CLICK and PLAYER_LEFT_CLICK. Other events: FALSE plus an "incompatible event" warning.
- A right click often fires twice, once per hand; use this condition with state 0 to react only once.
- Targets: first. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_EQUIPMENT_SLOT_EQUALS","switches":{"state":0},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Main hand click."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_HAS_GOLD_TRANSACTION
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_ANOTHER | targets: players
TRUE when the player has made a creative-coin purchase on this plot (see PLAYER_TRY_GOLD_TRANSACTION) with the name `key` or with the amount `creativecoins`.
args:
- key (TEXT): purchase name. Optional. Read as written, without placeholders.
- creativecoins (NUMBER): purchase amount in creative coins. Optional. Decimals are cut.
notes:
- With both given, one of them is enough. With neither, the result is TRUE.
- Works only in worlds where payments are available; otherwise always FALSE.
- Targets: all (a non-player fails). Empty selection → TRUE (on such a plot).
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_HAS_GOLD_TRANSACTION","args":{"key":"vip_kit"},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Thanks for buying the VIP kit!"}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_GAMEMODE_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_ANOTHER | targets: players
TRUE when the player's game mode is the one chosen by `gm`.
markers:
- gm: 0 = creative (default), 1 = survival, 2 = adventure, 3 = spectator
notes:
- Option 0 is creative, not survival.
- Targets: first player (non-players are skipped). Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_GAMEMODE_EQUALS","switches":{"gm":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You are in survival mode."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_HAS_PERMISSION
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_ANOTHER | targets: players
TRUE when the player has the plot right chosen by `permission`. The plot owner has rights 0–2 always.
markers:
- permission: 0 = is on the plot whitelist (default), 1 = may build on the plot, 2 = may edit the code, 3 = is allowed to fly
notes:
- Targets: first player (non-players are skipped). Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_HAS_PERMISSION","switches":{"permission":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Builder mode."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_INVENTORY_FULL
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_INVENTORY_AND_ITEMS | targets: players
TRUE when every slot of the player's inventory holds a full stack.
notes:
- All slots count, also the armor slots and the off hand. An empty armor slot makes it FALSE.
- Targets: first (a non-player fails). Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_INVENTORY_FULL","children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Your inventory is full."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_HAS_VAR_WITH_ITEM
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_INVENTORY_AND_ITEMS | targets: players | hidden in menu
Same as IF_PLAYER_HAS_ITEM, but the argument accepts any value, for example variables that hold items. Only values that are items are used.
args:
- items (ANY, up to 36 values): items or variables with items.
markers:
- all: 0 = at least one of the items (default), 1 = all of the items
notes:
- Prefer IF_PLAYER_HAS_ITEM; it accepts variables too.
- Targets: all (a non-player fails). Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_HAS_VAR_WITH_ITEM","args":{"items":{"var":"key_item","save":true}},"switches":{"all":0},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You have the key."}}]}

### IF_PLAYER_HAS_RESOURCE_PACK
kind: condition | category: IF_PLAYER_TOGGLE | targets: players
TRUE when the player has loaded the server resource pack.
notes:
- Targets: any (non-players are skipped). Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_PLAYER_HAS_RESOURCE_PACK","not":true,"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Please accept the resource pack."}}]}

### IF_ENTITY_IS_TYPE
kind: condition | entity condition | category: IF_ENTITY | targets: entities
TRUE when the entity type equals the type one of `items` stands for.
args:
- items (ITEM, up to 36 values): spawn eggs (for example `minecraft:zombie_spawn_egg`) or special items: arrow = arrow, snowball = snowball, tnt = primed TNT, armor_stand = armor stand, player_head = player, soul_sand = wither, zombie_head = giant, any other block = falling block, any other item = dropped item.
notes:
- Targets: all (every entity must match one item). Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_ENTITY_IS_TYPE","args":{"items":{"item":"minecraft:zombie_spawn_egg"}},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"That was a zombie."}}]}

### IF_ENTITY_NAME_EQUALS
kind: condition | entity condition | category: IF_ENTITY | targets: entities
TRUE when the entity name equals one of `name`. The name is the custom name if set, else the default name; for a player it is the player name.
args:
- name (TEXT, up to 36 values): names to compare with.
markers:
- case: 0 = case matters (default), 1 = ignore case
- colors: 0 = color codes matter (default), 1 = remove color codes before comparing
notes:
- Targets: all. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_ENTITY_NAME_EQUALS","args":{"name":"Boss"},"switches":{"case":0,"colors":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You hit the boss!"}}]}

### IF_ENTITY_STANDING_ON_BLOCK
kind: condition | entity condition | category: IF_ENTITY | targets: entities
TRUE when the block under the entity is one of `blocks` OR is at one of `locations`.
args:
- blocks (ITEM, up to 18 values): block items to compare with. A water or lava bucket means water or lava.
- locations (LOCATION, up to 18 values): block positions to compare with.
markers:
- state: 0 = exact block type (default), 1 = ignore the block variant (all wool colors, all planks, logs, leaves, saplings, wooden slabs and stairs, signs, banners, beds, shulker boxes, and blocks that shared one id in old versions)
notes:
- "Under the entity" is the block one below the block at its feet (on a slab or carpet: the block under it).
- With no `blocks`, the type test checks for air.
- Targets: all. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_ENTITY_STANDING_ON_BLOCK","args":{"blocks":{"item":"minecraft:magma_block"}},"switches":{"state":0},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"The mob is on magma."}}]}

### IF_ENTITY_IS_NEAR
kind: condition | entity condition | category: IF_ENTITY | targets: entities
TRUE when the entity is within `range` blocks of at least one of `locations`.
args:
- locations (LOCATION, up to 27 values): centers.
- range (NUMBER): radius in blocks, measured from the entity's feet in 3D. Default: 5.
notes:
- No locations → FALSE.
- Targets: first (only the first entity is checked). Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_ENTITY_IS_NEAR","target":"LAST_LIVING_ENTITY","args":{"locations":{"loc":[0,64,0]},"range":10},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"The pet is close to the base."}}]}

### IF_ENTITY_IS_MOB
kind: condition | entity condition | category: IF_ENTITY | targets: entities
TRUE when the entity is a living entity: mobs, animals, armor stands and players count; items, arrows, minecarts and similar do not.
notes:
- Targets: all. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_ENTITY_IS_MOB","children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"You hit a living thing."}}]}

### IF_ENTITY_IS_NPC
kind: condition | entity condition | category: IF_ENTITY | targets: entities
TRUE when the entity is a server NPC (a non-player character that looks like a player).
notes:
- Targets: all. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_ENTITY_IS_NPC","children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Hello, I am an NPC."}}]}

### IF_ENTITY_IS_PROJECTILE
kind: condition | entity condition | category: IF_ENTITY | targets: entities
TRUE when the entity is a projectile: arrows, tridents, snowballs, eggs, ender pearls, fireballs and similar.
notes:
- Targets: all. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_ENTITY_IS_PROJECTILE","target":"DAMAGER","children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Hit by a projectile."}}]}

### IF_ENTITY_HAS_EFFECT
kind: condition | entity condition | category: IF_ENTITY | targets: entities
TRUE when the entity has the potion effect type of `potion`. Level and duration are not checked.
args:
- potion (POTION): the effect to look for. Required; missing → FALSE.
notes:
- Non-living entities never have effects (FALSE).
- Targets: all. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_ENTITY_HAS_EFFECT","args":{"potion":{"potion":"minecraft:invisibility","duration":200,"amplifier":0}},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"It is invisible."}}]}

### IF_ENTITY_IN_VEHICLE
kind: condition | entity condition | category: IF_ENTITY | targets: entities
Meant to check that the entity rides a vehicle. The current code only checks that the entity is a living entity (same as IF_ENTITY_IS_MOB), so it does not detect riding. Do not use it to check riding.
notes:
- Targets: all. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_ENTITY_IN_VEHICLE","children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"Living entity."}}]}

### IF_ENTITY_IS_VALID
kind: condition | entity condition | category: IF_ENTITY | targets: entities
TRUE when the entity still exists in the world and is not dead.
notes:
- Targets: all. Empty selection → FALSE.
example: {"action":"IF_ENTITY_IS_VALID","target":"LAST_LIVING_ENTITY","children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"The boss is still alive."}}]}

### IF_GAME_BLOCK_EQUALS
kind: condition | category: IF_GAME | targets: the plot world
TRUE when the block at `location` is one of `blocks`.
args:
- blocks (ITEM, up to 36 values): block items to compare with. A water or lava bucket means water or lava. Air items are ignored.
- location (LOCATION): the block to check. Missing → FALSE.
markers:
- state: 0 = exact block type (default), 1 = ignore the block variant (all wool colors, all planks, logs, leaves, saplings, wooden slabs and stairs, signs, banners, beds, shulker boxes, and blocks that shared one id in old versions)
notes:
- To check for air, give no `blocks`: an air block is TRUE only when `blocks` is empty.
- Targets: all (runs per target; matters only with placeholders in arguments). Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_GAME_BLOCK_EQUALS","args":{"location":{"loc":[5,64,5]},"blocks":{"item":"minecraft:redstone_block"}},"switches":{"state":0},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"The door is powered."}}]}

### IF_GAME_CONTAINER_HAS_ITEM
kind: condition | category: IF_GAME | targets: the plot world
TRUE when the container at `location` (chest, barrel, hopper, furnace, shulker box and so on) has the items. The count of each item is the minimum needed; several stacks add up.
args:
- location (LOCATION): the container block. Not a container or missing → FALSE.
- items (ITEM, up to 36 values): items to look for. Compared without the amount.
markers:
- all: 0 = at least one of the items (default), 1 = all of the items
notes:
- With empty `items`: FALSE for all = 0, TRUE for all = 1.
- Targets: all (runs per target). Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_GAME_CONTAINER_HAS_ITEM","args":{"location":{"loc":[3,64,7]},"items":{"item":"minecraft:diamond","count":5}},"switches":{"all":0},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"The chest has 5 diamonds."}}]}

### IF_GAME_SIGN_HAS_TEXT
kind: condition | category: IF_GAME | targets: the plot world
TRUE when a line on the front side of the sign at `location` is equal to `text` (the whole line, not a part).
args:
- location (LOCATION): the sign block. Not a sign or missing → FALSE.
- text (TEXT): the text to compare with. Missing → FALSE.
- line (NUMBER): line number from 0 (top) to 3 (bottom). Values below 0 become 0, above 3 become 3. Optional; missing → any of the 4 lines.
markers:
- case: 0 = case matters (default), 1 = ignore case
- colors: 0 = color codes matter (default), 1 = remove color codes before comparing
notes:
- Only the front side of the sign is read.
- Targets: all (runs per target). Empty selection → TRUE.
example: {"action":"IF_GAME_SIGN_HAS_TEXT","args":{"location":{"loc":[2,65,9]},"text":"OPEN","line":0},"switches":{"case":1,"colors":1},"children":[{"action":"PLAYER_MESSAGE","args":{"texts":"The shop is open."}}]}
